      db: {},

      activeTargetId: '',
      availableRoles: [],
      activeMenu: 'dashboard',
      mobileDrawerOpen: false,
      // Sub-states untuk interaksi modul
      selectedAssignClassId: null,
      selectedTeacherClassId: null,
      selectedSessionId: null,
      sessionFilter: 'ALL',
      myClassesTab: 'intra',
      legerTab: 'raport',
      selectedReportStudentId: 'ALL',
      p5ProjectFilter: 'ALL',
      p5ClassFilter: 'ALL',
      p5StatusFilter: 'ALL',
      p5Search: '',
      protaSubjectFilter: 'ALL',
      protaGradeFilter: 'ALL',
      jurnalClassFilter: '',
      jurnalSubjectFilter: ''
    };

    const ROLE_META = {
      admin: { label: 'Administrator', badge: 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800', icon: 'shield-check' },
      kepala_sekolah: { label: 'Kepala Sekolah', badge: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800', icon: 'award' },
      guru: { label: 'Guru Mata Pelajaran', badge: 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800', icon: 'book-open' },
      wali_kelas: { label: 'Wali Kelas', badge: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800', icon: 'users' },

    };

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * UI UTILITIES (LOADER, TOAST, MODAL, PRINT, XLSX)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function showLoader(msg = 'Memproses data...') {
      document.getElementById('loaderText').textContent = msg;
      const el = document.getElementById('globalLoader');
      el.classList.remove('hidden');
      el.classList.add('flex');
    }

    function hideLoader() {
      const el = document.getElementById('globalLoader');
      el.classList.add('hidden');
      el.classList.remove('flex');
    }

    function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
      const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
      const colors = {
        success: 'bg-emerald-600 text-white',
        error: 'bg-rose-600 text-white',
        info: 'bg-slate-800 text-white',
        warning: 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-900'
      };
      const toast = document.createElement('div');
      toast.className = `${colors[type] || colors.info} px-4 py-3 rounded-xl shadow-lg text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all duration-300`;
      toast.innerHTML = `<span>${message}</span>`;
      container.appendChild(toast);
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
      }, 3500);
    }

    function openModal(title, bodyHtml, footerHtml = '', maxWidthClass = 'max-w-3xl') {
      document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;
      document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML = bodyHtml;
      document.getElementById('modalFooter').innerHTML = footerHtml || `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 font-semibold text-sm">Tutup</button>`;
      const box = document.getElementById('modalBox');
      box.className = `bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full ${maxWidthClass} max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden border border-slate-200`;
      const backdrop = document.getElementById('modalBackdrop');
      backdrop.classList.remove('hidden');
      backdrop.classList.add('flex');
      lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function closeModal() {
      const backdrop = document.getElementById('modalBackdrop');
      backdrop.classList.add('hidden');
      backdrop.classList.remove('flex');
    }

    function triggerPrintHtml(htmlContent) {
      const area = document.getElementById('printArea');
      area.innerHTML = htmlContent;
      area.classList.remove('hidden');
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.print();
        area.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 250);
    }

    function exportTableToExcel(rowsArray, sheetName, fileName) {
      if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined') {
        showToast('Library SheetJS sedang dimuat, coba sesaat lagi.', 'warning');
        return;
      }
      const ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rowsArray);
      const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
      XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, sheetName.substring(0, 30));
      XLSX.writeFile(wb, fileName);
      showToast('File Excel berhasil diunduh!', 'success');
    }

    function copyTableTsv(rowsArray) {
      const tsv = rowsArray.map(r => r.map(c => String(c ?? '').replace(/\t|\n/g, ' ')).join('\t')).join('\n');
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(tsv).then(() => {
        showToast('Data berhasil disalin (TSV)! Siap tempel di Google Sheets / Excel.', 'success');
      }).catch(() => {
        showToast('Gagal menyalin ke clipboard.', 'error');
      });
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * SHARED GRADING & ATP DESCRIPTION ENGINE (CLIENT MIRROR)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function shortenTpText(text, maxLen = 78) {
      const clean = String(text || '').trim().replace(/\.$/, '');
      if (clean.length <= maxLen) return clean;
      const sub = clean.substring(0, maxLen);
      const lastSpace = sub.lastIndexOf(' ');
      return (lastSpace > 20 ? sub.substring(0, lastSpace) : sub) + '...';
    }

    function getStudentSubjectReport(studentId, classId, subjectId) {
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const cls = classes.find(c => String(c.id) === String(classId)) || { kkm: 75, grade_level: '7' };
      const kkmNum = Number(cls.kkm) || 75;
      const sessions = (STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || []).filter(s => String(s.class_id) === String(classId) && String(s.subject_id) === String(subjectId));
      const sessionMap = {};
      sessions.forEach(s => { sessionMap[s.id] = s; });

      const records = (STATE.db.AttendanceRecords || []).filter(r => String(r.student_id) === String(studentId) && sessionMap[r.session_id]);
      const atpList = STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || [];
      const atpById = {};
      const atpByCode = {};
      atpList.forEach(a => {
        atpById[a.id] = a;
        if (a.atp_code) atpByCode[a.atp_code] = a;
      });

      const nhScores = [], stsScores = [], sasScores = [];
      const atpScoresMap = {};

      records.forEach(rec => {
        if (rec.score === '' || rec.score === null || rec.score === undefined || isNaN(Number(rec.score))) return;
        const val = Number(rec.score);
        const sess = sessionMap[rec.session_id];
        const type = String(sess.session_type || 'NH').toUpperCase();
        if (type === 'NH') nhScores.push(val);
        else if (type === 'STS') stsScores.push(val);
        else if (type === 'SAS') sasScores.push(val);
        else nhScores.push(val);

        const atpObj = atpById[sess.atp_id] || atpByCode[sess.atp_code];
        if (atpObj) {
          if (!atpScoresMap[atpObj.id]) {
            atpScoresMap[atpObj.id] = { id: atpObj.id, code: atpObj.atp_code, tp_text: atpObj.tp_text, scores: [] };
          }
          atpScoresMap[atpObj.id].scores.push(val);
        }
      });

      const avg = arr => arr.length ? arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length : null;
      const avgNH = avg(nhScores);
      const avgSTS = avg(stsScores);
      const avgSAS = avg(sasScores);

      const components = [avgNH, avgSTS, avgSAS].filter(v => v !== null);
      const finalScore = components.length > 0 ? Math.round(avg(components)) : null;

      let predicate = '-';
      let predicateFull = '-';
      if (finalScore !== null) {
        if (finalScore >= 90) { predicate = 'A'; predicateFull = 'A (Sangat Baik)'; }
        else if (finalScore >= kkmNum) { predicate = 'B'; predicateFull = 'B (Baik)'; }
        else if (finalScore >= 60) { predicate = 'C'; predicateFull = 'C (Cukup)'; }
        else { predicate = 'D'; predicateFull = 'D (Perlu Bimbingan)'; }
      }

      let description = 'Belum terdapat data penilaian pada semester ini.';
      const evaluatedAtps = Object.values(atpScoresMap).map(item => ({
        ...item,
        avg: Math.round(avg(item.scores))
      }));

      if (finalScore !== null) {
        const tuntasAtps = evaluatedAtps.filter(a => a.avg >= kkmNum).sort((a, b) => b.avg - a.avg);
        const belumTuntasAtps = evaluatedAtps.filter(a => a.avg < kkmNum).sort((a, b) => a.avg - b.avg);

        if (tuntasAtps.length > 0 && belumTuntasAtps.length > 0) {
          const top1 = tuntasAtps[0];
          const low1 = belumTuntasAtps[0];
          description = `Sudah tuntas dalam ${shortenTpText(top1.tp_text, 78)}, namun perlu perbaikan pada ${shortenTpText(low1.tp_text, 78)}.`;
        } else if (belumTuntasAtps.length > 0) {
          const picks = belumTuntasAtps.slice(0, 2).map(a => shortenTpText(a.tp_text, 75)).join(' serta ');
          description = `Perlu pendampingan dan perbaikan pada capaian ${picks} agar mencapai ketuntasan minimal.`;
        } else if (tuntasAtps.length > 0) {
          const picks = tuntasAtps.slice(0, 2).map(a => shortenTpText(a.tp_text, 78)).join(' dan ');
          const prefix = finalScore >= 90 ? 'Menunjukkan penguasaan sangat optimal dalam ' : 'Sudah tuntas dengan baik dalam ';
          description = `${prefix}${picks}.`;
        } else {
          const fallbackAtps = atpList
            .filter(a => String(a.subject_id) === String(subjectId) && String(a.grade_level) === String(cls.grade_level))
            .slice(0, 2);
          if (fallbackAtps.length > 0) {
            const tpSummary = fallbackAtps.map(a => shortenTpText(a.tp_text, 78)).join(' serta ');
            description = finalScore >= kkmNum
              ? `Sudah tuntas menguasai capaian pembelajaran ${tpSummary}.`
              : `Perlu bimbingan lebih lanjut dalam ${tpSummary}.`;
          } else {
            description = finalScore >= kkmNum
              ? `Tuntas mencapai kriteria ketuntasan minimal.`
              : `Belum mencapai kriteria ketuntasan minimal, perlu remedial terstruktur.`;
          }
        }
      }

      return {
        kkm: kkmNum,
        nhScores,
        avgNH: avgNH !== null ? Math.round(avgNH) : null,
        avgSTS: avgSTS !== null ? Math.round(avgSTS) : null,
        avgSAS: avgSAS !== null ? Math.round(avgSAS) : null,
        finalScore,
        predicate,
        predicateFull,
        description
      };
    }

    /**
     * Konversi Predikat Massal ke Skor Numerik Sesuai KKM
     */
    function predicateToScoreByKkm(pred, kkm) {
      const k = Number(kkm) || 75;
      const map = {
        'A+': 98,
        'A': 94,
        'A-': 90,
        'B+': Math.min(89, Math.max(k + 10, 86)),
        'B': Math.min(88, Math.max(k + 5, 81)),
        'B-': k,
        'C+': Math.max(68, k - 3),
        'C': 64,
        'D': 55
      };
      return map[pred] !== undefined ? map[pred] : k;
    }

    function scoreToGradeLetter(score, kkm) {
      if (score === '' || score === null || score === undefined || isNaN(Number(score))) return '-';
      const s = Number(score);
      const k = Number(kkm) || 75;
      if (s >= 96) return 'A+';
      if (s >= 92) return 'A';
      if (s >= 90) return 'A-';
      if (s >= Math.max(k + 9, 85)) return 'B+';
      if (s >= Math.max(k + 4, 80)) return 'B';
      if (s >= k) return 'B-';
      if (s >= 67) return 'C+';
      if (s >= 60) return 'C';
      return 'D';
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * BOOTSTRAP & GAS BRIDGE
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function callServer(fnName, ...args) {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
          reject(new Error('Google Apps Script runtime tidak terdeteksi.'));
          return;
        }
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(res => resolve(res))
          .withFailureHandler(err => reject(err))[fnName](...args);
      });
    }

    async function loadAppData(silent = false) {
      if (!silent) showLoader('Memuat database SMP Kristen Waibakul...');
      try {
        const data = await callServer('getInitialBootstrapData');
        if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
          throw new Error('Server mengembalikan data kosong. Jalankan fungsi "diagnosa" di editor Apps Script untuk melihat langkah yang gagal.');
        }
        STATE.db = data;
        renderApp();
      } catch (err) {
        showFatalError('Gagal memuat data', err);
        showToast('Gagal memuat data: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), 'error');
      } finally {
        if (!silent) hideLoader();
      }
    }

    async function apiSave(sheetName, obj, successMsg = 'Data berhasil disimpan!') {
      showLoader('Menyimpan perubahan...');
      try {
        const actor = STATE.user ? { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole } : null;
        const res = await callServer('saveRecord', sheetName, obj, actor);
        if (res && res.success) {
          await loadAppData(true);
          showToast(successMsg, 'success');
          return res.record;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal menyimpan: ' + err.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    async function apiDelete(sheetName, id, successMsg = 'Data berhasil dihapus!') {
      showLoader('Menghapus data...');
      try {
        const actor = STATE.user ? { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole } : null;
        await callServer('deleteRecord', sheetName, id, actor);
        await loadAppData(true);
        showToast(successMsg, 'success');
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal menghapus: ' + err.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * LOGIN & MULTI-JABATAN SELECTION
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    let loginTab = 'guru';

    function renderLoginScreen() {
      const users = STATE.db.UsersPublic || [];
      const guruList = users.filter(u => u.role === 'guru' && u.status === 'Aktif');
      const quickOthers = users.filter(u => ['admin', 'kepala_sekolah', 'admin_kokurikuler', 'wali_murid'].includes(u.role) && u.status === 'Aktif');

      return `
        <div class="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 via-indigo-950 to-teal-900 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div class="w-full max-w-4xl bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12">
            <!-- Left Branding Panel -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-5 bg-gradient-to-b from-indigo-700 to-teal-700 p-8 text-white flex flex-col justify-between">
              <div>
                <div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center mb-6 border border-white/25">
                  <i data-lucide="graduation-cap" class="w-8 h-8 text-white"></i>
                </div>
                <span class="inline-block px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-white/15 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wider uppercase mb-3">
                  T.A. ${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')} • ${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().curriculum_name)}
                </span>
                <h1 class="text-2xl font-extrabold leading-tight">${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}</h1>
                <p class="text-indigo-100 text-sm mt-2 leading-relaxed">
                  Sistem Informasi Akademik Terpadu, Penilaian Kurikulum, Kokurikuler (P5), Wali Kelas & Manajemen Sekolah.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div class="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-white/20 text-xs text-indigo-100 space-y-1">
                <p class="font-semibold text-white">${siEsc([getSchoolIdentity().school_regency, getSchoolIdentity().school_province].filter(Boolean).join(', '))}</p>
                <p>${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().school_address)}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Right Login Tabs -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-7 p-6 sm:p-8 flex flex-col">
              <div class="flex bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl mb-6 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold">
                <button onclick="switchLoginTab('guru')" class="flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg transition ${loginTab === 'guru' ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'}">
                  1. Panel Guru & GTK
                </button>
                <button onclick="switchLoginTab('manual')" class="flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg transition ${loginTab === 'manual' ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'}">
                  2. Login Akun
                </button>
                <button onclick="switchLoginTab('cepat')" class="flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg transition ${loginTab === 'cepat' ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'}">
                  3. Pimpinan & Wali
                </button>
              </div>

              ${loginTab === 'guru' ? `
                <div class="space-y-3">
                  <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <h2 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base">Pilih Nama Guru / Pendidik</h2>
                    <span class="text-xs text-slate-500">Deteksi otomatis multi-jabatan</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto custom-scroll pr-1">
                    ${guruList.map(g => {
                      const jabs = (STATE.db.TeacherJabatans || []).filter(j => j.teacher_id === g.id && j.status === 'Aktif');
                      return `
                        <button onclick="handleQuickLogin('${g.id}')" class="text-left p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-500 hover:bg-indigo-50/50 transition group flex items-start gap-3">
                          <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:bg-indigo-600 group-hover:text-white transition">
                            ${g.avatar_initials || 'GR'}
                          </div>
                          <div class="min-w-0">
                            <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm truncate">${g.full_name}</p>
                            <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">${jabs.length > 0 ? `${jabs.length + 1} Peran Aktif` : 'Guru Mata Pelajaran'}</p>
                          </div>
                        </button>
                      `;
                    }).join('')}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ` : ''}

              ${loginTab === 'manual' ? `
                <form onsubmit="handleManualLogin(event)" class="space-y-4">
                  <h2 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base">Masuk dengan Username & Password</h2>
                  <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">Username</label>
                    <input id="loginUsername" type="text" required placeholder="Contoh: yohanes.um atau admin" class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:outline-none text-sm">
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">Password</label>
                    <input id="loginPassword" type="password" required placeholder="Default demo: 123456" class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:outline-none text-sm">
                  </div>
                  <button type="submit" class="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 transition">
                    Masuk ke Sistem
                  </button>
                </form>
              ` : ''}

              ${loginTab === 'cepat' ? `
                <div class="space-y-3">
                  <h2 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base">Akses Cepat Pimpinan, Admin & Wali Murid</h2>
                  <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5">
                    ${quickOthers.map(u => {
                      const meta = ROLE_META[u.role] || { label: u.role, badge: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700' };
                      return `
                        <button onclick="handleQuickLogin('${u.id}')" class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-teal-500 hover:bg-teal-50/40 transition flex items-center justify-between">
                          <div class="flex items-center gap-3 text-left">
                            <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                              ${u.avatar_initials || 'US'}
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">${u.full_name}</p>
                              <span class="inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-semibold ${meta.badge}">${meta.label}</span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400"></i>
                        </button>
                      `;
                    }).join('')}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ` : ''}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function switchLoginTab(tab) {
      loginTab = tab;
      renderApp();
    }

    async function handleQuickLogin(userId) {
      showLoader('Memverifikasi akun & jabatan...');
      try {
        const res = await callServer('authenticateUser', { mode: 'quick', userId });
        processLoginResponse(res);
      } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    async function handleManualLogin(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const username = document.getElementById('loginUsername').value;
      const password = document.getElementById('loginPassword').value;
      showLoader('Memverifikasi kredensial...');
      try {
        const res = await callServer('authenticateUser', { mode: 'manual', username, password });
        processLoginResponse(res);
      } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    function processLoginResponse(res) {
      if (!res.success) {
        showToast(res.message, 'error');
        return;
      }
      STATE.user = res.user;
      STATE.availableRoles = res.availableRoles || [];

      if (STATE.availableRoles.length > 1) {
        showRoleSwitcherModal(true);
      } else {
        const single = STATE.availableRoles[0] || { role_key: res.user.role, target_id: '' };
        activateRole(single.role_key, single.target_id);
      }
    }

    function showRoleSwitcherModal(isInitialLogin = false) {
      const body = `
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-600 mb-3">
          Halo <strong>${STATE.user.full_name}</strong>, Anda memiliki <strong>${STATE.availableRoles.length} peran aktif</strong> di ${siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}. Silakan pilih ruang kerja yang ingin dibuka:
        </p>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          ${STATE.availableRoles.map(r => {
            const meta = ROLE_META[r.role_key] || { icon: 'briefcase', badge: 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800' };
            return `
              <button onclick="activateRole('${r.role_key}', '${r.target_id || ''}'); closeModal();" class="p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-50/40 text-left transition flex items-start gap-3">
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <i data-lucide="${meta.icon}" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">${r.label}</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">${r.detail || ''}</p>
                </div>
              </button>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      `;
      openModal('Pilih Peran Masuk (Multi-Jabatan)', body, isInitialLogin ? `<button onclick="logout()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-sm font-semibold">Batal</button>` : '');
    }

    function activateRole(roleKey, targetId = '') {
      STATE.activeRole = roleKey;
      STATE.activeTargetId = targetId;
      STATE.mobileDrawerOpen = false;

      // Default menu awal sesuai role
      if (roleKey === 'wali_murid') {
        STATE.activeMenu = 'wm_beranda';
      } else if (roleKey === 'admin_kokurikuler') {
        STATE.activeMenu = 'p5_dashboard';
      } else {
        STATE.activeMenu = 'dashboard';
      }

      // Auto-select kelas wali jika wali_kelas
      if (roleKey === 'wali_kelas' && !targetId) {
        const myCls = (STATE.db.Classes || []).find(c => c.wali_kelas_id === STATE.user.id);
        if (myCls) STATE.activeTargetId = myCls.id;
      }

      showToast(`Masuk sebagai ${ROLE_META[roleKey]?.label || roleKey}`, 'success');
      renderApp();
    }

    function logout() {
      STATE.user = null;
      STATE.activeRole = null;
      STATE.activeTargetId = '';
      STATE.availableRoles = [];
      renderApp();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * MENU & NAVIGATION CONFIGURATION PER ROLE
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function getMenusForActiveRole() {
      const r = STATE.activeRole;

      // Role Guru menggunakan Bottom Bar khusus 5 menu (sidebar dihilangkan)
      if (r === 'guru') {
        return [
          { id: 'my_classes', label: 'Kelas Saya', icon: 'book-open' },
          { id: 'pelanggaran', label: 'Pelanggaran', icon: 'alert-triangle' },
          { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Beranda', icon: 'home', center: true },
          { id: 'guru_wali', label: 'Guru Wali', icon: 'heart-handshake' },
          { id: 'jurnal', label: 'Jurnal KBM', icon: 'file-text' }
        ];
      }

      // Role Wali Murid: 4 menu spesifik TANPA Pengumuman
      if (r === 'wali_murid') {
        return [
          { id: 'wm_beranda', label: 'Beranda', icon: 'home' },
          { id: 'wm_rapor', label: 'Rapor & Capaian Anak', icon: 'award' },
          { id: 'wm_profil', label: 'Profil & Data Siswa', icon: 'user' },
          { id: 'wm_jadwal_ekskul', label: 'Jadwal & Ekskul Anak', icon: 'calendar' },
          { id: 'wm_karakter', label: 'Karakter & Bimbingan Wali', icon: 'heart-handshake' }
        ];
      }

      // Role Admin Kokurikuler (P5): Dashboard + 4 Menu Operasional + Pengumuman
      if (r === 'admin_kokurikuler') {
        return [
          { id: 'p5_dashboard', label: 'Dashboard Kokurikuler', icon: 'layout-dashboard' },
          { id: 'p5_proyek', label: 'Manajemen Proyek', icon: 'folder-kanban' },
          { id: 'p5_jadwal', label: 'Jadwal & Blok Proyek', icon: 'calendar-clock' },
          { id: 'p5_fasilitator', label: 'Penugasan Fasilitator', icon: 'users' },
          { id: 'p5_evaluasi', label: 'Evaluasi Karakter P5', icon: 'check-square' },
          { id: 'pengumuman', label: 'PENGUMUMAN', icon: 'megaphone' }
        ];
      }

      // Role Wali Kelas (Tanpa menu Waka)
      if (r === 'wali_kelas') {
        return [
          { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Beranda Wali Kelas', icon: 'layout-dashboard' },
          { id: 'wk_data_kelas', label: 'Data Kelas', icon: 'school' },
          { id: 'wk_data_siswa', label: 'Data Siswa', icon: 'users' },
          { id: 'wk_absensi', label: 'Absensi Siswa', icon: 'clipboard-check' },
          { id: 'leger', label: 'Penilaian & Leger Nilai', icon: 'table' },
          { id: 'raport', label: 'Pusat Cetak Raport', icon: 'printer' },
          { id: 'pengumuman', label: 'PENGUMUMAN', icon: 'megaphone' }
        ];
      }

      // Role Waka masing-masing
      if (r === 'waka_kurikulum') {
        return [
          { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Beranda Kurikulum', icon: 'layout-dashboard' },
          { id: 'waka_kurikulum', label: 'Modul Waka Kurikulum', icon: 'layers' },
          { id: 'cp_atp', label: 'Data CP & ATP (Prota)', icon: 'book-marked' },
          { id: 'jadwal_mengajar', label: 'Jadwal Pelajaran', icon: 'calendar' },
          { id: 'jadwal_admin', label: 'Atur Jadwal (Semua Guru)', icon: 'calendar-range' },
          { id: 'pengumuman', label: 'PENGUMUMAN', icon: 'megaphone' }
        ];
      }
      if (r === 'waka_kesiswaan') {
        return [
          { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Beranda Kesiswaan', icon: 'layout-dashboard' },
          { id: 'waka_kesiswaan', label: 'Modul Waka Kesiswaan', icon: 'user-check' },
          { id: 'pelanggaran', label: 'Input Pelanggaran', icon: 'alert-triangle' },
          { id: 'ekskul', label: 'Ekstrakurikuler', icon: 'trophy' },
          { id: 'pengumuman', label: 'PENGUMUMAN', icon: 'megaphone' }
        ];
      }
      if (r === 'waka_humas') {
        return [
          { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Beranda Humas', icon: 'layout-dashboard' },
          { id: 'waka_humas', label: 'Modul Waka Humas', icon: 'globe' },
          { id: 'pengumuman', label: 'PENGUMUMAN', icon: 'megaphone' }
        ];
      }
      if (r === 'waka_sarpras') {
        return [
          { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Beranda Sarpras', icon: 'layout-dashboard' },
          { id: 'waka_sarpras', label: 'Modul Waka Sarpras', icon: 'building-2' },
          { id: 'pengumuman', label: 'PENGUMUMAN', icon: 'megaphone' }
        ];
      }

      // Admin & Kepala Sekolah: Akses Penuh
      const baseAdminKs = [
        { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Dashboard Utama', icon: 'layout-dashboard' },
        { id: 'students', label: 'Data Siswa', icon: 'users' },
        { id: 'absensi_siswa', label: 'Absensi Siswa', icon: 'clipboard-check' },
        { id: 'classes_subjects', label: 'Kelas & Mapel', icon: 'school' },
        { id: 'teacher_jabatans', label: 'Jabatan & Tugas Guru', icon: 'briefcase' },
        { id: 'teaching_assignments', label: 'Penugasan Mengajar', icon: 'book-open-check' },
        { id: 'ekskul', label: 'Ekstrakurikuler', icon: 'trophy' },
        { id: 'guru_wali', label: 'Guru Wali', icon: 'heart-handshake' },
        { id: 'additional_assignments', label: 'Penugasan Tambahan', icon: 'award' },
        { id: 'waka_kurikulum', label: 'Waka Kurikulum', icon: 'layers' },
        { id: 'waka_kesiswaan', label: 'Waka Kesiswaan', icon: 'user-check' },
        { id: 'waka_humas', label: 'Waka Humas', icon: 'globe' },
        { id: 'waka_sarpras', label: 'Waka Sarpras', icon: 'building-2' },
        { id: 'p5_dashboard', label: 'Kokurikuler (P5)', icon: 'compass' },
        { id: 'leger', label: 'Leger & Raport', icon: 'printer' },

        baseAdminKs.push({ id: 'system_admin', label: 'Pengguna, RBAC & Backup', icon: 'settings' });
        baseAdminKs.push({ id: 'reset_data', label: 'Reset Data (Tahun Baru)', icon: 'rotate-ccw' });
      }
      return baseAdminKs;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * SHELL RENDERER (NAVBAR, SIDEBAR / GURU BOTTOM BAR, MAIN CONTENT)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderApp() {
      const root = document.getElementById('appRoot');
      if (!STATE.user) {
        root.innerHTML = renderLoginScreen();
        lucide.createIcons();
        return;
      }

      const isGuruRole = STATE.activeRole === 'guru';
      const menus = getMenusForActiveRole();
      const roleMeta = ROLE_META[STATE.activeRole] || { label: STATE.activeRole, badge: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-800' };

      // Hitung jumlah tugas aktif guru untuk badge Kelas Saya
      const myTcCount = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).filter(tc => tc.teacher_id === STATE.user.id).length;

      root.innerHTML = `
        <!-- TOP NAVBAR -->
        <header class="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 lg:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between shadow-2xs">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            ${!isGuruRole ? `
              <button onclick="toggleMobileDrawer()" class="lg:hidden p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100">
                <i data-lucide="menu" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
              </button>
            ` : ''}
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-extrabold text-sm">
                ${siEsc(siInitials(getSchoolIdentity().school_name))}
              </div>
              <div>
                <h1 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-sm sm:text-base leading-none">${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}</h1>
                <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">T.A. ${getSettingVal("academic_year", "2026/2027")} • Semester ${getSettingVal("active_semester", "Ganjil")}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
            <div class="hidden md:flex flex-col items-end">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">${STATE.user.full_name}</span>
              <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold ${roleMeta.badge}">${roleMeta.label}</span>
            </div>

            ${STATE.availableRoles.length > 1 ? `
              <button onclick="showRoleSwitcherModal(false)" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition" title="Ganti Peran Jabatan">
                <i data-lucide="repeat" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                <span class="hidden sm:inline">Ganti Peran</span>
              </button>
            ` : ''}

            <button onclick="logout()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition">
              <i data-lucide="log-out" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span class="hidden sm:inline">Logout</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <!-- BODY LAYOUT -->
        <div class="flex-1 flex relative">
          ${!isGuruRole ? `
            <!-- DESKTOP SIDEBAR -->
            <aside class="hidden lg:flex w-64 bg-white border-r border-slate-200 flex-col p-3 gap-1 shrink-0">
              ${menus.map(m => `
                <button onclick="navigateMenu('${m.id}')" class="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition ${STATE.activeMenu === m.id ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}">
                  <i data-lucide="${m.icon}" class="w-4 h-4 shrink-0"></i>
                  <span class="truncate">${m.label}</span>
                </button>
              `).join('')}
            </aside>

            <!-- MOBILE DRAWER OVERLAY -->
            ${STATE.mobileDrawerOpen ? `
              <div onclick="toggleMobileDrawer()" class="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 z-40 lg:hidden"></div>
              <aside class="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 w-72 bg-white z-50 p-4 flex flex-col gap-1.5 shadow-2xl lg:hidden overflow-y-auto">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-2 border-b border-slate-200">
                  <span class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">Menu Navigasi</span>
                  <button onclick="toggleMobileDrawer()" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"><i data-lucide="x" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
                </div>
                ${menus.map(m => `
                  <button onclick="navigateMenu('${m.id}')" class="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition ${STATE.activeMenu === m.id ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}">
                    <i data-lucide="${m.icon}" class="w-4 h-4 shrink-0"></i>
                    <span class="truncate">${m.label}</span>
                  </button>
                `).join('')}
              </aside>
            ` : ''}
          ` : ''}

          <!-- MAIN WORKSPACE -->
          <main class="flex-1 p-4 sm:p-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full ${isGuruRole ? 'pb-28' : 'pb-10'}">
            ${renderActivePage()}
          </main>
        </div>

        ${isGuruRole ? `
          <!-- BOTTOM NAVIGATION BAR STATIS TEPAT 5 MENU UNTUK ROLE GURU -->
          <nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-30 bg-white border-t border-slate-200 shadow-[0_-4px_20px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)] h-18 px-2 flex items-center justify-around">
            ${menus.map(m => {
              const active = STATE.activeMenu === m.id;
              if (m.center) {
                return `
                  <button onclick="navigateMenu('${m.id}')" class="relative -top-4 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
                    <div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl ${active ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white ring-4 ring-indigo-100' : 'bg-slate-800 text-white'} shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center transition transform hover:scale-105">
                      <i data-lucide="${m.icon}" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                    </div>
                    <span class="text-[11px] font-extrabold mt-1 ${active ? 'text-indigo-700' : 'text-slate-600'}">${m.label}</span>
                  </button>
                `;
              }
              return `
                <button onclick="navigateMenu('${m.id}')" class="flex-1 py-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative ${active ? 'text-indigo-600' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'}">
                  <div class="relative">
                    <i data-lucide="${m.icon}" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
                    ${m.id === 'my_classes' && myTcCount > 0 ? `
                      <span class="absolute -top-1.5 -right-2.5 px-1.5 py-0.2 bg-teal-600 text-white text-[10px] font-bold rounded-full">${myTcCount}</span>
                    ` : ''}
                  </div>
                  <span class="text-[11px] font-bold mt-1 truncate max-w-[76px]">${m.label}</span>
                </button>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </nav>
        ` : ''}
      `;

      lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function toggleMobileDrawer() {
      STATE.mobileDrawerOpen = !STATE.mobileDrawerOpen;
      renderApp();
    }

    function navigateMenu(menuId) {
      STATE.activeMenu = menuId;
      STATE.mobileDrawerOpen = false;
      renderApp();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * ROUTER HALAMAN AKTIF
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderActivePage() {
      const m = STATE.activeMenu;
      const r = STATE.activeRole;

      // Dashboard berdasarkan Role
      if (m === 'dashboard') {
        if (r === 'guru') return renderGuruBeranda();
        if (r === 'wali_kelas') return renderWaliKelasBeranda();
        return renderAdminKepsekDashboard();
      }

      // Modul Guru
      if (m === 'my_classes') return renderGuruMyClasses();
      if (m === 'jurnal') return renderGuruJurnal();
      if (m === 'jadwal_mengajar') return renderGuruJadwal();
      if (m === 'cp_atp') return renderCpAtpProta();
      if (m === 'pelanggaran') return renderPelanggaranModule();
      if (m === 'guru_wali') return renderGuruWaliModule();

      // Modul Admin & Kepsek
      if (m === 'students') return renderStudentsModule();
      if (m === 'classes_subjects') return renderClassesSubjectsModule();
      if (m === 'teacher_jabatans') return renderTeacherJabatansModule();
      if (m === 'teaching_assignments') return renderTeachingAssignmentsModule();
      if (m === 'ekskul') return renderEkskulModule();
      if (m === 'additional_assignments') return renderAdditionalAssignmentsModule();
      if (m === 'system_admin') return renderSystemAdminModule();
      if (m === 'jadwal_admin') {
        if (r !== 'admin' && r !== 'waka_kurikulum') return `<div class="p-8 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 rounded-2xl font-bold">Menu ini khusus Admin / Waka Kurikulum.</div>`;
        return renderJadwalAdminModule();
      }
      if (m === 'absensi_siswa') return renderAbsensiRekapModule();
      if (m === 'wk_data_kelas') return renderWkDataKelas();
      if (m === 'wk_data_siswa') return renderWkDataSiswa();
      if (m === 'wk_absensi') return renderAbsensiWali();
      if (m === 'school_identity') {
        if (r !== 'admin') return `<div class="p-8 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 rounded-2xl font-bold">Menu ini khusus Administrator.</div>`;
        return renderSchoolIdentityModule();
      }
      if (m === 'reset_data') {
        if (r !== 'admin') return `<div class="p-8 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 rounded-2xl font-bold">Menu ini khusus Administrator.</div>`;
        return renderResetDataModule();
      }

      // Modul Kokurikuler (P5)
      if (m.startsWith('p5_')) return renderKokurikulerModule(m);

      // Modul 4 Wakil Kepala Sekolah
      if (m === 'waka_kurikulum') return renderWakaKurikulumModule();
      if (m === 'waka_kesiswaan') return renderWakaKesiswaanModule();
      if (m === 'waka_humas') return renderWakaHumasModule();
      if (m === 'waka_sarpras') return renderWakaSarprasModule();

      // Modul Wali Kelas
      if (m === 'leger') return renderLegerNilaiModule();
      if (m === 'raport') return renderRaportCenterModule();

      // Modul Wali Murid
      if (m.startsWith('wm_')) return renderWaliMuridModule(m);

      // Pengumuman (Dilarang untuk Wali Murid)
      if (m === 'pengumuman') {
        if (r === 'wali_murid') return `<div class="p-8 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 rounded-2xl font-bold">Akses ditolak.</div>`;
        return renderPengumumanModule();
      }

      return `<div class="p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border">Halaman tidak ditemukan.</div>`;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 1. DASHBOARD ADMIN & KEPALA SEKOLAH
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderAdminKepsekDashboard() {
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const teachers = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const addAssign = STATE.db.AdditionalAssignments || [];
      const announcements = (STATE.db.Announcements || []).slice(0, 3);
      const isKepsek = STATE.activeRole === 'kepala_sekolah';

      return `
        <div class="space-y-6">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-700 to-teal-700 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-md">
            <div>
              <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-200">Ringkasan Eksekutif Sekolah</span>
              <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1">Selamat Datang, ${STATE.user.full_name}</h2>
              <p class="text-sm text-indigo-100 mt-1">${siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)} • ${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().school_regency)} • T.A. ${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex gap-2">
              <button onclick="navigateMenu('pengumuman')" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2 transition">
                <i data-lucide="megaphone" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Papan Pengumuman
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          ${renderAbsensiChartCard()}

          <!-- KPI CARDS -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <div onclick="navigateMenu('students')" class="cursor-pointer bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-400 transition shadow-2xs">
              <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Total Peserta Didik</p>
              <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-2">${students.length}</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-teal-600 font-semibold mt-1">L: ${students.filter(s => s.gender === 'L').length} • P: ${students.filter(s => s.gender === 'P').length}</p>
            </div>
            <div onclick="navigateMenu('teacher_jabatans')" class="cursor-pointer bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-400 transition shadow-2xs">
              <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Pendidik (Guru)</p>
              <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-2">${teachers.length}</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-indigo-600 font-semibold mt-1">${(STATE.db.TeacherJabatans || []).length} SK Jabatan Aktif</p>
            </div>
            <div onclick="navigateMenu('classes_subjects')" class="cursor-pointer bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-400 transition shadow-2xs">
              <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Rombongan Belajar</p>
              <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-2">${classes.length}</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold mt-1">KKM Standar: 75</p>
            </div>
            <!-- KARTU KEPANITIAAN & LOMBA YANG DAPAT DIKLIK MENUJU PENUGASAN TAMBAHAN -->
            <div onclick="navigateMenu('additional_assignments')" class="cursor-pointer bg-amber-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border-2 border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-100/70 transition shadow-2xs">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <p class="text-xs font-extrabold text-amber-800 uppercase">Kepanitiaan & Lomba</p>
                <i data-lucide="arrow-up-right" class="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700"></i>
              </div>
              <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-amber-900 mt-2">${addAssign.length}</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-amber-700 font-semibold mt-1">Klik untuk kelola Penugasan Tambahan</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          ${isKepsek ? `
            <!-- RINGKASAN KHUSUS KEPALA SEKOLAH: MONITORING KEPANITIAAN & LOMBA -->
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base flex items-center gap-2">
                  <i data-lucide="award" class="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600"></i> Monitoring Kegiatan Kepanitiaan & Pembimbingan Lomba
                </h3>
                <button onclick="navigateMenu('additional_assignments')" class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:underline">Lihat Semua →</button>
              </div>
              <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                ${addAssign.map(a => `
                  <div onclick="navigateMenu('additional_assignments')" class="cursor-pointer p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-amber-400 bg-slate-50/60 transition">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800">${a.category}</span>
                      <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700">${a.status}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm mt-2">${a.title}</p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">SK: ${a.sk_number || '-'} • Lokasi: ${a.location || '-'}</p>
                  </div>
                `).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>
          ` : ''}

          <!-- PENGUMUMAN TERBARU -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <h3 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base">Pengumuman & Edaran Sekolah Terbaru</h3>
              <button onclick="navigateMenu('pengumuman')" class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:underline">Kelola Pengumuman →</button>
            </div>
            <div class="space-y-3">
              ${announcements.map(ann => `
                <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50">
                  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs mb-1">
                    <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800">${ann.category}</span>
                    <span class="text-slate-400">${ann.created_at}</span>
                  </div>
                  <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">${ann.title}</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1 line-clamp-2">${ann.content}</p>
                </div>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 2. BERANDA KHUSUS GURU (KARTU JADWAL MENGAJAR BESAR + 3 TOMBOL NAVIGASI)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderGuruBeranda() {
      const mySchedules = (STATE.db.JadwalPelajaran || []).filter(j => j.teacher_id === STATE.user.id);
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const jpDuration = Number(((STATE.db.Settings || []).find(s => s.key === 'jp_duration_minutes') || {}).value) || 40;
      const totalJp = mySchedules.reduce((acc, j) => acc + (Number(j.jp_count) || 0), 0) + adminBlocksJp(STATE.user.id);

      return `
        <div class="space-y-6">
          <!-- GREETING HEADER -->
          <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-700 via-indigo-600 to-teal-600 rounded-3xl p-6 text-white shadow-lg">
            <p class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-200">Ruang Kerja Pendidik</p>
            <h2 class="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1">Syalom, ${STATE.user.full_name}</h2>
            <p class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-indigo-100 mt-1">Beban Mengajar Minggu Ini: <strong>${totalJp} JP</strong> (${totalJp * jpDuration} Menit)</p>
          </div>

          ${renderGuruTodayCard()}

          <!-- 3 TOMBOL AKSES CEPAT SESUAI SPESIFIKASI (JADWAL MENGAJAR, PENGUMUMAN, DATA CP & ATP) -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <button onclick="navigateMenu('jadwal_mengajar')" class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border-2 border-indigo-100 hover:border-indigo-500 shadow-2xs flex items-center gap-4 text-left transition group">
              <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 group-hover:bg-indigo-600 group-hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition">
                <i data-lucide="calendar-days" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">Jadwal Mengajar</p>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Atur jam & hari KBM</p>
              </div>
            </button>

            <button onclick="navigateMenu('pengumuman')" class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border-2 border-amber-100 hover:border-amber-500 shadow-2xs flex items-center gap-4 text-left transition group">
              <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 group-hover:bg-amber-500 group-hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition">
                <i data-lucide="megaphone" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">PENGUMUMAN</p>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">${(STATE.db.Announcements || []).length} informasi sekolah</p>
              </div>
            </button>

            <button onclick="navigateMenu('cp_atp')" class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border-2 border-teal-100 hover:border-teal-500 shadow-2xs flex items-center gap-4 text-left transition group">
              <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-teal-100 text-teal-700 group-hover:bg-teal-600 group-hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition">
                <i data-lucide="book-marked" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">Data CP & ATP (Prota)</p>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Kurikulum & Progres TP</p>
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>

          <!-- KARTU BESAR JADWAL MENGAJAR GURU -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6 shadow-xs">
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-5">
              <div>
                <h3 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <i data-lucide="clock" class="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600"></i> Jadwal Mengajar Minggu Ini
                </h3>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Klik kartu jadwal untuk langsung membuka Kelas Saya & mengisi Absensi/Nilai</p>
              </div>
              <button onclick="navigateMenu('jadwal_mengajar')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 font-bold text-xs hover:bg-indigo-100">
                + Kelola Jadwal
              </button>
            </div>

            ${mySchedules.length === 0 ? `
              <div class="p-8 text-center bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300">
                <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">Belum ada jadwal mengajar yang tercatat.</p>
                <button onclick="navigateMenu('jadwal_mengajar')" class="mt-3 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Tambah Jadwal Sekarang</button>
              </div>
            ` : `
              <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                ${mySchedules.map(j => {
                  const cls = classes.find(c => c.id === j.class_id) || { name: j.class_id };
                  const sub = subjects.find(s => s.id === j.subject_id) || { name: j.subject_id };
                  return `
                    <div onclick="openWorkspaceFromSchedule('${j.class_id}', '${j.subject_id}')" class="cursor-pointer p-5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-indigo-50/40 border-2 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-500 transition shadow-2xs">
                      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                        <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-extrabold text-xs uppercase">${j.day_name}</span>
                        <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 bg-white px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                          Pukul ${normTime(j.start_time)} – ${addMin(j.start_time, (Number(j.jp_count) || 0) * jpDuration)} • ${j.jp_count} JP (${Number(j.jp_count) * jpDuration} mnt)
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <h4 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">Kelas ${cls.name} — ${sub.name}</h4>
                      <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <i data-lucide="map-pin" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600"></i> Lokasi: ${j.room_note || 'Ruang Kelas'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  `;
                }).join('')}
              </div>
            `}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openWorkspaceFromSchedule(classId, subjectId) {
      const tc = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).find(t => t.teacher_id === STATE.user.id && t.class_id === classId && t.subject_id === subjectId);
      if (tc) {
        STATE.selectedTeacherClassId = tc.id;
        STATE.selectedSessionId = null;
      }
      STATE.myClassesTab = 'intra';
      navigateMenu('my_classes');
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 3. KELAS SAYA (4 TAB PILAR) & WORKSPACE INTRAKURIKULER
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderGuruMyClasses() {
      const tab = STATE.myClassesTab;
      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Kelas Saya (4 Pilar Pembelajaran)</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Kelola Intrakurikuler, Ekstrakurikuler, Kokurikuler (P5), dan Penugasan Tambahan</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- 4 TAB PILAR -->
          <div class="flex overflow-x-auto gap-2 bg-white p-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200">
            ${[
              { id: 'intra', label: '1. Intrakurikuler', icon: 'book-open' },
              { id: 'ekskul', label: '2. Ekstrakurikuler', icon: 'trophy' },
              { id: 'p5', label: '3. Kokurikuler (P5)', icon: 'compass' },
              { id: 'tambahan', label: '4. Penugasan Tambahan', icon: 'award' }
            ].map(t => `
              <button onclick="STATE.myClassesTab='${t.id}'; renderApp();" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap transition ${tab === t.id ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}">
                <i data-lucide="${t.icon}" class="w-4 h-4"></i> ${t.label}
              </button>
            `).join('')}
          </div>

          ${tab === 'intra' ? renderWorkspaceIntra() : ''}
          ${tab === 'ekskul' ? renderGuruEkskulTab() : ''}
          ${tab === 'p5' ? renderGuruP5Tab() : ''}
          ${tab === 'tambahan' ? renderGuruTambahanTab() : ''}
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function renderWorkspaceIntra() {
      const myTcs = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).filter(tc => tc.teacher_id === STATE.user.id);
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];

      if (myTcs.length === 0) {
        return `<div class="p-8 bg-white rounded-2xl border text-center text-sm text-slate-500">Anda belum memiliki penugasan mengajar intrakurikuler. Hubungi Admin/Waka Kurikulum.</div>`;
      }

      if (!STATE.selectedTeacherClassId || !myTcs.some(t => t.id === STATE.selectedTeacherClassId)) {
        STATE.selectedTeacherClassId = myTcs[0].id;
      }

      const activeTc = myTcs.find(t => t.id === STATE.selectedTeacherClassId);
      const activeClass = classes.find(c => c.id === activeTc.class_id) || { name: activeTc.class_id, kkm: 75, grade_level: '7' };
      const activeSub = subjects.find(s => s.id === activeTc.subject_id) || { name: activeTc.subject_id };

      // Filter sesi pertemuan untuk kelas & mapel aktif
      let sessions = (STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || []).filter(s => s.class_id === activeTc.class_id && s.subject_id === activeTc.subject_id);
      if (STATE.sessionFilter !== 'ALL') {
        sessions = sessions.filter(s => s.session_type === STATE.sessionFilter);
      }

      const selectedSession = sessions.find(s => s.id === STATE.selectedSessionId) || null;
      const classStudents = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(st => st.class_id === activeTc.class_id && st.status === 'Aktif');
      const allRecords = STATE.db.AttendanceRecords || [];

      return `
        <div class="space-y-4">
          <!-- PEMILIH ROMBEL & MAPEL -->
          <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Pilih Kelas & Mapel:</span>
              <select onchange="STATE.selectedTeacherClassId=this.value; STATE.selectedSessionId=null; renderApp();" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-bold text-sm text-slate-800 bg-slate-50">
                ${myTcs.map(tc => {
                  const c = classes.find(x => x.id === tc.class_id) || { name: tc.class_id };
                  const s = subjects.find(x => x.id === tc.subject_id) || { name: tc.subject_id };
                  return `<option value="${tc.id}" ${tc.id === activeTc.id ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas ${c.name} — ${s.name}</option>`;
                }).join('')}
              </select>
              <span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold text-xs border border-emerald-200">KKM: ${activeClass.kkm}</span>
            </div>

            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
              ${renderNilaiFinalControl(activeTc)}
              <button onclick="openOfficialScoreReportModal('${activeTc.class_id}', '${activeTc.subject_id}')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="file-spreadsheet" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Rekap & Cetak Nilai Resmi
              </button>
              <button onclick="openAddSessionModal('${activeTc.id}', '${activeTc.class_id}', '${activeTc.subject_id}')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="plus" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Tambah Sesi Pertemuan
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- SPLIT VIEW: KIRI DAFTAR SESI, KANAN TABEL ABSENSI & NILAI (MUNCUL SETELAH SESI DIKLIK) -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-5">
            <!-- PANEL KIRI: DAFTAR SESI PERTEMUAN -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-4 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">Daftar Sesi (${sessions.length})</h3>
                <div class="flex gap-1 bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold">
                  ${['ALL', 'NH', 'STS', 'SAS'].map(f => `
                    <button onclick="STATE.sessionFilter='${f}'; renderApp();" class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md ${STATE.sessionFilter === f ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-2xs' : 'text-slate-500'}">
                      ${f === 'ALL' ? 'Semua' : f}
                    </button>
                  `).join('')}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div class="space-y-2 max-h-[520px] overflow-y-auto custom-scroll pr-1">
                ${sessions.length === 0 ? `
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 text-center py-6">Belum ada sesi pertemuan. Klik "+ Tambah Sesi Pertemuan".</p>
                ` : sessions.map(s => {
                  const isSel = selectedSession && selectedSession.id === s.id;
                  const typeColors = { NH: 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800', STS: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800', SAS: 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800' };
                  return `
                    <div onclick="STATE.selectedSessionId='${s.id}'; renderApp();" class="cursor-pointer p-3.5 rounded-xl border-2 transition ${isSel ? 'border-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50/50' : 'border-slate-100 hover:border-slate-300 bg-slate-50/50'}">
                      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-extrabold ${typeColors[s.session_type] || 'bg-slate-200'}">${s.session_type}</span>
                        <span class="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500">${s.session_date}</span>
                      </div>
                      <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-xs sm:text-sm mt-1.5">${s.title}</p>
                      ${s.atp_code ? `<p class="text-[11px] text-indigo-600 font-semibold mt-1">ATP: ${s.atp_code}</p>` : ''}
                    </div>
                  `;
                }).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- PANEL KANAN: DETAIL SESI & INPUT NILAI/ABSENSI -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-8">
              ${!selectedSession ? `
                <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 p-12 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[340px]">
                  <div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
                    <i data-lucide="mouse-pointer-click" class="w-7 h-7"></i>
                  </div>
                  <h4 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base">Pilih Salah Satu Sesi Pertemuan</h4>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-md mt-1">
                    Klik daftar sesi pertemuan di sebelah kiri untuk membuka lembar absensi kehadiran, input nilai & predikat massal, serta catatan pembelajaran kelas.
                  </p>
                </div>
              ` : `
                <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4 shadow-2xs">
                  <!-- HEADER DETAIL SESI -->
                  <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-4 border-b border-slate-200">
                    <div>
                      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-extrabold">${selectedSession.session_type}</span>
                        <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base">${selectedSession.title}</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mt-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
                        <label class="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-semibold">
                          <span>Tanggal Pertemuan:</span>
                          <input type="date" value="${selectedSession.session_date}" onchange="updateSessionDate('${selectedSession.id}', this.value)" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">
                        </label>
                        ${selectedSession.atp_code ? `<span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 font-bold">ATP: ${selectedSession.atp_code}</span>` : ''}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <button onclick="openKbmNotesModal('${selectedSession.id}')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <i data-lucide="clipboard-edit" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Catatan Pembelajaran Kelas
                      </button>
                      <button onclick="deleteSessionConfirm('${selectedSession.id}')" class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-100" title="Hapus Sesi">
                        <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <!-- BAR AKSI CEPAT: HADIR SEMUA & ISI NILAI MASSAL PREDIKAT -->
                  <div class="bg-slate-50 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <button onclick="setAllPresent()" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 w-fit">
                      <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Set Hadir Semua (H)
                    </button>

                    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">Isi Nilai Massal Predikat:</span>
                      <select id="bulkPredicateSelect" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold bg-white">
                        <option value="A+">A+ (98)</option>
                        <option value="A">A (94)</option>
                        <option value="A-">A- (90)</option>
                        <option value="B+" selected>B+ (${predicateToScoreByKkm('B+', activeClass.kkm)})</option>
                        <option value="B">B (${predicateToScoreByKkm('B', activeClass.kkm)})</option>
                        <option value="B-">B- (KKM ${activeClass.kkm})</option>
                        <option value="C+">C+ (${predicateToScoreByKkm('C+', activeClass.kkm)})</option>
                        <option value="C">C (64)</option>
                        <option value="D">D (55)</option>
                      </select>
                      <button onclick="applyBulkPredicate(${activeClass.kkm})" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">
                        Terapkan ke Semua
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <!-- TABEL ABSENSI & PENILAIAN SISWA -->
                  <div class="overflow-x-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-xl">
                    <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
                      <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs">
                        <tr>
                          <th class="p-3 w-10">No</th>
                          <th class="p-3">Nama Peserta Didik</th>
                          <th class="p-3 text-center">Kehadiran (H/S/I/A)</th>
                          <th class="p-3 w-24 text-center">Nilai (0-100)</th>
                          <th class="p-3 w-28 text-center">Predikat</th>
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-200">
                        ${classStudents.map((st, idx) => {
                          const rec = allRecords.find(r => r.session_id === selectedSession.id && r.student_id === st.id) || { status: 'H', score: '', predicate: '' };
                          const autoPred = rec.predicate || scoreToGradeLetter(rec.score, activeClass.kkm);
                          return `
                            <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50" data-student-row="${st.id}">
                              <td class="p-3 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">${idx + 1}</td>
                              <td class="p-3">
                                <p class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-xs sm:text-sm">${st.full_name}</p>
                                <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">NIS: ${st.nis}</p>
                              </td>
                              <td class="p-3">
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                                  ${['H', 'S', 'I', 'A'].map(code => `
                                    <label class="cursor-pointer">
                                      <input type="radio" name="att_${st.id}" value="${code}" ${ (rec.status || 'H') === code ? 'checked' : '' } class="sr-only peer">
                                      <span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold border border-slate-200 peer-checked:bg-indigo-600 peer-checked:text-white peer-checked:border-indigo-600 block">
                                        ${code}
                                      </span>
                                    </label>
                                  `).join('')}
                                </div>
                              </td>
                              <td class="p-3 text-center">
                                <input type="number" min="0" max="100" id="score_${st.id}" value="${rec.score !== undefined ? rec.score : ''}" oninput="syncRowPredicate('${st.id}', ${activeClass.kkm})" class="w-20 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-center font-bold text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500">
                              </td>
                              <td class="p-3 text-center">
                                <select id="pred_${st.id}" onchange="syncRowScoreFromPred('${st.id}', ${activeClass.kkm})" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs font-extrabold bg-slate-50">
                                  <option value="-" ${autoPred === '-' ? 'selected' : ''}>-</option>
                                  ${['A+', 'A', 'A-', 'B+', 'B', 'B-', 'C+', 'C', 'D'].map(p => `
                                    <option value="${p}" ${autoPred === p ? 'selected' : ''}>${p}</option>
                                  `).join('')}
                                </select>
                              </td>
                            </tr>
                          `;
                        }).join('')}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </div>

                  <div class="flex justify-end pt-2">
                    <button onclick="saveCurrentSessionRecords('${selectedSession.id}')" class="px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-extrabold text-sm shadow-md flex items-center gap-2">
                      <i data-lucide="save" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Simpan Absensi & Nilai Sesi Ini
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              `}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function syncRowPredicate(studentId, kkm) {
      const val = document.getElementById('score_' + studentId).value;
      const predEl = document.getElementById('pred_' + studentId);
      predEl.value = scoreToGradeLetter(val, kkm);
    }

    function syncRowScoreFromPred(studentId, kkm) {
      const pred = document.getElementById('pred_' + studentId).value;
      const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score_' + studentId);
      if (pred === '-') {
        scoreEl.value = '';
      } else {
        scoreEl.value = predicateToScoreByKkm(pred, kkm);
      }
    }

    function setAllPresent() {
      document.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"][value="H"]').forEach(r => { r.checked = true; });
      showToast('Seluruh siswa ditandai Hadir (H). Jangan lupa klik Simpan.', 'info');
    }

    function applyBulkPredicate(kkm) {
      const pred = document.getElementById('bulkPredicateSelect').value;
      const score = predicateToScoreByKkm(pred, kkm);
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-student-row]').forEach(row => {
        const sid = row.getAttribute('data-student-row');
        document.getElementById('score_' + sid).value = score;
        document.getElementById('pred_' + sid).value = pred;
      });
      showToast(`Predikat ${pred} (Nilai ${score}) diterapkan ke seluruh siswa.`, 'info');
    }

    async function saveCurrentSessionRecords(sessionId) {
      const rows = document.querySelectorAll('[data-student-row]');
      const payload = [];
      rows.forEach(row => {
        const sid = row.getAttribute('data-student-row');
        const checkedRadio = row.querySelector(`input[name="att_${sid}"]:checked`);
        const status = checkedRadio ? checkedRadio.value : 'H';
        const scoreVal = document.getElementById('score_' + sid).value;
        const predVal = document.getElementById('pred_' + sid).value;
        payload.push({
          student_id: sid,
          status,
          score: scoreVal === '' ? '' : Number(scoreVal),
          predicate: predVal === '-' ? '' : predVal,
          note: ''
        });
      });

      showLoader('Menyimpan nilai & presensi ke Google Sheets...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        await callServer('saveSessionAttendanceBatch', sessionId, payload, actor);
        await loadAppData(true);
        showToast('Absensi & Nilai sesi berhasil disimpan!', 'success');
      } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    async function updateSessionDate(sessionId, newDate) {
      const sess = (STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || []).find(s => s.id === sessionId);
      if (!sess) return;
      await apiSave('AttendanceSessions', { ...sess, session_date: newDate }, 'Tanggal sesi diperbarui!');
    }

    async function deleteSessionConfirm(sessionId) {
      await apiDelete('AttendanceSessions', sessionId, 'Sesi pertemuan dihapus.');
      STATE.selectedSessionId = null;
      renderApp();
    }

    /**
     * MODAL TAMBAH SESI PERTEMUAN (MANUAL & OTOMATIS DARI PROTA)
     */
    function openAddSessionModal(tcId, classId, subjectId) {
      const cls = (STATE.db.Classes || []).find(c => c.id === classId) || { grade_level: '7' };
      const atps = (STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || []).filter(a => a.subject_id === subjectId && String(a.grade_level) === String(cls.grade_level));
      const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

      const body = `
        <div class="space-y-4">
          <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 text-xs text-indigo-900">
            Anda dapat menambahkan <strong>1 Sesi Manual</strong> maupun men-generate <strong>Banyak Sesi Otomatis dari Prota</strong> berdasarkan jumlah pertemuan pada materi Prota.
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <!-- FORM MANUAL -->
            <form onsubmit="submitManualSession(event, '${tcId}', '${classId}', '${subjectId}')" class="p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
              <h4 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">Opsi A: Tambah Sesi Manual</h4>
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Jenis Asesmen / Sesi</label>
                <select id="mSessType" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold">
                  <option value="NH">NH (Nilai Harian / Formatif)</option>
                  <option value="STS">STS (Sumatif Tengah Semester)</option>
                  <option value="SAS">SAS (Sumatif Akhir Semester)</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Tautkan Alur Tujuan Pembelajaran (ATP)</label>
                <select id="mSessAtp" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs">
                  <option value="">-- Pilih ATP dari Prota --</option>
                  ${atps.map(a => `<option value="${a.id}">${a.atp_code} — ${shortenTpText(a.tp_text, 55)}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Judul Pertemuan / Penilaian</label>
                <input id="mSessTitle" required placeholder="Contoh: NH 3: Kalor & Perpindahannya" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs">
              </div>
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Tanggal Pertemuan</label>
                <input id="mSessDate" type="date" required value="${today}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs">
              </div>
              <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs">Simpan Sesi Manual</button>
            </form>

            <!-- FORM OTOMATIS DARI PROTA -->
            <form onsubmit="submitAutoProtaSessions(event, '${tcId}', '${classId}', '${subjectId}')" class="p-4 rounded-2xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/30 space-y-3">
