              <h4 class="font-extrabold text-teal-900 text-sm">Opsi B: Generate Otomatis dari Prota</h4>
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Pilih Lingkup Materi / ATP Prota</label>
                <select id="pSessAtp" required onchange="onSelectProtaForAuto(this.value)" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs bg-white">
                  <option value="">-- Pilih Materi Prota --</option>
                  ${atps.map(a => `<option value="${a.id}" data-count="${a.meeting_count || 2}" data-scope="${a.scope_material}">${a.atp_code}: ${a.scope_material} (${a.meeting_count || 2} Pertemuan)</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <div>
                  <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Jumlah Pertemuan</label>
                  <input id="pSessCount" type="number" min="1" max="16" value="3" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs bg-white">
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Interval Hari</label>
                  <input id="pSessInterval" type="number" min="1" max="30" value="7" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs bg-white">
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Tanggal Mulai Pertemuan 1</label>
                <input id="pSessStart" type="date" required value="${today}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs bg-white">
              </div>
              <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white font-bold text-xs">
                ⚡ Generate Sesi Otomatis
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      openModal('Tambah Sesi Pertemuan & Penilaian', body);
    }

    function onSelectProtaForAuto(atpId) {
      const sel = document.getElementById('pSessAtp');
      const opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
      if (opt && opt.getAttribute('data-count')) {
        document.getElementById('pSessCount').value = opt.getAttribute('data-count');
      }
    }

    async function submitManualSession(e, tcId, classId, subjectId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const atpId = document.getElementById('mSessAtp').value;
      const atpObj = (STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || []).find(a => a.id === atpId);
      const rec = await apiSave('AttendanceSessions', {
        teacher_class_id: tcId,
        class_id: classId,
        subject_id: subjectId,
        teacher_id: STATE.user.id,
        session_type: document.getElementById('mSessType').value,
        atp_id: atpId,
        atp_code: atpObj ? atpObj.atp_code : '',
        title: document.getElementById('mSessTitle').value,
        session_date: document.getElementById('mSessDate').value
      }, 'Sesi pertemuan berhasil ditambahkan!');
      if (rec) STATE.selectedSessionId = rec.id;
      closeModal();
    }

    async function submitAutoProtaSessions(e, tcId, classId, subjectId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const atpId = document.getElementById('pSessAtp').value;
      const atpObj = (STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || []).find(a => a.id === atpId);
      if (!atpObj) return;

      showLoader('Men-generate sesi pertemuan dari Prota...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        await callServer('createSessionsFromProta', {
          teacher_class_id: tcId,
          class_id: classId,
          subject_id: subjectId,
          teacher_id: STATE.user.id,
          session_type: 'NH',
          atp_id: atpObj.id,
          atp_code: atpObj.atp_code,
          title_prefix: atpObj.scope_material,
          meeting_count: document.getElementById('pSessCount').value,
          interval_days: document.getElementById('pSessInterval').value,
          start_date: document.getElementById('pSessStart').value
        }, actor);
        await loadAppData(true);
        showToast('Sesi pertemuan otomatis dari Prota berhasil dibuat!', 'success');
        closeModal();
      } catch (err) {
        showToast(err.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    /**
     * MODAL CATATAN PEMBELAJARAN KELAS (TERSINKRON KE JURNAL MENGAJAR)
     */
    function openKbmNotesModal(sessionId) {
      const sess = (STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || []).find(s => s.id === sessionId);
      if (!sess) return;
      const actList = ['Ceramah Interaktif', 'Diskusi Kelompok', 'Presentasi', 'Penugasan', 'Asesmen Formatif', 'Praktik/Demonstrasi'];
      const currentActs = String(sess.kbm_activities || '').split(',').map(x => x.trim());

      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveKbmNotes(event, '${sessionId}')" class="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-2">Aktivitas Pembelajaran (Pilih Cepat)</label>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              ${actList.map(act => `
                <label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-50">
                  <input type="checkbox" name="kbmAct" value="${act}" ${currentActs.includes(act) ? 'checked' : ''} class="rounded text-indigo-600">
                  <span>${act}</span>
                </label>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Permasalahan dalam KBM</label>
            <textarea id="kbmProb" rows="2" placeholder="Catat kendala pemahaman siswa atau sarana..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs">${sess.kbm_problems || ''}</textarea>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Solusi & Tindak Lanjut</label>
            <textarea id="kbmSol" rows="2" placeholder="Langkah remedial / pengayaan yang dilakukan..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs">${sess.kbm_solutions || ''}</textarea>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs">Simpan Catatan Pembelajaran (Tersinkron ke Jurnal)</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Catatan Pembelajaran Kelas — ' + sess.title, body);
    }

    async function saveKbmNotes(e, sessionId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const sess = (STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || []).find(s => s.id === sessionId);
      const checked = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="kbmAct"]:checked')).map(c => c.value).join(', ');
      await apiSave('AttendanceSessions', {
        ...sess,
        kbm_activities: checked,
        kbm_problems: document.getElementById('kbmProb').value,
        kbm_solutions: document.getElementById('kbmSol').value
      }, 'Catatan pembelajaran disimpan & siap ditarik di Jurnal Mengajar!');
      closeModal();
    }

    /**
     * MODAL LAPORAN NILAI RESMI (31 KOLOM / RINGKAS, SIKAP 100 / 1-5, TSV, EXCEL, PRINT)
     */
    function openOfficialScoreReportModal(classId, subjectId) {
      const cls = (STATE.db.Classes || []).find(c => c.id === classId) || { name: classId, kkm: 75 };
      const sub = (STATE.db.Subjects || []).find(s => s.id === subjectId) || { name: subjectId };
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(st => st.class_id === classId && st.status === 'Aktif');
      const attitudes = (STATE.db.AttitudeRatings || []).filter(a => a.class_id === classId && a.subject_id === subjectId);

      let formatMode = '31col';
      let attitudeScale = '100';

      const buildReportTable = () => {
        const nhCols = Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => `NH${i + 1}`);
        const headers = formatMode === '31col'
          ? ['No', 'NIS', 'Nama Siswa', 'L/P', ...nhCols, 'Rerata NH', 'STS', 'SAS', 'Nilai Akhir', 'Predikat', 'Sikap', 'Ketuntasan', 'Deskripsi Capaian TP']
          : ['No', 'NIS', 'Nama Siswa', 'Rerata NH', 'STS', 'SAS', 'Nilai Akhir', 'Predikat', 'Sikap', 'Ketuntasan', 'Deskripsi Capaian Kompetensi'];

        const rows = students.map((st, idx) => {
          const rep = getStudentSubjectReport(st.id, classId, subjectId);
          const stAtt = attitudes.filter(a => a.student_id === st.id);
          const avgAtt5 = stAtt.length ? (stAtt.reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.score || 4), 0) / stAtt.length) : 4.5;
          const attDisplay = attitudeScale === '100' ? Math.round(avgAtt5 * 20) : avgAtt5.toFixed(1);
          const statusTuntas = rep.finalScore !== null ? (rep.finalScore >= cls.kkm ? 'Tuntas' : 'Belum Tuntas') : '-';

          if (formatMode === '31col') {
            const nhCells = Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => rep.nhScores[i] !== undefined ? rep.nhScores[i] : '-');
            return [idx + 1, st.nis, st.full_name, st.gender, ...nhCells, rep.avgNH ?? '-', rep.avgSTS ?? '-', rep.avgSAS ?? '-', rep.finalScore ?? '-', rep.predicate, attDisplay, statusTuntas, rep.description];
          }
          return [idx + 1, st.nis, st.full_name, rep.avgNH ?? '-', rep.avgSTS ?? '-', rep.avgSAS ?? '-', rep.finalScore ?? '-', rep.predicate, attDisplay, statusTuntas, rep.description];
        });

        return { headers, rows };
      };

      const renderInsideModal = () => {
        const { headers, rows } = buildReportTable();
        const bodyHtml = `
          <div class="space-y-4">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
              <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <select id="repFmtSel" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-bold">
                  <option value="31col" ${formatMode === '31col' ? 'selected' : ''}>Format Lengkap (31 Kolom)</option>
                  <option value="ringkas" ${formatMode === 'ringkas' ? 'selected' : ''}>Format Ringkas</option>
                </select>
                <select id="repAttSel" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-bold">
                  <option value="100" ${attitudeScale === '100' ? 'selected' : ''}>Sikap Skala 100</option>
                  <option value="5" ${attitudeScale === '5' ? 'selected' : ''}>Sikap Skala 1-5</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button id="btnCopyTsvRep" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">Salin TSV</button>
                <button id="btnXlsxRep" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">Download .xlsx</button>
                <button id="btnPrintRep" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">Cetak / PDF</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="overflow-x-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-xl max-h-96">
              <table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr>${headers.map(h => `<th class="p-2 border border-slate-700 whitespace-nowrap">${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-200">
                  ${rows.map(r => `<tr>${r.map((c, i) => `<td class="p-2 border border-slate-200 ${i === 2 ? 'font-bold whitespace-nowrap' : ''}">${c}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
        openModal(`Daftar Nilai Resmi — Kelas ${cls.name} (${sub.name})`, bodyHtml, '', 'max-w-6xl');

        document.getElementById('repFmtSel').onchange = e => { formatMode = e.target.value; renderInsideModal(); };
        document.getElementById('repAttSel').onchange = e => { attitudeScale = e.target.value; renderInsideModal(); };
        document.getElementById('btnCopyTsvRep').onclick = () => copyTableTsv([headers, ...rows]);
        document.getElementById('btnXlsxRep').onclick = () => exportTableToExcel([headers, ...rows], `Nilai_${cls.name}`, `Daftar_Nilai_${cls.name}_${sub.code || sub.name}.xlsx`);
        document.getElementById('btnPrintRep').onclick = () => {
          const printHtml = `
            ${renderOfficialLetterhead()}
            <h3 style="text-align:center;font-weight:bold;margin:10px 0;">DAFTAR NILAI HASIL BELAJAR PESERTA DIDIK</h3>
            <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:8px;">Mata Pelajaran: <strong>${sub.name}</strong> | Kelas: <strong>${cls.name}</strong> | KKM: <strong>${cls.kkm}</strong></p>
            <table>
              <thead><tr>${headers.map(h => `<th>${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
              <tbody>${rows.map(r => `<tr>${r.map(c => `<td>${c}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody>
            </table>
            ${renderSignatureBlock('Guru Mata Pelajaran', STATE.user.full_name, STATE.user.nip)}
          `;
          triggerPrintHtml(printHtml);
        };
      };

      renderInsideModal();
    }

    /**
     * TAB 2, 3, 4 DI KELAS SAYA GURU (EKSKUL, KOKURIKULER P5 FULL CRUD, PENUGASAN TAMBAHAN)
     */
    function renderGuruEkskulTab() {
      const myEkskuls = (STATE.db.Ekstrakurikuler || []).filter(e => e.coach_id === STATE.user.id || STATE.activeRole === 'admin');
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const assessments = STATE.db.EkskulAssessments || [];

      if (myEkskuls.length === 0) {
        return `<div class="p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border text-sm text-slate-500">Anda tidak terdaftar sebagai Pembina Ekstrakurikuler.</div>`;
      }

      return `
        <div class="space-y-4">
          ${myEkskuls.map(eks => {
            const members = assessments.filter(a => a.ekskul_id === eks.id);
            return `
              <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-3">
                <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base">${eks.name}</h3>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Jadwal: ${eks.schedule} • Peserta: ${members.length} Siswa</p>
                  </div>
                  <button onclick="openAddEkskulMemberModal('${eks.id}')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah / Nilai Anggota</button>
                </div>
                <div class="overflow-x-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-xl">
                  <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                    <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                      <tr><th class="p-2.5">Nama Siswa</th><th class="p-2.5">Predikat</th><th class="p-2.5">Catatan Pembina</th><th class="p-2.5 text-right">Aksi</th></tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody class="divide-y">
                      ${members.map(m => {
                        const st = students.find(s => s.id === m.student_id) || { full_name: m.student_id };
                        return `
                          <tr>
                            <td class="p-2.5 font-bold">${st.full_name}</td>
                            <td class="p-2.5"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-bold">${m.predicate}</span></td>
                            <td class="p-2.5">${m.note || '-'}</td>
                            <td class="p-2.5 text-right">
                              <button onclick="openEditEkskulAssessmentModal('${m.id}')" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold hover:underline">Edit</button>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        `;
                      }).join('')}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddEkskulMemberModal(ekskulId) {
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveEkskulAssessment(event, '', '${ekskulId}')" class="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold mb-1">Pilih Siswa</label>
            <select id="ekStudentId" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs">
              ${students.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}">${s.full_name} (${s.class_id})</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold mb-1">Predikat</label>
            <select id="ekPred" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
              <option value="Sangat Baik">Sangat Baik</option>
              <option value="Baik" selected>Baik</option>
              <option value="Cukup">Cukup</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold mb-1">Catatan Pembina</label>
            <textarea id="ekNote" rows="2" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs" placeholder="Deskripsi keaktifan & capaian siswa..."></textarea>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white font-bold text-xs">Simpan Penilaian Ekskul</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Input Penilaian Ekstrakurikuler', body);
    }

    function openEditEkskulAssessmentModal(assId) {
      const item = (STATE.db.EkskulAssessments || []).find(a => a.id === assId);
      if (!item) return;
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveEkskulAssessment(event, '${item.id}', '${item.ekskul_id}', '${item.student_id}')" class="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold mb-1">Predikat</label>
            <select id="ekPred" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
              ${['Sangat Baik', 'Baik', 'Cukup'].map(p => `<option value="${p}" ${item.predicate === p ? 'selected' : ''}>${p}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold mb-1">Catatan Pembina</label>
            <textarea id="ekNote" rows="3" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs">${item.note || ''}</textarea>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs">Perbarui Nilai</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Edit Penilaian Ekstrakurikuler', body);
    }

    async function saveEkskulAssessment(e, id, ekskulId, fixedStudentId = '') {
      e.preventDefault();
      await apiSave('EkskulAssessments', {
        id: id || '',
        ekskul_id: ekskulId,
        student_id: fixedStudentId || document.getElementById('ekStudentId').value,
        predicate: document.getElementById('ekPred').value,
        note: document.getElementById('ekNote').value,
        semester: getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil')
      }, 'Penilaian ekstrakurikuler tersimpan!');
      closeModal();
    }

    /**
     * TAB 3 GURU: KOKURIKULER (P5) — FULL CRUD MULTI-DIMENSI PER SISWA
     */
    function renderGuruP5Tab() {
      const projects = (STATE.db.KokurikulerProjects || []).filter(p =>
        p.coordinator_id === STATE.user.id || String(p.facilitator_ids || '').includes(STATE.user.id)
      );
      const evals = STATE.db.KokurikulerEvaluations || [];
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];

      if (projects.length === 0) {
        return `<div class="p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border text-sm text-slate-500">Anda belum ditugaskan sebagai Koordinator atau Fasilitator Proyek P5.</div>`;
      }

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          ${projects.map(prj => {
            const pEvals = evals.filter(ev => ev.project_id === prj.id);
            return `
              <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4">
                <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 text-xs font-bold">${prj.theme}</span>
                    <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base mt-1">${prj.title}</h3>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Dimensi: <strong>${prj.dimensions}</strong></p>
                  </div>
                  <button onclick="openP5EvaluationCrudModal('${prj.id}', '')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">
                    + Input Evaluasi P5 Siswa
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div class="overflow-x-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-xl">
                  <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                    <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                      <tr>
                        <th class="p-2.5">Siswa</th>
                        <th class="p-2.5">Kelas</th>
                        <th class="p-2.5">Capaian Per Dimensi</th>
                        <th class="p-2.5">Catatan Proses</th>
                        <th class="p-2.5 text-right">Aksi</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody class="divide-y">
                      ${pEvals.map(ev => {
                        const st = students.find(s => s.id === ev.student_id) || { full_name: ev.student_id };
                        const cls = classes.find(c => c.id === ev.class_id) || { name: ev.class_id };
                        let dimObj = {};
                        try { dimObj = JSON.parse(ev.dimension_scores || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
                        return `
                          <tr>
                            <td class="p-2.5 font-bold">${st.full_name}</td>
                            <td class="p-2.5">${cls.name}</td>
                            <td class="p-2.5">
                              ${Object.entries(dimObj).map(([k, v]) => `<span class="inline-block mr-1 mb-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-800 font-bold">${k}: ${v}</span>`).join('') || ev.final_predicate}
                            </td>
                            <td class="p-2.5">${ev.note || '-'}</td>
                            <td class="p-2.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap space-x-2">
                              <button onclick="openP5EvaluationCrudModal('${prj.id}', '${ev.id}')" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold">Edit</button>
                              <button onclick="apiDelete('KokurikulerEvaluations', '${ev.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        `;
                      }).join('')}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openP5EvaluationCrudModal(projectId, evalId = '') {
      const prj = (STATE.db.KokurikulerProjects || []).find(p => p.id === projectId);
      if (!prj) return;
      const existing = evalId ? (STATE.db.KokurikulerEvaluations || []).find(e => e.id === evalId) : null;
      const targetClasses = String(prj.target_classes || '').split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => targetClasses.length === 0 || targetClasses.includes(s.class_id));
      const dims = String(prj.dimensions || 'Gotong Royong, Kreatif').split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);

      let dimScores = {};
      if (existing && existing.dimension_scores) {
        try { dimScores = JSON.parse(existing.dimension_scores); } catch (e) {}
      }

      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveP5EvaluationForm(event, '${projectId}', '${evalId}')" class="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Peserta Didik</label>
            <select id="p5EvStudent" required ${existing ? 'disabled' : ''} class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold">
              ${students.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}" data-cls="${s.class_id}" ${existing && existing.student_id === s.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${s.full_name} (${s.class_id})</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div class="space-y-2">
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Capaian Multi-Dimensi Profil Pelajar</label>
            ${dims.map((d, idx) => `
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">${d}</span>
                <select data-dim-name="${d}" class="p5-dim-select px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs font-extrabold bg-white">
                  ${[
                    { k: 'BB', l: 'BB (Belum Berkembang)' },
                    { k: 'MB', l: 'MB (Mulai Berkembang)' },
                    { k: 'BSH', l: 'BSH (Berkembang Sesuai Harapan)' },
                    { k: 'SAB', l: 'SAB (Sangat Berkembang)' }
                  ].map(opt => `<option value="${opt.k}" ${(dimScores[d] || 'BSH') === opt.k ? 'selected' : ''}>${opt.l}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              </div>
            `).join('')}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Catatan Proses & Deskripsi Rapor P5</label>
            <textarea id="p5EvNote" rows="3" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs" placeholder="Catatan perkembangan karakter siswa dalam proyek ini...">${existing ? existing.note : ''}</textarea>
          </div>

          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs">Simpan Evaluasi Kokurikuler (Sinkron ke Rapor)</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal(existing ? 'Edit Evaluasi P5 Siswa' : 'Input Evaluasi P5 Siswa', body);
    }

    async function saveP5EvaluationForm(e, projectId, evalId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const sel = document.getElementById('p5EvStudent');
      const studentId = sel.value;
      const stObj = (STATE.db.Students || []).find(s => s.id === studentId);
      const dimMap = {};
      const scoresArr = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('.p5-dim-select').forEach(el => {
        const dName = el.getAttribute('data-dim-name');
        dimMap[dName] = el.value;
        scoresArr.push(el.value);
      });

      await apiSave('KokurikulerEvaluations', {
        id: evalId || '',
        project_id: projectId,
        student_id: studentId,
        class_id: stObj ? stObj.class_id : '',
        dimension_scores: JSON.stringify(dimMap),
        final_predicate: scoresArr[0] || 'BSH',
        note: document.getElementById('p5EvNote').value,
        reviewed_by: STATE.user.full_name
      }, 'Evaluasi P5 berhasil disimpan & disinkronkan ke Rapor!');
      closeModal();
    }

    function renderGuruTambahanTab() {
      const list = (STATE.db.AdditionalAssignments || []).filter(a =>
        a.pj_id === STATE.user.id || String(a.member_ids || '').includes(STATE.user.id)
      );
      return `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
          <h3 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base mb-4">Daftar Kepanitiaan & Penugasan Tambahan Anda</h3>
          ${list.length === 0 ? `<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Belum ada penugasan tambahan yang tercatat.</p>` : `
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              ${list.map(a => `
                <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
                  <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-xs font-bold">${a.category}</span>
                  <h4 class="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm mt-2">${a.title}</h4>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">SK: ${a.sk_number || '-'} • ${a.start_date} s.d. ${a.end_date}</p>
                </div>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          `}
        </div>
      `;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 4. DATA CP & ATP + PROTA (GENERATOR OTOMATIS & DIAGRAM BAR KEMAJUAN)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderCpAtpProta() {
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const allProta = STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || [];
      const sessions = STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || [];

      const filtered = allProta.filter(p =>
        (STATE.protaSubjectFilter === 'ALL' || p.subject_id === STATE.protaSubjectFilter) &&
        (STATE.protaGradeFilter === 'ALL' || String(p.grade_level) === String(STATE.protaGradeFilter))
      );

      // Hitung Diagram Bar Kemajuan Prota
      const totalTargetMeetings = filtered.reduce((acc, p) => acc + (Number(p.meeting_count) || 1), 0);
      const taughtMeetings = filtered.reduce((acc, p) => {
        const linkedSessions = sessions.filter(s => s.atp_id === p.id || s.atp_code === p.atp_code).length;
        return acc + Math.min(Number(p.meeting_count) || 1, linkedSessions);
      }, 0);
      const progressPct = totalTargetMeetings > 0 ? Math.round((taughtMeetings / totalTargetMeetings) * 100) : 0;

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Data CP & ATP — Program Tahunan (Prota)</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Fase D ${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().curriculum_name)} ${siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button onclick="openAutoProtaGeneratorModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="wand-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Susun Prota Otomatis
              </button>
              <button onclick="openAddProtaModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="plus" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Tambah CP/ATP
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- DIAGRAM BAR KEMAJUAN PROTA -->
          <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <span class="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-700 uppercase">Diagram Kemajuan Realisasi Prota</span>
              <span class="text-sm font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${taughtMeetings} / ${totalTargetMeetings} Pertemuan (${progressPct}%)</span>
            </div>
            <div class="w-full h-4 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
              <div class="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-teal-500 transition-all duration-500" style="width: ${progressPct}%"></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- FILTER -->
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-3 bg-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200">
            <select onchange="STATE.protaSubjectFilter=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
              <option value="ALL">Semua Mata Pelajaran</option>
              ${subjects.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}" ${STATE.protaSubjectFilter === s.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${s.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <select onchange="STATE.protaGradeFilter=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
              <option value="ALL">Semua Jenjang (7, 8, 9)</option>
              <option value="7" ${STATE.protaGradeFilter === '7' ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas 7</option>
              <option value="8" ${STATE.protaGradeFilter === '8' ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas 8</option>
              <option value="9" ${STATE.protaGradeFilter === '9' ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas 9</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <!-- TABEL PROTA -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left border-collapse">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr>
                    <th class="p-3">Fase/Kls</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Elemen</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Kode ATP</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Tujuan Pembelajaran (TP)</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Lingkup Materi</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Sem</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">JP</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">Pertemuan</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">Minggu Ke-</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-200">
                  ${filtered.map(p => `
                    <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                      <td class="p-3 font-bold">${p.phase || 'D'} / Kls ${p.grade_level}</td>
                      <td class="p-3">${p.element}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${p.atp_code}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 max-w-xs">${p.tp_text}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 font-semibold">${p.scope_material}</td>
                      <td class="p-3">${p.semester}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${p.jp_allocation}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold text-teal-700">${p.meeting_count}x</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-center">${p.week_number}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap space-x-2">
                        <button onclick="convertProtaToSession('${p.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 font-bold hover:bg-teal-100">
                          Jadikan Sesi Pertemuan
                        </button>
                        <button onclick="apiDelete('CpAtp_Prota', '${p.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  `).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddProtaModal() {
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveProtaRow(event)" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Mata Pelajaran</label>
            <select id="prSub" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              ${subjects.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}">${s.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jenjang Kelas</label>
            <select id="prGrade" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              <option value="7">Kelas 7</option><option value="8">Kelas 8</option><option value="9">Kelas 9</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Elemen</label>
            <input id="prElem" required placeholder="Contoh: Pemahaman IPA / Bilangan" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kode CP & ATP</label>
            <input id="prCode" required placeholder="Contoh: IPA.7.4" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <div class="sm:col-span-2">
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tujuan Pembelajaran (TP)</label>
            <textarea id="prTp" required rows="2" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></textarea>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Lingkup Materi</label>
            <input id="prScope" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Semester</label>
            <select id="prSem" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"><option value="Ganjil">Ganjil</option><option value="Genap">Genap</option></select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Alokasi JP</label>
            <input id="prJp" type="number" value="12" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jumlah Pertemuan</label>
            <input id="prMeet" type="number" value="3" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <div class="sm:col-span-2">
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Minggu Ke-</label>
            <input id="prWeek" placeholder="Contoh: 12-14" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="sm:col-span-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Data Prota</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Tambah Data CP & ATP (Prota)', body);
    }

    async function saveProtaRow(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      await apiSave('CpAtp_Prota', {
        subject_id: document.getElementById('prSub').value,
        grade_level: document.getElementById('prGrade').value,
        phase: 'D',
        element: document.getElementById('prElem').value,
        atp_code: document.getElementById('prCode').value,
        tp_text: document.getElementById('prTp').value,
        scope_material: document.getElementById('prScope').value,
        semester: document.getElementById('prSem').value,
        jp_allocation: Number(document.getElementById('prJp').value),
        meeting_count: Number(document.getElementById('prMeet').value),
        week_number: document.getElementById('prWeek').value
      }, 'Data CP & ATP berhasil ditambahkan!');
      closeModal();
    }

    function openAutoProtaGeneratorModal() {
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="runAutoProtaGenerator(event)" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <p class="text-slate-600">Sistem akan menyusun struktur ATP & distribusi minggu efektif Gasal/Genap secara otomatis.</p>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Mata Pelajaran</label>
              <select id="apSub" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
                ${subjects.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}" data-code="${s.code}" data-name="${s.name}">${s.name}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jenjang Kelas</label>
              <select id="apGrade" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
                <option value="7">Kelas 7</option><option value="8">Kelas 8</option><option value="9">Kelas 9</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">JP per Minggu</label>
              <input id="apJpWeek" type="number" value="4" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Minggu Efektif (Gasal / Genap)</label>
              <input id="apWeeks" type="number" value="18" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
            </div>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white font-bold">Generate Kerangka Prota</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Susun Prota Otomatis', body);
    }

    async function runAutoProtaGenerator(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const sel = document.getElementById('apSub');
      const opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
      const subId = sel.value;
      const code = opt.getAttribute('data-code') || 'MAPEL';
      const name = opt.getAttribute('data-name') || 'Mata Pelajaran';
      const grade = document.getElementById('apGrade').value;
      const jpWeek = Number(document.getElementById('apJpWeek').value) || 4;
      const effWeeks = Number(document.getElementById('apWeeks').value) || 18;
      const unitWeeks = Math.max(3, Math.floor(effWeeks / 3));

      for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
        const startW = (i - 1) * unitWeeks + 1;
        const endW = Math.min(effWeeks, i * unitWeeks);
        await apiSave('CpAtp_Prota', {
          subject_id: subId,
          grade_level: grade,
          phase: 'D',
          element: `Elemen ${i} (${name})`,
          atp_code: `${code}.${grade}.${Date.now().toString().slice(-2)}${i}`,
          tp_text: `Menganalisis dan menerapkan konsep utama unit ${i} pada mata pelajaran ${name} kelas ${grade} secara kontekstual.`,
          scope_material: `Unit Materi ${i} — ${name}`,
          semester: getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil'),
          jp_allocation: unitWeeks * jpWeek,
          meeting_count: unitWeeks,
          week_number: `${startW}-${endW}`
        }, i === 3 ? '3 Unit Prota otomatis berhasil disusun!' : '');
      }
      closeModal();
    }

    function convertProtaToSession(protaId) {
      const prota = (STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || []).find(p => p.id === protaId);
      if (!prota) return;
      const myTcs = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).filter(tc => tc.teacher_id === STATE.user.id && tc.subject_id === prota.subject_id);
      if (myTcs.length === 0) {
        showToast('Anda tidak mengampu mata pelajaran ini di Kelas Saya.', 'warning');
        return;
      }
      const tc = myTcs[0];
      STATE.selectedTeacherClassId = tc.id;
      STATE.myClassesTab = 'intra';
      navigateMenu('my_classes');
      setTimeout(() => openAddSessionModal(tc.id, tc.class_id, tc.subject_id), 150);
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 5. JURNAL MENGAJAR GURU (TARIK OTOMATIS DARI SESI, EXCEL, PRINT)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderGuruJurnal() {
      const myTcs = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).filter(tc => tc.teacher_id === STATE.user.id);
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];

      if (myTcs.length > 0 && (!STATE.jurnalClassFilter || !STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter)) {
        STATE.jurnalClassFilter = myTcs[0].class_id;
        STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter = myTcs[0].subject_id;
      }

      const journals = (STATE.db.JurnalMengajar || []).filter(j =>
        j.teacher_id === STATE.user.id &&
        (!STATE.jurnalClassFilter || j.class_id === STATE.jurnalClassFilter) &&
        (!STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter || j.subject_id === STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter)
      );

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Jurnal Mengajar Guru (KBM)</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Rekam jejak pelaksanaan pembelajaran harian terintegrasi presensi & ATP</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button onclick="pullJournalFromSessions()" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-slate-900 text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                ⚡ Tarik Otomatis dari Sesi Mengajar
              </button>
              <button onclick="openAddJournalModal()" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">
                + Tambah Manual
              </button>
              <button onclick="exportJournalExcel()" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">
                Excel .xlsx
              </button>
              <button onclick="printJournal()" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs font-bold">
                Cetak Jurnal
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 flex flex-wrap gap-3 items-center">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">Filter Kelas & Mapel:</span>
            <select onchange="const [c,s]=this.value.split('|'); STATE.jurnalClassFilter=c; STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter=s; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
              ${myTcs.map(tc => {
                const c = classes.find(x => x.id === tc.class_id) || { name: tc.class_id };
                const s = subjects.find(x => x.id === tc.subject_id) || { name: tc.subject_id };
                const val = `${tc.class_id}|${tc.subject_id}`;
                const cur = `${STATE.jurnalClassFilter}|${STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter}`;
                return `<option value="${val}" ${val === cur ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas ${c.name} — ${s.name}</option>`;
              }).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left border-collapse">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr>
                    <th class="p-3">No</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Hari/Tanggal</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">Pert. Ke</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Tujuan Pembelajaran</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Materi</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Kegiatan KBM</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">Absensi (H/S/I/A)</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">% Hadir</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Permasalahan & Solusi</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${journals.map((j, idx) => `
                    <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                      <td class="p-3 font-bold">${idx + 1}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 whitespace-nowrap font-semibold">${j.journal_date}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${j.meeting_no}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 max-w-xs">${j.tp_text}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 font-bold">${j.material}</td>
                      <td class="p-3">${j.activities}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${j.h_count}/${j.s_count}/${j.i_count}/${j.a_count}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold text-emerald-700">${j.attendance_pct}%</td>
                      <td class="p-3">${j.problems_solutions || '-'}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('JurnalMengajar', '${j.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td>
                    </tr>
                  `).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    async function pullJournalFromSessions() {
      const sessions = (STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || []).filter(s =>
        s.teacher_id === STATE.user.id &&
        s.class_id === STATE.jurnalClassFilter &&
        s.subject_id === STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter
      );
      const existingSessionIds = (STATE.db.JurnalMengajar || []).map(j => j.session_id);
      const records = STATE.db.AttendanceRecords || [];
      const atps = STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || [];

      let added = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < sessions.length; i++) {
        const s = sessions[i];
        if (existingSessionIds.includes(s.id)) continue;
        const sRecs = records.filter(r => r.session_id === s.id);
        const h = sRecs.filter(r => r.status === 'H').length;
        const sk = sRecs.filter(r => r.status === 'S').length;
        const iz = sRecs.filter(r => r.status === 'I').length;
        const al = sRecs.filter(r => r.status === 'A').length;
        const tot = Math.max(1, sRecs.length);
        const atp = atps.find(a => a.id === s.atp_id || a.atp_code === s.atp_code);

        await apiSave('JurnalMengajar', {
          teacher_id: STATE.user.id,
          class_id: s.class_id,
          subject_id: s.subject_id,
          session_id: s.id,
          journal_date: s.session_date,
          meeting_no: i + 1,
          tp_text: atp ? atp.tp_text : s.title,
          material: s.title,
          activities: s.kbm_activities || 'Diskusi & Penugasan',
          h_count: h,
          s_count: sk,
          i_count: iz,
          a_count: al,
          attendance_pct: Math.round((h / tot) * 100),
          problems_solutions: [s.kbm_problems, s.kbm_solutions].filter(Boolean).join(' | ') || 'KBM berjalan lancar.'
        }, '');
        added++;
      }
      showToast(added > 0 ? `${added} entri jurnal berhasil ditarik dari sesi mengajar!` : 'Semua sesi sudah tersinkron ke jurnal.', 'info');
    }

    function openAddJournalModal() {
      const atps = (STATE.db.CpAtp_Prota || []).filter(a => a.subject_id === STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter);
      const acts = ['Ceramah', 'Diskusi', 'Presentasi', 'Penugasan', 'Asesmen Formatif', 'Praktik/Demonstrasi'];
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveManualJournal(event)" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tanggal</label><input id="jnDate" type="date" required value="${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Pertemuan Ke-</label><input id="jnNo" type="number" value="1" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tujuan Pembelajaran (Tarik dari ATP Prota)</label>
            <select id="jnTp" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              ${atps.map(a => `<option value="${a.tp_text}">${a.atp_code} — ${shortenTpText(a.tp_text, 65)}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Materi Pokok</label><input id="jnMat" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kegiatan Belajar Mengajar</label>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              ${acts.map(a => `<label class="flex items-center gap-1.5"><input type="checkbox" name="jnAct" value="${a}"><span>${a}</span></label>`).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">
            <div><label class="block font-bold">Hadir</label><input id="jnH" type="number" value="25" class="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold">Sakit</label><input id="jnS" type="number" value="0" class="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold">Izin</label><input id="jnI" type="number" value="0" class="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold">Alpa</label><input id="jnA" type="number" value="0" class="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border"></div>
          </div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Permasalahan dalam KBM & Solusi</label><textarea id="jnProb" rows="2" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></textarea></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Jurnal</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Tambah Entri Jurnal Mengajar', body);
    }

    async function saveManualJournal(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const h = Number(document.getElementById('jnH').value) || 0;
      const s = Number(document.getElementById('jnS').value) || 0;
      const i = Number(document.getElementById('jnI').value) || 0;
      const a = Number(document.getElementById('jnA').value) || 0;
      const tot = Math.max(1, h + s + i + a);
      const checkedActs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="jnAct"]:checked')).map(c => c.value).join(', ');

      await apiSave('JurnalMengajar', {
        teacher_id: STATE.user.id,
        class_id: STATE.jurnalClassFilter,
        subject_id: STATE.jurnalSubjectFilter,
        journal_date: document.getElementById('jnDate').value,
        meeting_no: Number(document.getElementById('jnNo').value),
        tp_text: document.getElementById('jnTp').value,
        material: document.getElementById('jnMat').value,
        activities: checkedActs,
        h_count: h, s_count: s, i_count: i, a_count: a,
        attendance_pct: Math.round((h / tot) * 100),
        problems_solutions: document.getElementById('jnProb').value
      }, 'Jurnal mengajar berhasil disimpan!');
      closeModal();
    }

    function exportJournalExcel() {
      const journals = (STATE.db.JurnalMengajar || []).filter(j => j.teacher_id === STATE.user.id && j.class_id === STATE.jurnalClassFilter);
      const headers = ['No', 'Hari/Tanggal', 'Pertemuan Ke', 'Tujuan Pembelajaran', 'Materi', 'Kegiatan KBM', 'H/S/I/A', '% Hadir', 'Permasalahan & Solusi'];
      const rows = journals.map((j, i) => [i + 1, j.journal_date, j.meeting_no, j.tp_text, j.material, j.activities, `${j.h_count}/${j.s_count}/${j.i_count}/${j.a_count}`, `${j.attendance_pct}%`, j.problems_solutions]);
      exportTableToExcel([headers, ...rows], 'Jurnal_KBM', `Jurnal_Mengajar_${STATE.jurnalClassFilter}.xlsx`);
    }

    function printJournal() {
      const journals = (STATE.db.JurnalMengajar || []).filter(j => j.teacher_id === STATE.user.id && j.class_id === STATE.jurnalClassFilter);
      const headers = ['No', 'Tanggal', 'Pert.', 'Tujuan Pembelajaran', 'Materi', 'Kegiatan', 'H/S/I/A', '%', 'Permasalahan & Solusi'];
      const rows = journals.map((j, i) => [i + 1, j.journal_date, j.meeting_no, j.tp_text, j.material, j.activities, `${j.h_count}/${j.s_count}/${j.i_count}/${j.a_count}`, `${j.attendance_pct}%`, j.problems_solutions]);
      triggerPrintHtml(`
        ${renderOfficialLetterhead()}
        <h3 style="text-align:center;font-weight:bold;margin:10px 0;">JURNAL PELAKSANAAN PEMBELAJARAN (KBM)</h3>
        <table>
          <thead><tr>${headers.map(h => `<th>${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>${rows.map(r => `<tr>${r.map(c => `<td>${c}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody>
        </table>
        ${renderSignatureBlock('Guru Mata Pelajaran', STATE.user.full_name, STATE.user.nip)}
      `);
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 6. JADWAL MENGAJAR GURU
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderGuruJadwal() {
      const mySchedules = (STATE.db.JadwalPelajaran || []).filter(j => j.teacher_id === STATE.user.id);
      const myTcs = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).filter(tc => tc.teacher_id === STATE.user.id);
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const jpSet = (STATE.db.Settings || []).find(s => s.key === 'jp_duration_minutes') || { value: '40' };
      const totalJp = mySchedules.reduce((a, b) => a + (Number(b.jp_count) || 0), 0) + adminBlocksJp(STATE.user.id);

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Jadwal Mengajar & Pengaturan JP</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Total Beban Minggu Ini: <strong>${totalJp} JP</strong> (${totalJp * Number(jpSet.value)} Menit)</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs">
              <span class="font-bold text-slate-600">Durasi 1 JP (Menit):</span>
              <input type="number" value="${jpSet.value}" onchange="updateJpDuration(this.value)" class="w-16 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border font-bold text-center">
            </div>
          </div>

          ${renderTeacherAdminScheduleCard()}

          <!-- FORM TAMBAH JADWAL -->
          <form onsubmit="saveNewSchedule(event)" class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-7 gap-3 text-xs items-end">
            <div class="sm:col-span-2">
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kelas & Mata Pelajaran</label>
              <select id="jdTc" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
                ${myTcs.map(tc => {
                  const c = classes.find(x => x.id === tc.class_id) || { name: tc.class_id };
                  const s = subjects.find(x => x.id === tc.subject_id) || { name: tc.subject_id };
                  return `<option value="${tc.class_id}|${tc.subject_id}">Kelas ${c.name} — ${s.name}</option>`;
                }).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Hari</label>
              <select id="jdDay" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
                ${['Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu'].map(d => `<option value="${d}">${d}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jam Mulai</label>
              <input id="jdTime" type="time" value="07:30" oninput="updateScheduleEndUI()" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jam Selesai (otomatis)</label>
              <input id="jdEnd" type="text" value="${addMin('07:30', 2 * Number(jpSet.value))}" readonly tabindex="-1" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-extrabold">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jumlah JP</label>
              <input id="jdJp" type="number" min="1" max="8" value="2" oninput="updateScheduleEndUI()" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Ruangan</label>
              <input id="jdRoom" placeholder="Ruang 7A" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
            </div>
            <button type="submit" class="sm:col-span-7 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">+ Tambahkan ke Jadwal Mengajar</button>
          </form>

          <!-- TABEL JADWAL -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr><th class="p-3">Hari</th><th class="p-3">Jam Mulai</th><th class="p-3">Jam Selesai</th><th class="p-3">Kelas</th><th class="p-3">Mata Pelajaran</th><th class="p-3 text-center">JP</th><th class="p-3">Ruangan</th><th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th></tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${mySchedules.map(j => {
                    const c = classes.find(x => x.id === j.class_id) || { name: j.class_id };
                    const s = subjects.find(x => x.id === j.subject_id) || { name: j.subject_id };
                    return `
                      <tr>
                        <td class="p-3 font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${j.day_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">${normTime(j.start_time) || '-'} WITA</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">${addMin(j.start_time, (Number(j.jp_count) || 0) * getJpDuration()) || '-'} WITA</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">Kelas ${c.name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3">${s.name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${j.jp_count} JP</td>
                        <td class="p-3">${j.room_note || '-'}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('JadwalPelajaran', '${j.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function updateScheduleEndUI() {
      const start = document.getElementById('jdTime');
      const jp = document.getElementById('jdJp');
      const end = document.getElementById('jdEnd');
      if (!start || !jp || !end) return;
      const count = Math.max(1, Math.min(8, Number(jp.value) || 2));
      end.value = addMin(start.value, count * getJpDuration()) || '-';
    }

    async function updateJpDuration(val) {
      showLoader('Menyimpan durasi JP...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        await callServer('saveSchoolSettings', { jp_duration_minutes: String(val) }, actor);
        await loadAppData(true);
        showToast('Durasi 1 JP diperbarui!', 'success');
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal menyimpan: ' + err.message, 'error');
      } finally { hideLoader(); }
    }

    async function saveNewSchedule(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const [classId, subjectId] = document.getElementById('jdTc').value.split('|');
      await apiSave('JadwalPelajaran', {
        teacher_id: STATE.user.id,
        class_id: classId,
        subject_id: subjectId,
        day_name: document.getElementById('jdDay').value,
        start_time: normTime(document.getElementById('jdTime').value),
        jp_count: Number(document.getElementById('jdJp').value),
        room_note: document.getElementById('jdRoom').value
      }, 'Jadwal mengajar berhasil ditambahkan!');
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 7. PELANGGARAN TATA TERTIB (TERSEDIA UNTUK SEMUA GURU & KESISWAAN)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderPelanggaranModule() {
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const violations = STATE.db.PelanggaranSiswa || [];
      const users = STATE.db.UsersPublic || [];

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Catatan Pelanggaran Tata Tertib & Sanksi</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Setiap guru dapat mencatat pelanggaran siswa; data otomatis terakumulasi di Data Siswa & Waka Kesiswaan</p>
            </div>
            <button onclick="openAddPelanggaranModal()" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <i data-lucide="alert-circle" class="w-4 h-4"></i> + Catat Pelanggaran Siswa
            </button>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr>
                    <th class="p-3">Tanggal</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Nama Siswa & Kelas</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Kategori</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Uraian Pelanggaran</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">Poin</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Sanksi / Tindak Lanjut</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Pelapor</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Status</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${violations.map(v => {
                    const st = students.find(s => s.id === v.student_id) || { full_name: v.student_id, class_id: '-' };
                    const cls = classes.find(c => c.id === st.class_id) || { name: st.class_id };
                    const rep = users.find(u => u.id === v.reporter_id) || { full_name: 'Guru' };
                    const badgeColor = v.category === 'Berat' ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800' : v.category === 'Sedang' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700';
                    return `
                      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                        <td class="p-3 whitespace-nowrap">${v.incident_date}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">${st.full_name} <span class="text-slate-400 font-normal">(${cls.name})</span></td>
                        <td class="p-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${badgeColor}">${v.category}</span></td>
                        <td class="p-3">${v.description}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold text-rose-700">+${v.points}</td>
                        <td class="p-3">${v.sanction}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-slate-500">${rep.full_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-semibold text-emerald-700">${v.status}</td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddPelanggaranModal() {
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="savePelanggaranForm(event)" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Peserta Didik</label>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-2">
              <select id="plgClass" onchange="plgFilterStudents()" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
                <option value="ALL">Semua Kelas</option>
                ${sortedClasses().map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">Kelas ${siEsc(c.name)}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
              <input id="plgSearch" type="search" oninput="plgFilterStudents()" placeholder="Cari nama / NIS..." class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
            </div>
            <select id="plgStudent" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
              ${students.filter(s => String(s.status || 'Aktif') === 'Aktif').map(s => `<option value="${s.id}">${siEsc(s.full_name)} (${siEsc(className(s.class_id))})</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <p id="plgCount" class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1"></p>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tanggal Kejadian</label>
              <input id="plgDate" type="date" required value="${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kategori</label>
              <select id="plgCat" onchange="document.getElementById('plgPts').value = this.value==='Berat'?40:(this.value==='Sedang'?15:5)" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
                <option value="Ringan">Ringan</option>
                <option value="Sedang">Sedang</option>
                <option value="Berat">Berat</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Poin</label>
              <input id="plgPts" type="number" value="5" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Uraian Pelanggaran</label>
            <textarea id="plgDesc" required rows="2" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></textarea>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Sanksi / Tindak Lanjut Pembinaan</label>
            <input id="plgSanc" required placeholder="Teguran lisan / pembinaan wali kelas..." class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Catatan Pelanggaran</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Input Catatan Pelanggaran Tata Tertib', body);
      setTimeout(() => { try { plgFilterStudents(); } catch (e) {} }, 0);
    }

    async function savePelanggaranForm(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      await apiSave('PelanggaranSiswa', {
        student_id: document.getElementById('plgStudent').value,
        reporter_id: STATE.user.id,
        incident_date: document.getElementById('plgDate').value,
        category: document.getElementById('plgCat').value,
        description: document.getElementById('plgDesc').value,
        points: Number(document.getElementById('plgPts').value),
        sanction: document.getElementById('plgSanc').value,
        status: 'Dalam Pembinaan'
      }, 'Pelanggaran siswa berhasil dicatat!');
      closeModal();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 8. MODUL GURU WALI (PEMETAAN & PENILAIAN 6 ASPEK KARAKTER SKALA 1-5)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderGuruWaliModule() {
      const isAdminOrKs = ['admin', 'kepala_sekolah'].includes(STATE.activeRole);
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => isAdminOrKs ? true : s.guru_wali_id === STATE.user.id);
      const records = STATE.db.GuruWaliRecords || [];
      const users = STATE.db.UsersPublic || [];

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div>
            <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Bimbingan Karakter & Guru Wali</h2>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Penilaian 6 aspek karakter (Disiplin, Kemandirian, Kejujuran, Relasi Sosial, Spiritual, Keaktifan skala 1–5) & rekomendasi pembinaan</p>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr>
                    <th class="p-3">Nama Siswa Binaan</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Kelas</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Guru Wali</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">Skor 6 Aspek (1-5)</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Catatan Perkembangan</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${students.map(st => {
                    const rec = records.find(r => r.student_id === st.id);
                    const gw = users.find(u => u.id === st.guru_wali_id) || { full_name: '-' };
                    const avgScore = rec
                      ? ((Number(rec.disiplin) + Number(rec.kemandirian) + Number(rec.kejujuran) + Number(rec.relasi_sosial) + Number(rec.spiritual) + Number(rec.keaktifan)) / 6).toFixed(1)
                      : 'Belum dinilai';
                    return `
                      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">${st.full_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3">${st.class_id}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-slate-500">${gw.full_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${avgScore}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 max-w-xs">${rec ? rec.note : '-'}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-right">
                          <button onclick="openGuruWaliAssessModal('${st.id}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">
                            Nilai Karakter
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openGuruWaliAssessModal(studentId) {
      const st = (STATE.db.Students || []).find(s => s.id === studentId);
      const rec = (STATE.db.GuruWaliRecords || []).find(r => r.student_id === studentId) || {
        disiplin: 4, kemandirian: 4, kejujuran: 5, relasi_sosial: 4, spiritual: 5, keaktifan: 4, note: '', recommendation: ''
      };
      const aspects = [
        { k: 'disiplin', l: '1. Kedisiplinan' },
        { k: 'kemandirian', l: '2. Kemandirian Belajar' },
        { k: 'kejujuran', l: '3. Kejujuran & Integritas' },
        { k: 'relasi_sosial', l: '4. Relasi Sosial & Empati' },
        { k: 'spiritual', l: '5. Kehidupan Spiritual' },
        { k: 'keaktifan', l: '6. Keaktifan & Tanggung Jawab' }
      ];

      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveGuruWaliRecord(event, '${rec.id || ''}', '${studentId}')" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <p class="font-bold text-slate-800">Peserta Didik: ${st.full_name} (${st.class_id})</p>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
            ${aspects.map(a => `
              <div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border flex items-center justify-between">
                <span class="font-bold">${a.l}</span>
                <select id="gw_${a.k}" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border font-extrabold bg-white">
                  ${[5, 4, 3, 2, 1].map(n => `<option value="${n}" ${Number(rec[a.k]) === n ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} / 5</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              </div>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Catatan Perkembangan Karakter (Tampil di Rapor & Portal Ortu)</label>
            <textarea id="gwNote" rows="2" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">${rec.note || ''}</textarea>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Rekomendasi Pembinaan</label>
            <input id="gwRec" value="${rec.recommendation || ''}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Penilaian Guru Wali</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Penilaian 6 Aspek Karakter Guru Wali', body);
    }

    async function saveGuruWaliRecord(e, recId, studentId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      await apiSave('GuruWaliRecords', {
        id: recId,
        guru_wali_id: STATE.user.id,
        student_id: studentId,
        semester: getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil'),
        disiplin: Number(document.getElementById('gw_disiplin').value),
        kemandirian: Number(document.getElementById('gw_kemandirian').value),
        kejujuran: Number(document.getElementById('gw_kejujuran').value),
        relasi_sosial: Number(document.getElementById('gw_relasi_sosial').value),
        spiritual: Number(document.getElementById('gw_spiritual').value),
        keaktifan: Number(document.getElementById('gw_keaktifan').value),
        note: document.getElementById('gwNote').value,
        recommendation: document.getElementById('gwRec').value
      }, 'Penilaian karakter Guru Wali berhasil disimpan!');
      closeModal();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 9. MODUL ADMIN: DATA SISWA (OTOMATIS REKAP PELANGGARAN + AUTO PRAMUKA)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderStudentsModule() {
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const violations = STATE.db.PelanggaranSiswa || [];
      const users = STATE.db.UsersPublic || [];

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Data Peserta Didik ${siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Siswa baru otomatis terdaftar pada Ekstrakurikuler wajib Pramuka • Kolom Pelanggaran terhitung otomatis</p>
            </div>
            <button onclick="openStudentFormModal('')" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">
              + Tambah Siswa Baru
            </button>
          </div>

          ${(() => {
            const orphans = students.filter(s => !classes.some(c => c.id === s.class_id));
            return orphans.length ? `<div class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 text-xs text-amber-900">
              <p class="font-extrabold">${orphans.length} siswa belum masuk rombel</p>
              <p class="mt-1">Kolom <strong>class_id</strong> pada sheet Students tidak cocok dengan ID rombel di sheet Classes (contoh ID yang benar: ${classes.slice(0, 3).map(c => '<strong>' + siEsc(c.id) + '</strong>').join(', ')}). Perbaiki di Google Sheet lalu muat ulang. Contoh data bermasalah: ${orphans.slice(0, 5).map(s => siEsc(s.full_name) + ' (“' + siEsc(s.class_id || 'kosong') + '”)').join(', ')}.</p>
            </div>` : '';
          })()}

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr>
                    <th class="p-3">NIS / NISN</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Nama Lengkap</th>
                    <th class="p-3">L/P</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Kelas</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Orang Tua / HP</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Literasi / Numerasi</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Pelanggaran (R/S/B & Poin)</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${students.map(st => {
                    const cls = classes.find(c => c.id === st.class_id) || { name: st.class_id };
                    const stV = violations.filter(v => v.student_id === st.id);
                    const rCnt = stV.filter(v => v.category === 'Ringan').length;
                    const sCnt = stV.filter(v => v.category === 'Sedang').length;
                    const bCnt = stV.filter(v => v.category === 'Berat').length;
                    const totPts = stV.reduce((a, b) => a + (Number(b.points) || 0), 0);
                    return `
                      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                        <td class="p-3 font-mono">${st.nis}<br><span class="text-slate-400">${st.nisn}</span></td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold text-slate-800">${st.full_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">${st.gender}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold text-indigo-700">${cls.name}</td>
