                        <td class="p-3">${st.parent_name}<br><span class="text-slate-400">${st.parent_phone}</span></td>
                        <td class="p-3">Lit: <strong>${st.literacy_level}</strong><br>Num: <strong>${st.numeracy_level}</strong></td>
                        <td class="p-3">
                          <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${totPts > 0 ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 font-bold' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'}">
                            R:${rCnt} | S:${sCnt} | B:${bCnt} (${totPts} Poin)
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap space-x-2">
                          <button onclick="openStudentFormModal('${st.id}')" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold">Edit</button>
                          <button onclick="apiDelete('Students', '${st.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openStudentFormModal(studentId = '') {
      const st = studentId ? (STATE.db.Students || []).find(s => s.id === studentId) : {};
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');

      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveStudentForm(event, '${studentId}')" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">NIS</label><input id="stNis" required value="${st.nis || ''}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">NISN</label><input id="stNisn" required value="${st.nisn || ''}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div class="sm:col-span-2"><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Lengkap Siswa</label><input id="stName" required value="${st.full_name || ''}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jenis Kelamin</label>
            <select id="stGender" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              <option value="L" ${st.gender === 'L' ? 'selected' : ''}>Laki-laki (L)</option>
              <option value="P" ${st.gender === 'P' ? 'selected' : ''}>Perempuan (P)</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kelas</label>
            <select id="stClass" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              ${classes.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}" ${st.class_id === c.id ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas ${c.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Orang Tua / Wali</label><input id="stParent" value="${st.parent_name || ''}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">No. HP Orang Tua</label><input id="stPhone" value="${st.parent_phone || ''}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Guru Wali</label>
            <select id="stGw" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              ${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}" ${st.guru_wali_id === g.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Cita-cita</label><input id="stDream" value="${st.dream_job || ''}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tingkat Literasi</label>
            <select id="stLit" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              ${['Perlu Intervensi', 'Berkembang', 'Cakap', 'Sangat Cakap'].map(l => `<option value="${l}" ${(st.literacy_level || 'Cakap') === l ? 'selected' : ''}>${l}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tingkat Numerasi</label>
            <select id="stNum" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
              ${['Perlu Intervensi', 'Berkembang', 'Cakap', 'Sangat Cakap'].map(l => `<option value="${l}" ${(st.numeracy_level || 'Cakap') === l ? 'selected' : ''}>${l}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="sm:col-span-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Data Siswa</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal(studentId ? 'Edit Data Siswa' : 'Tambah Siswa Baru (+ Otomatis Ekskul Pramuka)', body);
    }

    async function saveStudentForm(e, studentId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      await apiSave('Students', {
        id: studentId,
        nis: document.getElementById('stNis').value,
        nisn: document.getElementById('stNisn').value,
        full_name: document.getElementById('stName').value,
        gender: document.getElementById('stGender').value,
        class_id: document.getElementById('stClass').value,
        parent_name: document.getElementById('stParent').value,
        parent_phone: document.getElementById('stPhone').value,
        guru_wali_id: document.getElementById('stGw').value,
        literacy_level: document.getElementById('stLit').value,
        numeracy_level: document.getElementById('stNum').value,
        dream_job: document.getElementById('stDream').value,
        status: 'Aktif'
      }, studentId ? 'Data siswa diperbarui!' : 'Siswa baru berhasil ditambahkan & otomatis terdaftar di Pramuka!');
      closeModal();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 10. ADMIN: JABATAN & TUGAS TAMBAHAN GURU (FOKUS LANGSUNG TABEL & FILTER)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    let jabatanFilterGuru = 'ALL';

    function renderTeacherJabatansModule() {
      const jabs = (STATE.db.TeacherJabatans || []).filter(j => jabatanFilterGuru === 'ALL' || j.teacher_id === jabatanFilterGuru);
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');

      return `
        <div class="space-y-4">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Jabatan & Tugas Tambahan Guru (Multi-Jabatan)</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Satu guru dapat memiliki lebih dari satu jabatan aktif (Waka, Wali Kelas, Pembina, Koordinator)</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <select onchange="jabatanFilterGuru=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold bg-white">
                <option value="ALL">Filter: Semua Guru</option>
                ${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}" ${jabatanFilterGuru === g.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
              <button onclick="openAddJabatanModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah SK Jabatan</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr>
                    <th class="p-3">Nama Guru</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Jabatan / Tugas Tambahan</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Role Akses</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Nomor SK</th>
                    <th class="p-3">TMT</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-center">Ekiv. JP</th>
                    <th class="p-3">Status</th>
                    <th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${jabs.map(j => {
                    const g = gurus.find(u => u.id === j.teacher_id) || { full_name: j.teacher_id };
                    return `
                      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">${g.full_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-semibold text-indigo-700">${j.jabatan_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 font-mono">${j.role_key}</span></td>
                        <td class="p-3">${j.sk_number || '-'}</td>
                        <td class="p-3">${j.tmt_date || '-'}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${j.jp_eq || 0} JP</td>
                        <td class="p-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-bold">${j.status}</span></td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('TeacherJabatans', '${j.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddJabatanModal() {
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveJabatanForm(event)" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Pilih Guru</label>
            <select id="jbTeacher" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
              ${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}">${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Role Akses Sistem</label>
              <select id="jbRoleKey" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
                <option value="wali_kelas">Wali Kelas</option>
                <option value="waka_kurikulum">Waka Kurikulum</option>
                <option value="waka_kesiswaan">Waka Kesiswaan</option>
                <option value="waka_humas">Waka Humas</option>
                <option value="waka_sarpras">Waka Sarpras</option>
                <option value="admin_kokurikuler">Koordinator P5</option>
                <option value="guru">Tugas Tambahan Internal (Pembina/Ka.Lab/TPPK)</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Target Kelas (Jika Wali Kelas)</label>
              <select id="jbTarget" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
                <option value="">-- Tidak Ada --</option>
                ${classes.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">Kelas ${c.name}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Jabatan Lengkap</label><input id="jbName" required placeholder="Contoh: Wali Kelas 8A / Kepala Lab IPA" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">No. SK</label><input id="jbSk" placeholder="421.3/..." class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">TMT</label><input id="jbTmt" type="date" value="2026-07-13" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Ekivalensi JP</label><input id="jbJp" type="number" value="2" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan SK Jabatan</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Tambah Jabatan / Tugas Tambahan Guru', body);
    }

    async function saveJabatanForm(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      await apiSave('TeacherJabatans', {
        teacher_id: document.getElementById('jbTeacher').value,
        jabatan_name: document.getElementById('jbName').value,
        role_key: document.getElementById('jbRoleKey').value,
        target_id: document.getElementById('jbTarget').value,
        sk_number: document.getElementById('jbSk').value,
        tmt_date: document.getElementById('jbTmt').value,
        jp_eq: Number(document.getElementById('jbJp').value),
        status: 'Aktif'
      }, 'Jabatan tambahan guru berhasil disimpan!');
      closeModal();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 11. ADMIN: PENUGASAN MENGAJAR (REKAPAN + GRID KARTU KELAS -> TABEL TANPA KATEGORI)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderTeachingAssignmentsModule() {
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const tcs = STATE.db.TeacherClasses || [];
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');

      // Jika kelas sudah diklik, masuk ke tampilan tabel penugasan untuk kelas tersebut (kolom Mata Pelajaran, tanpa kolom Kategori)
      if (STATE.selectedAssignClassId) {
        const cls = classes.find(c => c.id === STATE.selectedAssignClassId) || { id: STATE.selectedAssignClassId, name: STATE.selectedAssignClassId };
        const classTcs = tcs.filter(t => t.class_id === cls.id);

        return `
          <div class="space-y-5">
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                <button onclick="STATE.selectedAssignClassId=null; renderApp();" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-800 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  ← Kembali ke Daftar Kelas
                </button>
                <div>
                  <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Penugasan Mengajar — Kelas ${cls.name}</h2>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Atur guru pengampu mata pelajaran untuk Kelas ${cls.name}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onclick="openAddTeachingAssignModal('${cls.id}')" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Guru Mapel</button>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
              <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                  <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                    <tr>
                      <th class="p-3 w-12">No</th>
                      <th class="p-3">Mata Pelajaran</th>
                      <th class="p-3">Guru Pengampu</th>
                      <th class="p-3">Tahun Ajaran / Semester</th>
                      <th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody class="divide-y">
                    ${classTcs.map((tc, idx) => {
                      const sub = subjects.find(s => s.id === tc.subject_id) || { name: tc.subject_id };
                      const gr = gurus.find(g => g.id === tc.teacher_id) || { full_name: tc.teacher_id };
                      return `
                        <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                          <td class="p-3 font-bold">${idx + 1}</td>
                          <td class="p-3 font-extrabold text-slate-800">${sub.name}</td>
                          <td class="p-3 font-semibold text-indigo-700">${gr.full_name}</td>
                          <td class="p-3">${tc.academic_year} • ${tc.semester}</td>
                          <td class="p-3 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('TeacherClasses', '${tc.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td>
                        </tr>
                      `;
                    }).join('')}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      // Tampilan Awal: Rekapan Penugasan Mengajar (Semua Kelas) & Grid Kartu Kelas
      return `
        <div class="space-y-6">
          <div>
            <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Penugasan Mengajar (Semua Kelas)</h2>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Pilih kartu rombongan belajar di bawah ini untuk mengatur guru pengampu mata pelajaran</p>
          </div>

          <!-- REKAPAN RINGKAS -->
          <div class="bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
              <p class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-800 uppercase">Rekapan Penugasan Mengajar Aktif</p>
              <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-indigo-950 mt-0.5">${tcs.length} Penugasan Mata Pelajaran tersebar di ${classes.length} Rombongan Belajar</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- GRID KARTU NAMA-NAMA KELAS -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            ${classes.map(cls => {
              const count = tcs.filter(t => t.class_id === cls.id).length;
              const wali = gurus.find(g => g.id === cls.wali_kelas_id) || { full_name: '-' };
              return `
                <div onclick="STATE.selectedAssignClassId='${cls.id}'; renderApp();" class="cursor-pointer bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border-2 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-600 hover:shadow-md transition group">
                  <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 group-hover:bg-indigo-600 group-hover:text-white font-extrabold text-lg flex items-center justify-center transition">
                      ${cls.name}
                    </span>
                    <span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 text-xs font-bold">${count} Mapel</span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base mt-4">Rombel Kelas ${cls.name}</h3>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Wali Kelas: ${wali.full_name}</p>
                  <p class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 mt-3 group-hover:underline">Kelola Penugasan Mapel →</p>
                </div>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddTeachingAssignModal(classId) {
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      const body = `
        <form onsubmit="saveTeachingAssign(event, '${classId}')" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Mata Pelajaran</label>
            <select id="taSub" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
              ${subjects.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}">${s.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Guru Pengampu</label>
            <select id="taGuru" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
              ${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}">${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Penugasan</button>
        </form>
      `;
      openModal('Tambah Penugasan Mengajar', body);
    }

    async function saveTeachingAssign(e, classId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      await apiSave('TeacherClasses', {
        teacher_id: document.getElementById('taGuru').value,
        class_id: classId,
        subject_id: document.getElementById('taSub').value,
        academic_year: getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027'),
        semester: getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil')
      }, 'Penugasan mengajar berhasil ditambahkan!');
      closeModal();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 12. ADMIN: KELAS & MAPEL, EKSKUL, PENUGASAN TAMBAHAN, SYSTEM ADMIN
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderClassesSubjectsModule() {
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');

      return `
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <!-- KELAS -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base">Daftar Rombongan Belajar & KKM</h3>
              <button onclick="openAddClassModal()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Kelas</button>
            </div>
            <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
              <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-2.5">Kelas</th><th class="p-2.5">KKM</th><th class="p-2.5">Wali Kelas</th><th class="p-2.5 text-right">Aksi</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody class="divide-y">
                ${classes.map(c => {
                  const w = gurus.find(g => g.id === c.wali_kelas_id) || { full_name: '-' };
                  return `<tr><td class="p-2.5 font-bold">${c.name}</td><td class="p-2.5 font-extrabold text-emerald-700">${c.kkm}</td><td class="p-2.5">${w.full_name}</td><td class="p-2.5 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('Classes', '${c.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td></tr>`;
                }).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <!-- MAPEL -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base">Daftar Mata Pelajaran</h3>
              <button onclick="openAddSubjectModal()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Mapel</button>
            </div>
            <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
              <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-2.5">Kode</th><th class="p-2.5">Mata Pelajaran</th><th class="p-2.5 text-center">JP/Mgg</th><th class="p-2.5 text-right">Aksi</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody class="divide-y">
                ${subjects.map(s => `<tr><td class="p-2.5 font-mono font-bold">${s.code}</td><td class="p-2.5 font-bold">${s.name}</td><td class="p-2.5 text-center">${s.jp_per_week}</td><td class="p-2.5 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('Subjects', '${s.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td></tr>`).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddClassModal() {
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      openModal('Tambah Rombongan Belajar', `
        <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('Classes', { name: document.getElementById('cName').value, grade_level: document.getElementById('cGrade').value, wali_kelas_id: document.getElementById('cWali').value, kkm: Number(document.getElementById('cKkm').value), academic_year: '${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')}' }, 'Kelas ditambahkan!').then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Kelas (Contoh: 8B)</label><input id="cName" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tingkat</label><select id="cGrade" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"><option value="7">7</option><option value="8">8</option><option value="9">9</option></select></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">KKM Kelas</label><input id="cKkm" type="number" value="75" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Wali Kelas</label><select id="cWali" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}">${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Kelas</button>
        </form>
      `);
    }

    function openAddSubjectModal() {
      openModal('Tambah Mata Pelajaran', `
        <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('Subjects', { code: document.getElementById('sCode').value, name: document.getElementById('sName').value, phase: 'D', jp_per_week: Number(document.getElementById('sJp').value) }, 'Mapel ditambahkan!').then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kode Mapel</label><input id="sCode" required placeholder="INF" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Mata Pelajaran</label><input id="sName" required placeholder="Informatika" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">JP per Minggu</label><input id="sJp" type="number" value="3" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Mapel</button>
        </form>
      `);
    }

    function renderEkskulModule() {
      const ekskuls = STATE.db.Ekstrakurikuler || [];
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      const assessments = STATE.db.EkskulAssessments || [];

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Manajemen Ekstrakurikuler</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Pramuka wajib diikuti seluruh peserta didik; ekskul pilihan dapat dikelola pembina & anggotanya</p>
            </div>
            <button onclick="openAddEkskulModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Ekstrakurikuler</button>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            ${ekskuls.map(e => {
              const coach = gurus.find(g => g.id === e.coach_id) || { full_name: '-' };
              const cnt = assessments.filter(a => a.ekskul_id === e.id).length;
              return `
                <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-bold ${String(e.is_mandatory) === 'true' ? 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800' : 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800'}">
                        ${String(e.is_mandatory) === 'true' ? 'Ekstrakurikuler Wajib' : 'Ekstrakurikuler Pilihan'}
                      </span>
                      <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">${cnt} Anggota</span>
                    </div>
                    <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base mt-2">${e.name}</h3>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Pembina: <strong>${coach.full_name}</strong></p>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Jadwal: ${e.schedule}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div class="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t">
                    <button onclick="openAddEkskulMemberModal('${e.id}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 text-xs font-bold">+ Anggota / Nilai</button>
                    <button onclick="apiDelete('Ekstrakurikuler', '${e.id}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 text-xs font-bold">Hapus</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddEkskulModal() {
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      openModal('Tambah Ekstrakurikuler Baru', `
        <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('Ekstrakurikuler', { name: document.getElementById('ekN').value, coach_id: document.getElementById('ekC').value, schedule: document.getElementById('ekS').value, is_mandatory: 'false', description: document.getElementById('ekD').value }, 'Ekskul ditambahkan!').then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Ekstrakurikuler</label><input id="ekN" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Guru Pembina</label><select id="ekC" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}">${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Jadwal Latihan</label><input id="ekS" placeholder="Sabtu, 15:00 WITA" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Deskripsi</label><textarea id="ekD" rows="2" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></textarea></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Ekstrakurikuler</button>
        </form>
      `);
    }

    function renderAdditionalAssignmentsModule() {

                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Kategori</th><th class="p-3">Nama Kegiatan / Tugas</th><th class="p-3">Penanggung Jawab</th><th class="p-3">No. SK</th><th class="p-3">Lokasi & Tanggal</th><th class="p-3">Status</th><th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th></tr></thead>

                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

    }

          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Penugasan</button>

    function renderSystemAdminModule() {
      const users = STATE.db.UsersPublic || [];
      const logs = (STATE.db.ActivityLogs || []).slice(-15).reverse();
      return `
        <div class="space-y-6">

          </div>

            <div class="space-y-1.5 text-xs max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
              ${logs.map(l => `<div class="p-2 rounded bg-slate-50 flex justify-between"><span><strong>[${l.action}]</strong> ${l.user_name}: ${l.detail}</span><span class="text-slate-400">${l.timestamp}</span></div>`).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>

    function openAddUserModal() {
      openModal('Tambah Pengguna Sistem', `
        <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('Users', { full_name: document.getElementById('uFull').value, username: document.getElementById('uName').value, role: document.getElementById('uRole').value, nip: document.getElementById('uNip').value, avatar_initials: document.getElementById('uFull').value.substring(0,2).toUpperCase(), status: 'Aktif' }, 'Pengguna baru ditambahkan (Password default: 123456)').then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Lengkap & Gelar</label><input id="uFull" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Username</label><input id="uName" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Role Dasar</label><select id="uRole" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">${Object.keys(ROLE_META).map(r => `<option value="${r}">${ROLE_META[r].label}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">NIP / Student ID (untuk Wali Murid)</label><input id="uNip" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Pengguna</button>
        </form>
      `);
    }

    async function downloadJsonBackup() {
      showLoader('Membuat file backup JSON...');
      try {
        const json = await callServer('exportDatabaseJson');
        const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `Backup_SMPK_Waibakul_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
        a.click();
        showToast('Backup JSON berhasil diunduh!', 'success');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    function openRestoreJsonModal() {
      openModal('Restore Database dari File JSON', `
        <div class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <p class="text-rose-700 font-semibold">Perhatian: Proses restore akan menimpa data pada Google Sheets sesuai isi file JSON.</p>
          <textarea id="restoreJsonText" rows="6" placeholder="Tempelkan isi file JSON backup di sini..." class="w-full p-3 rounded-xl border font-mono"></textarea>
          <button onclick="runRestoreJson()" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 text-slate-900 font-bold">Jalankan Restore Database</button>
        </div>
      `);
    }

    async function runRestoreJson() {
      const txt = document.getElementById('restoreJsonText').value;
      if (!txt) return;
      showLoader('Merestore database...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        await callServer('importDatabaseJson', txt, actor);
        await loadAppData(true);
        showToast('Database berhasil direstore!', 'success');
        closeModal();
      } catch (e) {
        showToast('JSON tidak valid: ' + e.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 13. MODUL ADMIN KOKURIKULER (P5) — 4 MENU OPERASIONAL PENUH
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderKokurikulerModule(subMenu) {
      const projects = STATE.db.KokurikulerProjects || [];
      const evals = STATE.db.KokurikulerEvaluations || [];
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');

      // Nav tabs internal juga disediakan agar Admin & Kepsek mudah berpindah antar 4 menu P5
      const navPills = `
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 bg-white p-2 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 mb-5">
          ${[
            { id: 'p5_dashboard', label: 'Dashboard P5' },
            { id: 'p5_proyek', label: '1. Manajemen Proyek' },
            { id: 'p5_jadwal', label: '2. Jadwal & Blok Proyek' },
            { id: 'p5_fasilitator', label: '3. Penugasan Fasilitator' },
            { id: 'p5_evaluasi', label: '4. Evaluasi Karakter P5 (Review Rapor)' }
          ].map(t => `
            <button onclick="navigateMenu('${t.id}')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold ${subMenu === t.id ? 'bg-cyan-700 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}">
              ${t.label}
            </button>
          `).join('')}
        </div>
      `;

      if (subMenu === 'p5_dashboard') {
        return `
          <div>
            ${navPills}
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-6">
              <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border"><p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">Total Proyek P5</p><p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-cyan-800 mt-1">${projects.length}</p></div>
              <div onclick="navigateMenu('p5_jadwal')" class="cursor-pointer bg-cyan-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-cyan-200 hover:bg-cyan-100"><p class="text-xs font-bold text-cyan-800">Pintasan</p><p class="font-extrabold text-sm mt-1">Atur Jadwal & Blok →</p></div>
              <div onclick="navigateMenu('p5_fasilitator')" class="cursor-pointer bg-indigo-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-100"><p class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-800">Pintasan</p><p class="font-extrabold text-sm mt-1">Penugasan Fasilitator →</p></div>
              <div onclick="navigateMenu('p5_evaluasi')" class="cursor-pointer bg-emerald-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-100"><p class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-800">Review Rapor</p><p class="font-extrabold text-sm mt-1">${evals.length} Evaluasi Siswa →</p></div>
            </div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              ${projects.map(p => `
                <div onclick="navigateMenu('p5_evaluasi')" class="cursor-pointer bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border hover:border-cyan-500 transition">
                  <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 text-xs font-bold">${p.theme}</span>
                  <h3 class="font-extrabold text-base text-slate-900 mt-2">${p.title}</h3>
                  <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">${p.description}</p>
                  <p class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700 mt-3">Jadwal: ${p.schedule_block}</p>
                </div>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      if (subMenu === 'p5_proyek') {
        return `
          <div>
            ${navPills}
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold">Manajemen Proyek Kokurikuler (P5)</h2>
              <button onclick="openAddP5ProjectModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-cyan-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Proyek P5</button>
            </div>
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Judul Proyek</th><th class="p-3">Tema</th><th class="p-3">Dimensi</th><th class="p-3">Kelas Sasaran</th><th class="p-3">Status</th><th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${projects.map(p => `
                    <tr>
                      <td class="p-3 font-bold">${p.title}</td>
                      <td class="p-3">${p.theme}</td>
                      <td class="p-3">${p.dimensions}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 font-bold">${p.target_classes}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-emerald-700 font-bold">${p.status}</td>
                      <td class="p-3 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('KokurikulerProjects', '${p.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td>
                    </tr>
                  `).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      if (subMenu === 'p5_jadwal') {
        return `
          <div>
            ${navPills}
            <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold mb-4">Jadwal & Blok Pelaksanaan Proyek P5</h2>
            <div class="space-y-4">
              ${projects.map(p => `
                <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('KokurikulerProjects', { ...${JSON.stringify(p).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}, schedule_block: document.getElementById('blk_${p.id}').value, target_classes: document.getElementById('cls_${p.id}').value, status: document.getElementById('sts_${p.id}').value }, 'Jadwal blok proyek diperbarui!');" class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 items-end text-xs">
                  <div class="sm:col-span-2">
                    <p class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-sm mb-1">${p.title}</p>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-500 mb-1">Jadwal Blok Pelaksanaan</label>
                    <input id="blk_${p.id}" value="${p.schedule_block}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-500 mb-1">Kelas Sasaran (ID dipisah koma)</label>
                    <input id="cls_${p.id}" value="${p.target_classes}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
                  </div>
                  <div class="flex gap-2">
                    <select id="sts_${p.id}" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
                      ${['Perencanaan', 'Berjalan', 'Selesai'].map(s => `<option value="${s}" ${p.status === s ? 'selected' : ''}>${s}</option>`).join('')}
                    </select>
                    <button type="submit" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold">Simpan</button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      if (subMenu === 'p5_fasilitator') {
        return `
          <div>
            ${navPills}
            <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold mb-4">Penugasan Koordinator & Fasilitator Proyek P5</h2>
            <div class="space-y-4">
              ${projects.map(p => {
                const curFacs = String(p.facilitator_ids || '').split(',').map(x => x.trim());
                return `
                  <form onsubmit="saveP5Facilitators(event, '${p.id}')" class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border space-y-3 text-xs">
                    <h3 class="font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-900">${p.title}</h3>
                    <div>
                      <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Koordinator Utama</label>
                      <select id="coord_${p.id}" class="w-full sm:w-80 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
                        ${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}" ${p.coordinator_id === g.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label class="block font-bold mb-1">Pilih Guru Fasilitator Pendamping (Multi-select):</label>
                      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                        ${gurus.map(g => `
                          <label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50">
                            <input type="checkbox" name="fac_${p.id}" value="${g.id}" ${curFacs.includes(g.id) ? 'checked' : ''}>
                            <span>${g.full_name}</span>
                          </label>
                        `).join('')}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <button type="submit" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Penugasan Tim P5</button>
                  </form>
                `;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      // p5_evaluasi: Review Rapor & CRUD Lengkap
      const filteredEvals = evals.filter(ev => {
        const st = students.find(s => s.id === ev.student_id) || { full_name: '' };
        const matchPrj = STATE.p5ProjectFilter === 'ALL' || ev.project_id === STATE.p5ProjectFilter;
        const matchCls = STATE.p5ClassFilter === 'ALL' || ev.class_id === STATE.p5ClassFilter;
        const matchSearch = !STATE.p5Search || st.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(STATE.p5Search.toLowerCase());
        return matchPrj && matchCls && matchSearch;
      });

      return `
        <div>
          ${navPills}
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-4">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold">Evaluasi Karakter P5 (Review Rapor Siswa)</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Tinjau, tambah, edit, atau hapus capaian dimensi (BB/MB/BSH/SAB) dan catatan sebelum dicetak pada Rapor</p>
            </div>
            ${projects[0] ? `<button onclick="openP5EvaluationCrudModal('${STATE.p5ProjectFilter !== 'ALL' ? STATE.p5ProjectFilter : projects[0].id}', '')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-cyan-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Evaluasi P5</button>` : ''}
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl border flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4 text-xs">
            <select onchange="STATE.p5ProjectFilter=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
              <option value="ALL">Semua Proyek P5</option>
              ${projects.map(p => `<option value="${p.id}" ${STATE.p5ProjectFilter === p.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${p.title}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <select onchange="STATE.p5ClassFilter=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold">
              <option value="ALL">Semua Kelas</option>
              ${classes.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}" ${STATE.p5ClassFilter === c.id ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas ${c.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Cari nama siswa..." value="${STATE.p5Search}" oninput="STATE.p5Search=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Siswa</th><th class="p-3">Kelas</th><th class="p-3">Proyek P5</th><th class="p-3">Capaian Dimensi</th><th class="p-3">Catatan Rapor</th><th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${filteredEvals.map(ev => {
                    const st = students.find(s => s.id === ev.student_id) || { full_name: ev.student_id };
                    const prj = projects.find(p => p.id === ev.project_id) || { title: ev.project_id };
                    let dimObj = {};
                    try { dimObj = JSON.parse(ev.dimension_scores || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
                    return `
                      <tr>
                        <td class="p-3 font-bold">${st.full_name}</td>
                        <td class="p-3">${ev.class_id}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 font-semibold">${prj.title}</td>
                        <td class="p-3">${Object.entries(dimObj).map(([k, v]) => `<span class="inline-block mr-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-cyan-50 text-cyan-800 font-bold">${k}: ${v}</span>`).join('')}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 max-w-xs">${ev.note}</td>
                        <td class="p-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap space-x-2">
                          <button onclick="openP5EvaluationCrudModal('${ev.project_id}', '${ev.id}')" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold">Edit</button>
                          <button onclick="apiDelete('KokurikulerEvaluations', '${ev.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddP5ProjectModal() {
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      openModal('Tambah Proyek Kokurikuler (P5)', `
        <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('KokurikulerProjects', { title: document.getElementById('p5T').value, theme: document.getElementById('p5Th').value, description: document.getElementById('p5D').value, dimensions: document.getElementById('p5Dim').value, target_classes: document.getElementById('p5Cls').value, coordinator_id: document.getElementById('p5C').value, facilitator_ids: document.getElementById('p5C').value, schedule_block: 'Semester ${getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil')} ${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')}', academic_year: '${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')}', status: 'Berjalan' }, 'Proyek P5 ditambahkan!').then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Judul Proyek</label><input id="p5T" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Tema P5</label><input id="p5Th" required placeholder="Kearifan Lokal / Kewirausahaan / Suara Demokrasi" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Dimensi (pisahkan koma)</label><input id="p5Dim" required value="Gotong Royong, Bernalar Kritis, Kreatif" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kelas Sasaran (Contoh: CLS-7A,CLS-7B)</label><input id="p5Cls" value="CLS-7A,CLS-7B" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Koordinator</label><select id="p5C" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">${gurus.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}">${g.full_name}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>

        </form>
      `);
    }

    async function saveP5Facilitators(e, projectId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const prj = (STATE.db.KokurikulerProjects || []).find(p => p.id === projectId);
      const coord = document.getElementById('coord_' + projectId).value;
      const facs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(`input[name="fac_${projectId}"]:checked`)).map(c => c.value).join(',');
      await apiSave('KokurikulerProjects', { ...prj, coordinator_id: coord, facilitator_ids: facs }, 'Tim fasilitator P5 diperbarui!');
    }

    /**

    function renderWakaKurikulumModule() {
      const kaldik = STATE.db.KaldikEvents || [];
      const supervisi = STATE.db.SupervisiGuru || [];
      const gurus = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).filter(u => u.role === 'guru');
      return `
        <div class="space-y-6">
          <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Panel Wakil Kepala Sekolah Bidang Kurikulum</h2>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border space-y-3">
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-sm">Kalender Pendidikan (Kaldik)</h3>
                <button onclick="openAddKaldikModal()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Agenda Kaldik</button>
              </div>
              <div class="space-y-2 text-xs">
                ${kaldik.map(k => `<div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border flex justify-between"><div><p class="font-bold">${k.title}</p><p class="text-slate-500">${k.start_date} s.d. ${k.end_date} • ${k.category}</p></div><button onclick="apiDelete('KaldikEvents','${k.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></div>`).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border space-y-3">
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-sm">Supervisi Akademik Guru</h3>
                <button onclick="openAddSupervisiModal()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-teal-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Input Supervisi</button>
              </div>
              <div class="space-y-2 text-xs">
                ${supervisi.map(s => {
                  const g = gurus.find(u => u.id === s.teacher_id) || { full_name: s.teacher_id };
                  return `<div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border"><div class="flex justify-between font-bold"><span>${g.full_name}</span><span class="text-emerald-700">Skor: ${s.score}</span></div><p class="text-slate-500 mt-1">Modul: ${s.has_modul} | ATP: ${s.has_atp} | Asesmen: ${s.has_asesmen} | Jurnal: ${s.has_jurnal}</p><p class="mt-1">${s.feedback}</p></div>`;
                }).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function renderWakaKesiswaanModule() {
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      const violations = STATE.db.PelanggaranSiswa || [];
      const prestasi = STATE.db.PrestasiSiswa || [];
      const osis = STATE.db.OsisMembers || [];
      const pembiasaan = STATE.db.PembiasaanKarakter || [];

      // Rekapan terpadu pelanggaran per siswa dari semua guru
      const studentViolationSummary = students.map(st => {
        const list = violations.filter(v => v.student_id === st.id);
        const r = list.filter(v => v.category === 'Ringan').length;
        const s = list.filter(v => v.category === 'Sedang').length;
        const b = list.filter(v => v.category === 'Berat').length;
        const pts = list.reduce((acc, v) => acc + (Number(v.points) || 0), 0);
        let recSp = 'Aman / Pembinaan Rutin';
        if (pts >= 50 || b > 0) recSp = 'Rekomendasi SP-2 & Panggilan Orang Tua';
        else if (pts >= 20 || s > 0) recSp = 'Rekomendasi SP-1 & Konseling';
        return { st, list, r, s, b, pts, recSp };
      }).filter(x => x.list.length > 0);

      return `
        <div class="space-y-6">
          <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Panel Wakil Kepala Sekolah Bidang Kesiswaan</h2>
            <button onclick="openAddPelanggaranModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Input Pelanggaran</button>
          </div>

          <!-- REKAPAN TERPADU PELANGGARAN PER SISWA -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-5 space-y-3">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-sm text-rose-800">Rekapan Terpadu Pelanggaran Tata Tertib Per Siswa (Input Semua Guru)</h3>
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-2.5">Siswa</th><th class="p-2.5">Kelas</th><th class="p-2.5 text-center">Ringan</th><th class="p-2.5 text-center">Sedang</th><th class="p-2.5 text-center">Berat</th><th class="p-2.5 text-center">Total Poin</th><th class="p-2.5">Rekomendasi Tindak Lanjut</th><th class="p-2.5 text-right">Histori</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${studentViolationSummary.map(item => `
                    <tr>
                      <td class="p-2.5 font-bold">${item.st.full_name}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5">${item.st.class_id}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 text-center">${item.r}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 text-center">${item.s}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 text-center">${item.b}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 text-center font-extrabold text-rose-700">${item.pts}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 font-semibold text-amber-800">${item.recSp}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 text-right"><button onclick="openStudentViolationHistory('${item.st.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-100 font-bold">Detail Histori</button></td>
                    </tr>
                  `).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- PRESTASI, OSIS & PEMBIASAAN -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-xs">
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border space-y-2">
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center"><h4 class="font-bold">Prestasi Siswa</h4><button onclick="openAddPrestasiModal()" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold">+ Tambah</button></div>
              ${prestasi.map(p => {
                const st = students.find(s => s.id === p.student_id) || { full_name: p.student_id };
                return `<div class="p-2.5 rounded bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200"><p class="font-bold">${st.full_name}</p><p>${p.title} (${p.level})</p></div>`;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border space-y-2">
              <h4 class="font-bold">Pengurus OSIS & MPK</h4>
              ${osis.map(o => {
                const st = students.find(s => s.id === o.student_id) || { full_name: o.student_id };
                return `<div class="p-2.5 rounded bg-slate-50 border"><p class="font-bold">${st.full_name}</p><p class="text-indigo-700 font-semibold">${o.position} (${o.period})</p></div>`;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border space-y-2">
              <h4 class="font-bold">Pembiasaan Karakter & Ibadah</h4>
              ${pembiasaan.map(pm => `<div class="p-2.5 rounded bg-slate-50 border"><p class="font-bold">${pm.activity_name}</p><p class="text-slate-500">${pm.activity_date} • Partisipasi ${pm.participation_pct}%</p></div>`).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openStudentViolationHistory(studentId) {
      const st = (STATE.db.Students || []).find(s => s.id === studentId);
      const list = (STATE.db.PelanggaranSiswa || []).filter(v => v.student_id === studentId);
      openModal(`Detail Histori Pelanggaran — ${st.full_name}`, `
        <div class="space-y-2 text-xs">
          ${list.map(v => `<div class="p-3 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50"><div class="flex justify-between font-bold"><span>${v.incident_date} • ${v.category} (+${v.points} Poin)</span><span>${v.status}</span></div><p class="mt-1">${v.description}</p><p class="text-teal-700 mt-1">Sanksi: ${v.sanction}</p></div>`).join('')}
        </div>
      `);
    }

    function openSimpleHumasModal(type) {
      const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
      if (type === 'BukuTamu') {
        openModal('Catat Buku Tamu Sekolah', `
          <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('BukuTamu', { visit_date: '${today}', guest_name: document.getElementById('btN').value, institution: document.getElementById('btI').value, purpose: document.getElementById('btP').value, met_with: 'Kepala Sekolah' }, 'Tamu tercatat!').then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Tamu</label><input id="btN" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Instansi</label><input id="btI" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Keperluan</label><input id="btP" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
            <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan</button>
          </form>
        `);
      } else {
        openModal('Tambah Data Humas', `
          <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); const v=document.getElementById('hm1').value; const v2=document.getElementById('hm2').value; if('${type}'==='KemitraanKomite') apiSave('KemitraanKomite',{agenda_date:'${today}',topic:v,participants:'Komite & Sekolah',decision:v2,status:'Terlaksana'}).then(closeModal); else if('${type}'==='MouKerjasama') apiSave('MouKerjasama',{partner_name:v,scope:v2,start_date:'${today}',end_date:'2027-06-30',pic_name:STATE.user.full_name,status:'Aktif'}).then(closeModal); else apiSave('PublikasiSekolah',{pub_date:'${today}',title:v,channel:'Website Sekolah',author_id:STATE.user.id,summary:v2}).then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Judul / Nama Mitra</label><input id="hm1" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Keterangan / Keputusan</label><textarea id="hm2" required rows="2" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></textarea></div>
            <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan</button>
          </form>
        `);
      }
    }
