    function openSarprasModal(type) {
      const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
      openModal('Input Data Sarpras', `
        <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); const a=document.getElementById('spA').value; const b=document.getElementById('spB').value; if('${type}'==='InventarisRuang') apiSave('InventarisRuang',{room_code:a,room_name:b,condition:'Baik',capacity:30,pic_name:STATE.user.full_name}).then(closeModal); else if('${type}'==='BarangSarpras') apiSave('BarangSarpras',{item_code:'KIB-'+Date.now().toString().slice(-3),item_name:a,room_id:'R-UMUM',quantity:Number(b)||1,condition:'Baik',source_fund:'BOS'}).then(closeModal); else if('${type}'==='TiketPerbaikan') apiSave('TiketPerbaikan',{report_date:'${today}',reporter_id:STATE.user.id,item_name:a,location:'Sekolah',issue_desc:b,priority:'Sedang',status:'Dilaporkan'}).then(closeModal); else apiSave('PeminjamanSarpras',{borrow_date:'${today}',return_date:'${today}',borrower_name:a,item_name:b,quantity:1,purpose:'Kegiatan Sekolah',status:'Dipinjam'}).then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kode / Nama Barang / Peminjam</label><input id="spA" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Nama Ruang / Jumlah / Keterangan</label><input id="spB" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan Data</button>
        </form>
      `);
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 15. PANEL WALI KELAS (BERANDA, LEGER NILAI IDENTIK, PUSAT CETAK RAPORT)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function getActiveWaliClass() {
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      if (STATE.activeTargetId) {
        const found = classes.find(c => c.id === STATE.activeTargetId);
        if (found) return found;
      }
      return classes.find(c => c.wali_kelas_id === STATE.user.id) || classes[0] || { id: 'CLS-7A', name: '7A', kkm: 75, grade_level: '7' };
    }

    function renderWaliKelasBeranda() {
      const cls = getActiveWaliClass();
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => s.class_id === cls.id && s.status === 'Aktif');
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const maleCount = students.filter(s => s.gender === 'L').length;
      const femaleCount = students.filter(s => s.gender === 'P').length;

      // Hitung rerata kelas & peringkat siswa
      const rankings = students.map(st => {
        const scores = subjects.map(sub => getStudentSubjectReport(st.id, cls.id, sub.id).finalScore).filter(v => v !== null);
        const avg = scores.length ? (scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scores.length) : 0;
        const tuntasAll = scores.length > 0 && scores.every(v => v >= Number(cls.kkm));
        return { st, avg: Number(avg.toFixed(1)), tuntasAll };
      }).sort((a, b) => b.avg - a.avg);

      const classAvg = rankings.length ? (rankings.reduce((a, b) => a + b.avg, 0) / rankings.length).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
      const tuntasPct = rankings.length ? Math.round((rankings.filter(r => r.tuntasAll).length / rankings.length) * 100) : 0;

      return `
        <div class="space-y-6">
          <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-700 to-teal-700 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-md flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
              <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-200">Panel Wali Kelas Rombel ${cls.name}</span>
              <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1">Kelas ${cls.name} — ${siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-emerald-100 mt-1">KKM Kelas: ${cls.kkm} • Tahun Ajaran ${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex gap-2">
              <button onclick="navigateMenu('leger')" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white text-emerald-900 font-extrabold text-xs">Buka Leger Nilai</button>
              <button onclick="navigateMenu('raport')" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-900/50 text-white border border-white/30 font-extrabold text-xs">Cetak Raport</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border">
              <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Total Peserta Didik</p>
              <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1">${students.length} Siswa</p>
              <p class="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-700 mt-1">Laki-laki: ${maleCount} • Perempuan: ${femaleCount}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border">
              <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Rerata Nilai Kelas</p>
              <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-teal-700 mt-1">${classAvg}</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Berdasarkan nilai akhir terpadu</p>
            </div>
            <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border">
              <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Ketuntasan KKM (${cls.kkm})</p>
              <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-emerald-700 mt-1">${tuntasPct}%</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Siswa tuntas seluruh mapel dinilai</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-5">
            <h3 class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm mb-3">Peringkat Akademik Siswa Kelas ${cls.name}</h3>
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-2.5">Peringkat</th><th class="p-2.5">NIS</th><th class="p-2.5">Nama Siswa</th><th class="p-2.5 text-center">L/P</th><th class="p-2.5 text-center">Rerata Nilai</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${rankings.map((r, i) => `
                    <tr>
                      <td class="p-2.5 font-extrabold text-indigo-700">#${i + 1}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5">${r.st.nis}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 font-bold">${r.st.full_name}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 text-center">${r.st.gender}</td>
                      <td class="p-2.5 text-center font-extrabold">${r.avg}</td>
                    </tr>
                  `).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function renderLegerNilaiModule() {
      const classes = STATE.db.Classes || [];
      const cls = getActiveWaliClass();
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => s.class_id === cls.id && s.status === 'Aktif');
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const attitudes = STATE.db.AttitudeRatings || [];

      const subState = subjects.map(s => getSubjectFinalState(cls.id, s.id));
      const pendingSubs = subjects.filter((s, i) => subState[i] === 'pending');
      const headers = ['No', 'NIS', 'Nama Siswa', 'L/P', ...subjects.map(s => s.code), 'Jumlah', 'Rerata'];
      const rows = students.map((st, idx) => {
        const subVals = subjects.map(sub => getStudentSubjectReport(st.id, cls.id, sub.id).finalScore);
        const valid = subVals.filter(v => v !== null);
        const sum = valid.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
        const avg = valid.length ? (sum / valid.length).toFixed(1) : '-';
        return [idx + 1, st.nis, st.full_name, st.gender, ...subVals.map(v => v ?? '-'), valid.length ? sum : '-', avg];
      });

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Penilaian & Leger Nilai — Kelas ${cls.name}</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Nilai akhir terhitung otomatis & identik 100% dengan Guru Mapel dan Raport Siswa</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              ${['admin', 'kepala_sekolah'].includes(STATE.activeRole) ? `
                <select onchange="STATE.activeTargetId=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
                  ${classes.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}" ${c.id === cls.id ? 'selected' : ''}>Kelas ${c.name}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              ` : ''}
              <button onclick='copyTableTsv(${JSON.stringify([headers, ...rows])})' class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">Salin TSV</button>
              <button onclick='exportTableToExcel(${JSON.stringify([headers, ...rows])}, "Leger_${cls.name}", "Leger_Nilai_${cls.name}.xlsx")' class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Excel .xlsx</button>
              <button onclick="printLegerTable('${cls.id}')" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Cetak Leger</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-xs ${pendingSubs.length ? 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-200 text-rose-800' : 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800'}">
            ${pendingSubs.length
              ? `<strong>${pendingSubs.length} mata pelajaran belum final</strong> (kolom merah): ${pendingSubs.map(s => siEsc(s.name)).join(', ')}. Tunggu guru mapel menekan "Setujui Nilai" sebelum mencetak raport.`
              : '<strong>Seluruh nilai mata pelajaran sudah disetujui guru</strong> (kolom hijau) — leger siap dicetak.'}
            <span class="ml-2 inline-flex gap-3 font-semibold"><span><i class="inline-block w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-sm bg-emerald-600"></i> Final</span><span><i class="inline-block w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-sm bg-rose-600"></i> Belum final</span></span>
          </div>

          <div class="flex gap-2 bg-white p-1.5 rounded-2xl border w-fit text-xs font-bold">
            <button onclick="STATE.legerTab='raport'; renderApp();" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl ${STATE.legerTab === 'raport' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'}">Leger Nilai Raport</button>
            <button onclick="STATE.legerTab='sikap'; renderApp();" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl ${STATE.legerTab === 'sikap' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'}">Rekapitulasi Nilai Sikap (1-5)</button>
          </div>

          ${STATE.legerTab === 'raport' ? `
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">
              <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                <table class="w-full text-xs text-left border-collapse">
                  <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                    <tr>${headers.map((h, hi) => { const si = hi - 4; const st = (si >= 0 && si < subjects.length) ? subState[si] : ''; return `<th class="p-3 border border-slate-700 text-center ${st === 'final' ? 'bg-emerald-600' : st === 'pending' ? 'bg-rose-600' : ''}" title="${st === 'final' ? 'Nilai sudah final' : st === 'pending' ? 'Guru belum menyetujui nilai' : ''}">${h}</th>`; }).join('')}</tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody class="divide-y">
                    ${rows.map(r => `
                      <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">
                        ${r.map((c, i) => { const si = i - 4; const st = (si >= 0 && si < subjects.length) ? subState[si] : ''; return `<td class="p-2.5 border border-slate-200 ${i === 2 ? 'font-bold whitespace-nowrap' : 'text-center font-semibold'} ${st === 'final' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800' : st === 'pending' ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700' : ''}">${c}</td>`; }).join('')}
                      </tr>
                    `).join('')}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          ` : `
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">
              <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                <table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                  <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                    <tr><th class="p-3">No</th><th class="p-3">Nama Siswa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Rerata Sikap (1-5)</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Predikat Sikap</th></tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody class="divide-y">
                    ${students.map((st, idx) => {
                      const stAtt = attitudes.filter(a => a.student_id === st.id);
                      const avg = stAtt.length ? (stAtt.reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.score), 0) / stAtt.length).toFixed(2) : '4.50';
                      return `<tr><td class="p-3">${idx + 1}</td><td class="p-3 font-bold">${st.full_name}</td><td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${avg}</td><td class="p-3 text-center font-bold text-emerald-700">Sangat Baik (A)</td></tr>`;
                    }).join('')}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          `}
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function printLegerTable(classId) {
      const cls = (STATE.db.Classes || []).find(c => c.id === classId) || { name: classId };
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => s.class_id === classId && s.status === 'Aktif');
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const headers = ['No', 'NIS', 'Nama Siswa', 'L/P', ...subjects.map(s => s.code), 'Jumlah', 'Rerata'];
      const rows = students.map((st, idx) => {
        const subVals = subjects.map(sub => getStudentSubjectReport(st.id, classId, sub.id).finalScore);
        const valid = subVals.filter(v => v !== null);
        const sum = valid.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
        const avg = valid.length ? (sum / valid.length).toFixed(1) : '-';
        return [idx + 1, st.nis, st.full_name, st.gender, ...subVals.map(v => v ?? '-'), valid.length ? sum : '-', avg];
      });
      triggerPrintHtml(`
        ${renderOfficialLetterhead()}
        <h3 style="text-align:center;font-weight:bold;margin:10px 0;">LEGER NILAI RAPORT PESERTA DIDIK — KELAS ${cls.name}</h3>
        <table>
          <thead><tr>${headers.map(h => `<th>${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>${rows.map(r => `<tr>${r.map(c => `<td>${c}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody>
        </table>
        ${renderSignatureBlock('Wali Kelas ' + cls.name, STATE.user.full_name, STATE.user.nip)}
      `);
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 16. PUSAT CETAK BUKU RAPORT SISWA (BAGIAN A, B, C, D, E + KOP + TTD RESMI)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderRaportCenterModule() {
      const cls = getActiveWaliClass();
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => s.class_id === cls.id && s.status === 'Aktif');
      const selectedId = STATE.selectedReportStudentId === 'ALL' ? (students[0]?.id || '') : STATE.selectedReportStudentId;
      const previewStudent = students.find(s => s.id === selectedId) || students[0];

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">Pilih Siswa (Kelas ${cls.name}):</span>
              <select onchange="STATE.selectedReportStudentId=this.value; renderApp();" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
                ${students.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}" ${previewStudent && s.id === previewStudent.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${s.full_name} (${s.nis})</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              ${previewStudent ? `
                <button onclick="printSingleRaport('${previewStudent.id}')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <i data-lucide="printer" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Cetak Raport Siswa Ini
                </button>
              ` : ''}
              <button onclick="printAllClassRaports('${cls.id}')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="layers" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Cetak Seluruh Kelas (${students.length} Siswa)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          ${previewStudent ? `
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-300 p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-sm">
              ${buildStudentRaportHtml(previewStudent.id, false)}
            </div>
          ` : ''}
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function buildStudentRaportHtml(studentId, isPrintMode = false) {
      const st = (STATE.db.Students || []).find(s => s.id === studentId);
      if (!st) return '';
      const cls = (STATE.db.Classes || []).find(c => c.id === st.class_id) || { id: st.class_id, name: st.class_id, kkm: 75 };
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const users = STATE.db.UsersPublic || [];
      const waliKelas = users.find(u => u.id === cls.wali_kelas_id) || { full_name: 'Wali Kelas', nip: '-' };

      // Bagian B: Ekstrakurikuler (Pramuka ditampilkan sebagai "Praja Muda Karana (Pramuka)" TANPA tulisan "Wajib")
      const ekskuls = STATE.db.Ekstrakurikuler || [];
      const ekAssessments = (STATE.db.EkskulAssessments || []).filter(a => a.student_id === st.id);
      const ekskulRows = [];
      const pramukaMaster = ekskuls.find(e => String(e.is_mandatory) === 'true' || String(e.name).toLowerCase().includes('pramuka'));
      const pramukaAss = pramukaMaster ? ekAssessments.find(a => a.ekskul_id === pramukaMaster.id) : null;
      ekskulRows.push({
        name: 'Praja Muda Karana (Pramuka)',
        predicate: pramukaAss ? pramukaAss.predicate : 'Baik',
        note: pramukaAss ? pramukaAss.note : 'Aktif mengikuti kegiatan kepramukaan dan pembinaan karakter.'
      });
      ekAssessments.forEach(a => {
        if (pramukaMaster && a.ekskul_id === pramukaMaster.id) return;
        const ekObj = ekskuls.find(e => e.id === a.ekskul_id);
        if (ekObj) {
          ekskulRows.push({ name: ekObj.name, predicate: a.predicate, note: a.note });
        }
      });

      // Bagian C: Kokurikuler (P5) sasaran kelas siswa
      const targetProjects = (STATE.db.KokurikulerProjects || []).filter(p => String(p.target_classes || '').includes(cls.id));
      const p5Evals = (STATE.db.KokurikulerEvaluations || []).filter(e => e.student_id === st.id);

      // Bagian D: Ketidakhadiran dari AttendanceRecords
      const attRecs = (STATE.db.AttendanceRecords || []).filter(r => r.student_id === st.id);
      const sakitCnt = attRecs.filter(r => r.status === 'S').length;
      const izinCnt = attRecs.filter(r => r.status === 'I').length;
      const alpaCnt = attRecs.filter(r => r.status === 'A').length;

      // Bagian E: Catatan Wali Kelas & Guru Wali
      const gwRec = (STATE.db.GuruWaliRecords || []).find(r => r.student_id === st.id);
      const catatanWali = gwRec
        ? `${gwRec.note} ${gwRec.recommendation ? 'Rekomendasi: ' + gwRec.recommendation : ''}`
        : 'Peserta didik menunjukkan sikap sopan, beriman, dan semangat belajar yang baik. Terus tingkatkan prestasi di semester berikutnya.';

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5 text-slate-900">
          ${renderOfficialLetterhead()}
          <div class="text-center border-b-2 border-slate-800 pb-2">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-base uppercase">LAPORAN HASIL BELAJAR PESERTA DIDIK (RAPORT)</h3>
            <p class="text-xs">Tahun Ajaran ${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')} • Semester ${getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil')}</p>
          </div>

          <!-- IDENTITAS SISWA -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
            <div class="space-y-1">
              <p>Nama Peserta Didik : <strong>${st.full_name}</strong></p>
              <p>NIS / NISN : <strong>${st.nis} / ${st.nisn}</strong></p>
              
            </div>
            <div class="space-y-1">
              <p>Kelas / Fase : <strong>${cls.name} / Fase D</strong></p>
              <p>Semester : <strong>${getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil') === 'Genap' ? '2 (Genap)' : '1 (Ganjil)'}</strong></p>
              <p>Tahun Ajaran : <strong>${getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')}</strong></p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- BAGIAN A. CAPAIAN PEMBELAJARAN (INTRAKURIKULER) -->
          <div>
            <h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs uppercase mb-1.5">A. Capaian Pembelajaran (Intrakurikuler)</h4>
            <table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse border border-slate-400">
              <thead class="bg-slate-100">
                <tr>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 w-8">No</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-left">Mata Pelajaran</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 w-12 text-center">KKM</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 w-16 text-center">Nilai Akhir</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 w-16 text-center">Predikat</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-left">Capaian Kompetensi / Alur Tujuan Pembelajaran</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                ${subjects.map((sub, idx) => {
                  const rep = getStudentSubjectReport(st.id, cls.id, sub.id);
                  return `
                    <tr>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center">${idx + 1}</td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 font-semibold">${sub.name}</td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center">${cls.kkm}</td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center font-bold">${rep.finalScore !== null ? rep.finalScore : '-'}</td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center font-bold">${rep.predicate}</td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 leading-snug">${rep.description}</td>
                    </tr>
                  `;
                }).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <!-- BAGIAN B. KEGIATAN EKSTRAKURIKULER -->
          <div>
            <h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs uppercase mb-1.5">B. Kegiatan Ekstrakurikuler</h4>
            <table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse border border-slate-400">
              <thead class="bg-slate-100">
                <tr>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 w-8">No</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-left">Kegiatan Ekstrakurikuler</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 w-28 text-center">Predikat</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-left">Keterangan</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                ${ekskulRows.map((ek, idx) => `
                  <tr>
                    <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center">${idx + 1}</td>
                    <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 font-semibold">${ek.name}</td>
                    <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center font-bold">${ek.predicate}</td>
                    <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2">${ek.note}</td>
                  </tr>
                `).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <!-- BAGIAN C. KOKURIKULER (PROJEK PENGUATAN PROFIL PELAJAR) -->
          <div>
            <h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs uppercase mb-1.5">C. Kokurikuler (Projek Penguatan Profil Pelajar)</h4>
            <table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse border border-slate-400">
              <thead class="bg-slate-100">
                <tr>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 w-8">No</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-left">Judul & Tema Projek</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-left">Capaian Dimensi</th>
                  <th class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-left">Catatan Proses Fasilitator</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                ${targetProjects.length === 0 ? `<tr><td colspan="4" class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center">Belum ada proyek kokurikuler pada semester ini.</td></tr>` : targetProjects.map((prj, idx) => {
                  const ev = p5Evals.find(e => e.project_id === prj.id);
                  let dimText = prj.dimensions + ' (BSH)';
                  if (ev && ev.dimension_scores) {
                    try {
                      const dObj = JSON.parse(ev.dimension_scores);
                      dimText = Object.entries(dObj).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: <strong>${v}</strong>`).join('; ');
                    } catch (e) {}
                  }
                  return `
                    <tr>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 text-center">${idx + 1}</td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2"><strong>${prj.title}</strong><br><span class="text-[11px]">Tema: ${prj.theme}</span></td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2">${dimText}</td>
                      <td class="border border-slate-400 p-2">${ev ? ev.note : 'Peserta didik berpartisipasi aktif dalam seluruh tahapan proyek.'}</td>
                    </tr>
                  `;
                }).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <!-- BAGIAN D & E. KETIDAKHADIRAN & CATATAN WALI KELAS -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs uppercase mb-1.5">D. Ketidakhadiran</h4>
              <table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse border border-slate-400">
                <tr><td class="border border-slate-400 p-2">Sakit</td><td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 font-bold">${sakitCnt} hari</td></tr>
                <tr><td class="border border-slate-400 p-2">Izin</td><td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 font-bold">${izinCnt} hari</td></tr>
                <tr><td class="border border-slate-400 p-2">Tanpa Keterangan</td><td class="border border-slate-400 p-2 font-bold">${alpaCnt} hari</td></tr>
              </table>
            </div>
            <div>
              <h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs uppercase mb-1.5">E. Catatan Wali Kelas & Karakter</h4>
              <div class="border border-slate-400 p-3 text-xs min-h-[88px]">
                ${catatanWali}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- BLOK TANDA TANGAN RESMI -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 pt-6 text-xs text-center">
            <div>
              <p>Mengetahui,</p>
              <p>Orang Tua / Wali Murid</p>
              <div class="h-16"></div>
              <p class="font-bold underline">(${st.parent_name || '....................................'})</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p>Mengetahui,</p>
              <p>Kepala ${siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}</p>
              <div class="h-16"></div>
              <p class="font-bold underline">${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().headmaster_name)}</p>
              <p>NIP. ${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().headmaster_nip || '-')}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p>${siEsc(siPlaceDate())}</p>
              <p>Wali Kelas ${cls.name}</p>
              <div class="h-16"></div>
              <p class="font-bold underline">${waliKelas.full_name}</p>
              <p>NIP. ${waliKelas.nip || '-'}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function printSingleRaport(studentId) {
      triggerPrintHtml(buildStudentRaportHtml(studentId, true));
    }

    function printAllClassRaports(classId) {
      const students = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => s.class_id === classId && s.status === 'Aktif');
      const allHtml = students.map((s, i) => `
        <div class="${i < students.length - 1 ? 'page-break' : ''}">
          ${buildStudentRaportHtml(s.id, true)}
        </div>
      `).join('');
      triggerPrintHtml(allHtml);
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 17. PANEL WALI MURID (4 MENU SINKRON, TANPA MENU PENGUMUMAN)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderWaliMuridModule(menuId) {
      const students = STATE.db.Students || [];
      // Akun wali murid wajib ditautkan ke ID siswa melalui kolom Users.nip.
      // Jangan memakai siswa pertama sebagai fallback karena berisiko menampilkan data anak yang salah.
      const linkedStudentId = String(STATE.user.nip || '').trim();
      const child = linkedStudentId ? students.find(s => String(s.id).trim() === linkedStudentId) : null;
      if (!child) return `
        <div class="max-w-2xl mx-auto p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 space-y-2">
          <h2 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900">Data peserta didik belum tertaut</h2>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-600">Akun wali murid ini belum terhubung dengan ID siswa yang valid. Silakan hubungi Administrator agar kolom NIP pada akun wali murid diisi dengan ID siswa yang benar.</p>
        </div>`;

      const cls = (STATE.db.Classes || []).find(c => c.id === child.class_id) || { id: child.class_id, name: child.class_id, kkm: 75 };
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const users = STATE.db.UsersPublic || [];
      const guruWali = users.find(u => u.id === child.guru_wali_id) || { full_name: '-' };

      // Nav tabs sinkron dengan sidebar
      const topBar = `
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 bg-white p-2 rounded-2xl border mb-5">
          ${[
            { id: 'wm_beranda', label: 'Beranda' },
            { id: 'wm_rapor', label: '1. Rapor & Capaian Anak' },
            { id: 'wm_profil', label: '2. Profil & Data Siswa' },
            { id: 'wm_jadwal_ekskul', label: '3. Jadwal & Ekskul Anak' },
            { id: 'wm_karakter', label: '4. Karakter & Bimbingan Wali' }
          ].map(t => `
            <button onclick="navigateMenu('${t.id}')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold ${menuId === t.id ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}">
              ${t.label}
            </button>
          `).join('')}
        </div>
      `;

      if (menuId === 'wm_beranda') return renderWmBeranda(child, cls, topBar);

      if (menuId === 'wm_rapor') {
        return `
          <div>
            ${topBar}
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-5 space-y-4">
              <div>
                <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">Rapor Akademik & Capaian Kompetensi — ${child.full_name}</h2>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Kelas ${cls.name} • KKM ${cls.kkm} • Real-time dari penilaian guru mata pelajaran</p>
              </div>
              <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                <table class="w-full text-xs text-left border-collapse">
                  <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                    <tr><th class="p-3">Mata Pelajaran</th><th class="p-3 text-center">KKM</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Nilai Akhir</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Predikat</th><th class="p-3">Deskripsi Capaian Kompetensi / Alur TP</th></tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody class="divide-y">
                    ${subjects.map(sub => {
                      const rep = getStudentSubjectReport(child.id, cls.id, sub.id);
                      return `
                        <tr>
                          <td class="p-3 font-bold">${sub.name}</td>
                          <td class="p-3 text-center">${cls.kkm}</td>
                          <td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${rep.finalScore ?? '-'}</td>
                          <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${rep.predicateFull}</td>
                          <td class="p-3">${rep.description}</td>
                        </tr>
                      `;
                    }).join('')}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      if (menuId === 'wm_profil') {
        return `
          <div>
            ${topBar}
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-6 max-w-2xl space-y-3 text-xs">
              <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">Profil & Data Peserta Didik</h2>
              <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2">
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50"><p class="text-slate-400">Nama Lengkap</p><p class="font-bold text-sm mt-0.5">${child.full_name}</p></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50"><p class="text-slate-400">NIS / NISN</p><p class="font-bold text-sm mt-0.5">${child.nis} / ${child.nisn}</p></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50"><p class="text-slate-400">Kelas</p><p class="font-bold text-sm mt-0.5">Kelas ${cls.name}</p></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50"><p class="text-slate-400">Cita-cita</p><p class="font-bold text-sm mt-0.5">${child.dream_job || '-'}</p></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50"><p class="text-indigo-600">Tingkat Literasi</p><p class="font-extrabold text-sm text-indigo-900 mt-0.5">${child.literacy_level}</p></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-teal-50"><p class="text-teal-600">Tingkat Numerasi</p><p class="font-extrabold text-sm text-teal-900 mt-0.5">${child.numeracy_level}</p></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      if (menuId === 'wm_jadwal_ekskul') {
        const schedules = (STATE.db.JadwalPelajaran || []).filter(j => j.class_id === cls.id);
        const ekAssess = (STATE.db.EkskulAssessments || []).filter(a => a.student_id === child.id);
        const ekskuls = STATE.db.Ekstrakurikuler || [];
        return `
          <div>
            ${topBar}
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 text-xs">
              <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border space-y-3">
                <h3 class="font-extrabold text-sm">Jadwal Pelajaran Kelas ${cls.name}</h3>
                ${(() => {
                  const jk = (STATE.db.JadwalKelas || []).filter(r => r.class_id === cls.id);
                  if (!jk.length) {
                    return schedules.length ? schedules.map(j => {
                      const sub = subjects.find(s => s.id === j.subject_id) || { name: j.subject_id };
                      return `<div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border flex justify-between"><span><strong>${j.day_name}</strong> (${normTime(j.start_time)}–${addMin(j.start_time, (Number(j.jp_count) || 0) * getJpDuration())}) — ${sub.name}</span><span class="font-bold">${j.jp_count} JP</span></div>`;
                    }).join('') : '<p class="text-slate-500">Jadwal belum disusun oleh sekolah.</p>';
                  }
                  const dur = getJpDuration(), today = todayDayName();
                  const days = DAY_NAMES.filter(d => d !== 'Sabtu' || jk.some(x => x.day_name === 'Sabtu'));
                  return `<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-[11px] border-collapse">
                    <thead><tr class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><th class="p-2 text-left">Jam</th>${days.map(d => `<th class="p-2 ${d === today ? 'bg-indigo-600' : ''}">${d}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
                    <tbody>${getSlots().map(s => `<tr><td class="p-2 border whitespace-nowrap font-bold bg-slate-50">${s.slot_no}<div class="text-[10px] font-normal text-slate-500">${normTime(s.start_time)}–${addMin(s.start_time, dur)}</div></td>
                      ${days.map(d => { const r = jk.find(x => x.day_name === d && Number(x.slot_no) === Number(s.slot_no)); return `<td class="p-2 border ${d === today ? 'bg-indigo-50' : ''}">${r ? `<div class="font-bold">${siEsc(subjectName(r.subject_id))}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">${siEsc(userName(r.teacher_id))}</div>` : ''}</td>`; }).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
                })()}
              </div>
              <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border space-y-3">
                <h3 class="font-extrabold text-sm">Ekstrakurikuler yang Diikuti</h3>
                ${ekAssess.map(a => {
                  const ek = ekskuls.find(e => e.id === a.ekskul_id) || { name: 'Pramuka' };
                  return `<div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200"><div class="flex justify-between font-bold"><span>${ek.name}</span><span class="text-emerald-800">${a.predicate}</span></div><p class="mt-1 text-slate-600">${a.note}</p></div>`;
                }).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      // wm_karakter
      const gwRec = (STATE.db.GuruWaliRecords || []).find(r => r.student_id === child.id);
      return `
        <div>
          ${topBar}
          <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border max-w-3xl space-y-4 text-xs">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">Catatan Pembimbingan Karakter dari Guru Wali</h2>
              <p class="text-slate-500">Guru Wali Pembimbing: <strong>${guruWali.full_name}</strong></p>
            </div>
            ${gwRec ? `
              <div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border">Disiplin: <strong>${gwRec.disiplin}/5</strong></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border">Kemandirian: <strong>${gwRec.kemandirian}/5</strong></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border">Kejujuran: <strong>${gwRec.kejujuran}/5</strong></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border">Relasi Sosial: <strong>${gwRec.relasi_sosial}/5</strong></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border">Spiritual: <strong>${gwRec.spiritual}/5</strong></div>
                <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border">Keaktifan: <strong>${gwRec.keaktifan}/5</strong></div>
              </div>
              <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200">
                <p class="font-bold text-indigo-900">Catatan Perkembangan:</p>
                <p class="mt-1 text-slate-800">${gwRec.note}</p>
                <p class="font-bold text-teal-800 mt-2">Rekomendasi: ${gwRec.recommendation || '-'}</p>
              </div>
            ` : `<p class="text-slate-500">Belum ada catatan pembimbingan pada semester ini.</p>`}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * 18. PENGUMUMAN & EDARAN RESMI (CRUD + PIN + CETAK EDARAN)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderPengumumanModule() {
      const list = [...(STATE.db.Announcements || [])].sort((a, b) => (String(b.is_pinned) === 'true' ? 1 : 0) - (String(a.is_pinned) === 'true' ? 1 : 0));
      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Papan Pengumuman & Edaran Resmi Sekolah</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Informasi kedinasan, akademik, kesiswaan, dan agenda ${siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name)}</p>
            </div>
            <button onclick="openAddAnnouncementModal()" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Buat Pengumuman</button>
          </div>

          <div class="space-y-3">
            ${list.map(a => `
              <div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border ${String(a.is_pinned) === 'true' ? 'border-amber-400 bg-amber-50/20' : 'border-slate-200'} space-y-2">
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                    ${String(a.is_pinned) === 'true' ? `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold">📌 PIN</span>` : ''}
                    <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 font-bold">${a.category}</span>
                    <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded ${a.priority === 'Mendesak' ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700'} font-bold">${a.priority}</span>
                    <span class="text-slate-400">${a.created_at} • Oleh: ${a.author_name}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="flex gap-2 text-xs">
                    <button onclick="printAnnouncement('${a.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-white font-bold">Cetak Edaran</button>
                    <button onclick="apiDelete('Announcements', '${a.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 font-bold">Hapus</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <h3 class="font-extrabold text-base text-slate-900">${a.title}</h3>
                <p class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">${a.content}</p>
              </div>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function openAddAnnouncementModal() {
      openModal('Buat Pengumuman / Edaran Baru', `
        <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); apiSave('Announcements', { created_at: new Date().toISOString().slice(0,16).replace('T',' '), author_id: STATE.user.id, author_name: STATE.user.full_name, category: document.getElementById('anCat').value, priority: document.getElementById('anPri').value, is_pinned: document.getElementById('anPin').checked ? 'true' : 'false', title: document.getElementById('anTit').value, content: document.getElementById('anCon').value }, 'Pengumuman diterbitkan!').then(closeModal);" class="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Kategori</label><select id="anCat" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"><option>Akademik</option><option>Kedinasan</option><option>Kesiswaan</option><option>Sarpras</option><option>Kegiatan</option><option>Umum</option></select></div>
            <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Prioritas</label><select id="anPri" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"><option>Biasa</option><option>Penting</option><option>Mendesak</option></select></div>
          </div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Judul Pengumuman</label><input id="anTit" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></div>
          <div><label class="block font-bold mb-1">Isi Pengumuman</label><textarea id="anCon" required rows="4" class="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"></textarea></div>
          <label class="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold"><input type="checkbox" id="anPin"> Sematkan (Pin) di paling atas</label>
          <button type="submit" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Terbitkan Pengumuman</button>
        </form>
      `);
    }

    function printAnnouncement(annId) {
      const a = (STATE.db.Announcements || []).find(x => x.id === annId);
      if (!a) return;
      triggerPrintHtml(`
        ${renderOfficialLetterhead()}
        <h3 style="text-align:center;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;margin:14px 0;">PENGUMUMAN / EDARAN SEKOLAH</h3>
        <p style="font-size:10pt;">Perihal: <strong>${a.title}</strong> (${a.category})</p>
        <p style="font-size:10pt;">Tanggal: ${a.created_at}</p>
        <div style="margin:18px 0;font-size:11pt;line-height:1.6;">${a.content}</div>
        ${renderSignatureBlock('Kepala ' + siNameCase(getSchoolIdentity().school_name), siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().headmaster_name), siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().headmaster_nip))}
      `);
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * HELPER KOP SURAT & BLOK TANDA TANGAN RESMI SMP KRISTEN WAIBAKUL
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderOfficialLetterhead(idn) {
      idn = idn || getSchoolIdentity();
      const addr = [idn.school_address, idn.school_postal_code ? 'Kode Pos ' + idn.school_postal_code : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' • ');
      const line1 = [idn.school_npsn ? 'NPSN: ' + idn.school_npsn : '', addr].filter(Boolean).join(' • ');
      const line2 = [idn.school_phone ? 'Telp. ' + idn.school_phone : '', idn.school_email ? 'Email: ' + idn.school_email : '', idn.school_website].filter(Boolean).join(' • ');
      const hasLogo = !!(idn.school_logo_left || idn.school_logo_right);
      const logo = src => src
        ? `<img src="${siEsc(src)}" style="height:70px;width:70px;object-fit:contain;flex:none;">`
        : (hasLogo ? '<div style="width:70px;flex:none;"></div>' : '');
      return `
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;border-bottom:3px double #000;padding-bottom:8px;margin-bottom:12px;">
          ${logo(idn.school_logo_left)}
          <div style="flex:1;text-align:center;">
            ${idn.school_foundation ? `<div style="font-size:11pt;font-weight:bold;">${siEsc(idn.school_foundation)}</div>` : ''}
            <div style="font-size:15pt;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:0.5px;">${siEsc(idn.school_name)}</div>
            <div style="font-size:9pt;">${siEsc(line1)}</div>
            ${line2 ? `<div style="font-size:9pt;">${siEsc(line2)}</div>` : ''}
          </div>
          ${logo(idn.school_logo_right)}
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function renderSignatureBlock(roleTitle, personName, nip = '-') {
      return `
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:24px;font-size:10pt;">
          <div style="text-align:center;width:240px;">
            <p>${siEsc(getSchoolIdentity().report_place)}, ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}</p>
            <p>${roleTitle},</p>
            <div style="height:60px;"></div>
            <p style="font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;">${personName}</p>
            <p>NIP. ${nip || '-'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    
    function getSettingVal(key, fallback) {
      const found = (STATE.db.Settings || []).find(s => s.key === key);
      return found && found.value ? found.value : fallback;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * MODUL KHUSUS ADMIN: RESET DATA UNTUK TAHUN PELAJARAN BARU
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderResetDataModule() {
      const curYear = getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027');
      const curSem = getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil');
      const totalStudents = (STATE.db.Students || []).length;
      const totalClasses = (STATE.db.Classes || []).length;
      const totalSessions = (STATE.db.AttendanceSessions || []).length;
      const totalRecords = (STATE.db.AttendanceRecords || []).length;

      return `
        <div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
          <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-700 to-amber-700 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-md">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-white/15 flex items-center justify-center">
                <i data-lucide="rotate-ccw" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-200">Manajemen Siklus Tahunan • Khusus Admin</span>
                <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-0.5">Reset Data Aplikasi (Tahun Pelajaran Baru)</h2>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-rose-100 mt-3 leading-relaxed">
              Gunakan fitur ini pada akhir tahun pelajaran untuk mengosongkan seluruh data akademik, nilai, absensi, jurnal, raport, dan rombongan belajar sehingga aplikasi siap digunakan kembali dari awal pada tahun pelajaran berikutnya.
            </p>
          </div>

          <!-- RINGKASAN DATA SAAT INI -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">Ringkasan Data Tersimpan Saat Ini (T.A. ${curYear} — ${curSem})</h3>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-center">

              <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border"><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Kelas</p><p class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">${totalClasses}</p></div>
              <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border"><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Sesi KBM</p><p class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">${totalSessions}</p></div>
              <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border"><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Data Nilai/Absen</p><p class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">${totalRecords}</p></div>
            </div>

            <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <p class="font-bold text-amber-900 text-xs sm:text-sm">Langkah 1 (Disarankan): Unduh Arsip Cadangan JSON</p>
                <p class="text-xs text-amber-800 mt-0.5">Simpan arsip seluruh nilai, leger, dan data tahun ${curYear} ke komputer sebelum menghapus.</p>
              </div>
              <button onclick="downloadJsonBackup()" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white text-xs font-extrabold shrink-0">
                Download Backup JSON
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- KONFIGURASI RESET & KONFIRMASI -->
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border-2 border-rose-200 p-6 space-y-4">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-rose-800 text-base">Langkah 2: Konfigurasi Tahun Pelajaran Baru & Reset</h3>

            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
              <div>
                <label class="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Tahun Pelajaran Baru</label>
                <input id="resetNewYear" type="text" value="2027/2028" placeholder="Contoh: 2027/2028" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-bold">
              </div>
              <div>
                <label class="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Semester Awal</label>
                <select id="resetNewSem" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-bold">
                  <option value="Ganjil" selected>Ganjil</option>
                  <option value="Genap">Genap</option>
                </select>
              </div>

            <div class="space-y-2 pt-1 text-xs">
              <label class="flex items-start gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 cursor-pointer">

                  <span class="font-bold text-slate-800">Pertahankan Data Akun Guru & Pegawai (Users)</span>
                  <p class="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Jika dicentang, akun login guru tetap tersedia tanpa perlu input ulang. Jika tidak dicentang, hanya akun Admin utama yang dipertahankan.</p>
                </div>
              </label>

              <label class="flex items-start gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 cursor-pointer">
                <input id="resetKeepSubjects" type="checkbox" checked class="mt-0.5 rounded text-indigo-600">
                <div>
                  <span class="font-bold text-slate-800">Pertahankan Master Daftar Mata Pelajaran</span>
                  <p class="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Mempertahankan daftar kode dan nama mata pelajaran standar SMP Kristen Waibakul.</p>
                </div>
              </label>
            </div>

            <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 space-y-2 text-xs">
              <p class="font-bold text-rose-800">Konfirmasi Keamanan:</p>
              <p class="text-rose-700">Seluruh data Siswa, Rombel/Kelas, Penugasan Mengajar, Jabatan Tambahan, Absensi, Nilai, Jurnal KBM, Kokurikuler P5, Pelanggaran, Prestasi, Sarpras, Humas, dan Pengumuman akan dikosongkan permanen. Ketik <strong>RESET DATA</strong> di bawah ini untuk mengaktifkan tombol reset:</p>
              <input id="resetConfirmInput" type="text" oninput="toggleResetBtnState()" placeholder="Ketik: RESET DATA" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-rose-300 font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider focus:outline-none focus:border-rose-600">
            </div>

            <button id="btnExecuteReset" disabled onclick="executeDatabaseReset()" class="w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 disabled:bg-slate-300 disabled:text-slate-500 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white font-extrabold text-sm shadow-md transition">
              Kosongkan Semua Data & Mulai Tahun Pelajaran Baru
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function toggleResetBtnState() {
      const inp = document.getElementById('resetConfirmInput');
      const btn = document.getElementById('btnExecuteReset');
      if (!inp || !btn) return;
      btn.disabled = inp.value.trim().toUpperCase() !== 'RESET DATA';
    }

    async function executeDatabaseReset() {
      const inp = document.getElementById('resetConfirmInput');
      if (!inp || inp.value.trim().toUpperCase() !== 'RESET DATA') {
        showToast('Ketik RESET DATA terlebih dahulu untuk konfirmasi.', 'warning');
        return;
      }

      const options = {
        newAcademicYear: document.getElementById('resetNewYear').value.trim() || '2027/2028',
        newSemester: document.getElementById('resetNewSem').value,
        keepUsers: document.getElementById('resetKeepUsers').checked,
        keepSubjects: document.getElementById('resetKeepSubjects').checked
      };

      showLoader('Mengosongkan database & menyiapkan tahun pelajaran baru...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        const res = await callServer('resetDatabaseForNewAcademicYear', options, actor);
        if (res && res.success) {
          STATE.selectedAssignClassId = null;
          STATE.selectedTeacherClassId = null;
          STATE.selectedSessionId = null;
          await loadAppData(true);
          showToast(`Database berhasil dikosongkan! Aplikasi siap untuk T.A. ${res.newAcademicYear} (${res.newSemester}).`, 'success');
          navigateMenu('dashboard');
        }
      } catch (e) {
        showToast('Gagal mereset database: ' + e.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }
