    function siEsc(s) {
      return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
    }
    function siNameCase(name) {
      return siEsc(String(name || '').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map(w => w.length <= 3 ? w : w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase()).join(' '));
    }
    function siInitials(name) {
      const w = String(name || '').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
      return w.length ? (w[0][0] + (w.length > 1 ? w[w.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase() : 'SK';
    }
    function formatIndoDate(iso) {
      const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(String(iso || ''));
      if (!m) return '';
      const bln = ['Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni', 'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'];
      return `${Number(m[3])} ${bln[Number(m[2]) - 1]} ${m[1]}`;
    }
    function getSchoolIdentity() {
      const o = {};
      SCHOOL_FIELDS.forEach(g => g.items.forEach(f => {
        const found = (STATE.db.Settings || []).find(s => s.key === f.k);
        o[f.k] = (found && found.value !== undefined && found.value !== null && String(found.value) !== '') ? String(found.value) : f.def;
      }));
      ['school_logo_left', 'school_logo_right'].forEach(k => {
        const f = (STATE.db.Settings || []).find(s => s.key === k);
        o[k] = f && f.value ? String(f.value) : '';
      });
      return o;
    }
    function siPlaceDate() {
      const idn = getSchoolIdentity();
      const d = formatIndoDate(idn.report_date) || new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      return `${idn.report_place}, ${d}`;
    }

    function renderSchoolIdentityModule() {
      const idn = getSchoolIdentity();
      window.__siLogos = { left: idn.school_logo_left || '', right: idn.school_logo_right || '' };
      const inputCls = 'w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500';
      const field = f => {
        const val = siEsc(idn[f.k]);
        let ctl;
        if (f.t === 'select') {
          ctl = `<select id="si_${f.k}" oninput="refreshIdentityPreview()" class="${inputCls}">${f.opts.map(o => `<option value="${siEsc(o)}" ${o === idn[f.k] ? 'selected' : ''}>${o === '' ? '— Pilih —' : siEsc(o)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
        } else {
          ctl = `<input id="si_${f.k}" type="${f.t}" value="${val}" ${f.ph ? `placeholder="${siEsc(f.ph)}"` : ''} ${f.max ? `maxlength="${f.max}"` : ''} ${f.t === 'number' ? 'min="1" max="120"' : ''} oninput="refreshIdentityPreview()" class="${inputCls}">`;
        }
        return `
          <div class="${f.span === 2 ? 'sm:col-span-2' : ''}">
            <label class="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">${f.l}${f.req ? ' <span class="text-rose-600">*</span>' : ''}</label>
            ${ctl}
            ${f.help ? `<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">${f.help}</p>` : ''}
          </div>`;
      };

      return `
        <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-5">
          <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-700 to-teal-700 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-md flex items-center gap-4">
            <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-white/15 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><i data-lucide="landmark" class="w-6 h-6"></i></div>
            <div>
              <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-200">Pengaturan • Khusus Admin</span>
              <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-0.5">Identitas Sekolah</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-indigo-100 mt-1">Data di sini dipakai otomatis pada kop surat, raport, tanda tangan kepala sekolah, halaman login, dan header aplikasi.</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          ${SCHOOL_FIELDS.map(g => `
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4">
              <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="${g.icon}" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i> ${g.g}</h3>
              <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">${g.items.map(field).join('')}</div>
              ${g.g === 'Tahun Ajaran & Dokumen' ? `<div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-xs text-amber-800">Mengubah tahun ajaran / semester di sini <strong>hanya mengganti label & nilai bawaan</strong>, data lama tidak dihapus. Untuk memulai tahun pelajaran baru dengan data kosong, gunakan menu <button type="button" onclick="navigateMenu('reset_data')" class="font-extrabold underline">Reset Data (Tahun Baru)</button>.</div>` : ''}
            </div>`).join('')}

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="image" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i> Logo Kop Surat</h3>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
              ${['left', 'right'].map(side => `
                <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div id="siLogo_${side}" class="w-20 h-20 shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">${logoBoxHtml(window.__siLogos[side])}</div>
                  <div class="space-y-2">
                    <p class="font-bold text-slate-700">Logo ${side === 'left' ? 'Kiri' : 'Kanan'}</p>
                    <input type="file" accept="image/*" onchange="handleLogoUpload('${side}', this)" class="text-[11px] block max-w-full">
                    <button type="button" onclick="removeLogo('${side}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus logo</button>
                  </div>
                </div>`).join('')}
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Gambar diperkecil otomatis (±220 px). Gunakan PNG/JPG berbentuk persegi. Klik "Simpan Identitas Sekolah" agar logo tersimpan dan tampil di semua kop surat.</p>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-3">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i> Pratinjau Kop Surat & Tanda Tangan</h3>
            <div id="siPreview" class="border border-dashed border-slate-300 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white text-slate-900 overflow-x-auto"></div>
          </div>

          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
            <button onclick="navigateMenu('school_identity')" class="sm:w-48 py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 font-bold text-sm">Batalkan Perubahan</button>
            <button onclick="saveSchoolIdentity()" class="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-extrabold text-sm shadow-md">Simpan Identitas Sekolah</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function collectIdentityForm() {
      const o = {};
      SCHOOL_FIELDS.forEach(g => g.items.forEach(f => {
        const el = document.getElementById('si_' + f.k);
        o[f.k] = el ? el.value.trim() : '';
      }));
      o.school_logo_left = (window.__siLogos || {}).left || '';
      o.school_logo_right = (window.__siLogos || {}).right || '';
      return o;
    }

    function refreshIdentityPreview() {
      const box = document.getElementById('siPreview');
      if (!box) return;
      const v = collectIdentityForm();
      const d = formatIndoDate(v.report_date) || '…';
      box.innerHTML = renderOfficialLetterhead(v) + `
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;font-size:10pt;">
          <div style="text-align:center;width:260px;">
            <p>${siEsc(v.report_place)}, ${siEsc(d)}</p>
            <p>Kepala ${siNameCase(v.school_name)},</p>
            <div style="height:40px;"></div>
            <p style="font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;">${siEsc(v.headmaster_name)}</p>
            <p>NIP. ${siEsc(v.headmaster_nip || '-')}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p style="font-size:9pt;margin-top:8px;color:#64748b;">Tahun Ajaran ${siEsc(v.academic_year)} • Semester ${siEsc(v.active_semester)} • ${siEsc(v.curriculum_name)}</p>`;
    }

    function validateSchoolIdentity(v) {
      if (!v.school_name) return 'Nama sekolah wajib diisi.';
      if (!v.headmaster_name) return 'Nama kepala sekolah wajib diisi.';
      if (v.school_npsn && !/^\d{8}$/.test(v.school_npsn)) return 'NPSN harus 8 digit angka.';
      if (v.school_postal_code && !/^\d{5}$/.test(v.school_postal_code)) return 'Kode pos harus 5 digit angka.';
      if (v.school_email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v.school_email)) return 'Format email tidak valid.';
      const m = /^(\d{4})\/(\d{4})$/.exec(v.academic_year);
      if (!m || Number(m[2]) !== Number(m[1]) + 1) return 'Tahun ajaran harus berformat 2026/2027 (tahun kedua = tahun pertama + 1).';
      const jp = Number(v.jp_duration_minutes);
      if (!(jp >= 1 && jp <= 120) || Math.floor(jp) !== jp) return 'Durasi 1 JP harus angka bulat 1–120 menit.';
      return '';
    }

    function saveSchoolIdentity() {
      const v = collectIdentityForm();
      const err = validateSchoolIdentity(v);
      if (err) { showToast(err, 'warning'); return; }
      const cur = getSchoolIdentity();
      if (v.academic_year !== cur.academic_year || v.active_semester !== cur.active_semester) {
        window.__siPending = v;
        openModal('Konfirmasi Perubahan Tahun Ajaran / Semester',
          `<p class="text-sm">Tahun ajaran/semester aktif akan berubah dari <strong>${siEsc(cur.academic_year)} (${siEsc(cur.active_semester)})</strong> menjadi <strong>${siEsc(v.academic_year)} (${siEsc(v.active_semester)})</strong>. Data nilai dan absensi yang sudah ada <strong>tidak</strong> ikut berubah atau terhapus. Lanjutkan?</p>`,
          `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold">Batal</button><button onclick="closeModal(); persistSchoolIdentity(window.__siPending)" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-sm font-bold">Ya, Simpan</button>`, 'max-w-lg');
        return;
      }
      persistSchoolIdentity(v);
    }

    async function persistSchoolIdentity(v) {
      showLoader('Menyimpan identitas sekolah...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        const res = await callServer('saveSchoolSettings', v, actor);
        if (res && res.success) {
          await loadAppData(true);
          showToast(res.changed && res.changed.length ? 'Identitas sekolah berhasil disimpan!' : 'Tidak ada perubahan untuk disimpan.', 'success');
        }
      } catch (e) {
        showToast('Gagal menyimpan: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e), 'error');
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }

    // Isi pratinjau setelah halaman Identitas Sekolah tampil
    (function () {
      const _ra = renderApp;
      renderApp = function () {
        _ra.apply(this, arguments);
        if (STATE.user && STATE.activeMenu === 'school_identity') { try { refreshIdentityPreview(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); } }
      };
    })();

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — HELPER UMUM (waktu, jadwal, SVG chart)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    const DAY_NAMES = ['Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu'];
    const ABS_STATUS = {
      H: { l: 'Hadir', c: '#16a34a' },
      S: { l: 'Sakit', c: '#f59e0b' },
      I: { l: 'Izin', c: '#3b82f6' },
      A: { l: 'Alpa', c: '#ef4444' }
    };

    function normTime(v) {
      const raw = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim();
      let m = /^(\d{1,2})[:.](\d{2})/.exec(raw);
      if (!m) m = /1899-12-30[T ](\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/i.exec(raw);
      return m ? ('0' + m[1]).slice(-2) + ':' + m[2] : '';
    }
    function addMin(t, mins) {
      const n = normTime(t);
      if (!n) return '';
      const p = n.split(':').map(Number);
      const x = p[0] * 60 + p[1] + Number(mins || 0);
      return ('0' + (Math.floor(x / 60) % 24)).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + (x % 60)).slice(-2);
    }
    function getJpDuration() { return Number(getSettingVal('jp_duration_minutes', '40')) || 40; }
    function getSlots() { return [...(STATE.db.JamPelajaran || [])].sort((a, b) => Number(a.slot_no) - Number(b.slot_no)); }
    function localISO(d) {
      d = d || new Date();
      return d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2);
    }
    function todayDayName() { return ['Minggu', 'Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu'][new Date().getDay()]; }
    function userName(id) { const u = (STATE.db.UsersPublic || []).find(x => x.id === id); return u ? u.full_name : (id || '-'); }
    function className(id) { const c = (STATE.db.Classes || []).find(x => x.id === id); return c ? c.name : (id || '-'); }
    function subjectName(id) { const s = (STATE.db.Subjects || []).find(x => x.id === id); return s ? s.name : (id || '-'); }
    function sortedClasses() {
      return [...(STATE.db.Classes || [])].sort((a, b) => String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name), 'id', { numeric: true }));
    }
    function activeStudentsOf(classId) {
      return (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => s.class_id === classId && String(s.status || 'Aktif') === 'Aktif')
        .sort((a, b) => String(a.full_name).localeCompare(String(b.full_name), 'id'));
    }

    /** Gabungkan jam-jam berurutan (kelas+mapel+guru sama) menjadi satu blok mengajar. */
    function buildScheduleBlocks(rows) {
      const slotMap = {};
      getSlots().forEach(s => { slotMap[Number(s.slot_no)] = s; });
      const dur = getJpDuration();
      const sorted = [...rows].sort((a, b) =>
        DAY_NAMES.indexOf(a.day_name) - DAY_NAMES.indexOf(b.day_name) ||
        String(a.class_id).localeCompare(String(b.class_id)) || Number(a.slot_no) - Number(b.slot_no));
      const out = [];
      sorted.forEach(r => {
        const last = out[out.length - 1];
        if (last && last.day_name === r.day_name && last.class_id === r.class_id && last.subject_id === r.subject_id &&
            last.teacher_id === r.teacher_id && last.endSlot + 1 === Number(r.slot_no)) {
          last.endSlot = Number(r.slot_no); last.jp++;
        } else {
          out.push({ day_name: r.day_name, class_id: r.class_id, subject_id: r.subject_id, teacher_id: r.teacher_id,
            startSlot: Number(r.slot_no), endSlot: Number(r.slot_no), jp: 1 });
        }
      });
      out.forEach(b => {
        const s = slotMap[b.startSlot], e = slotMap[b.endSlot];
        b.start = s ? normTime(s.start_time) : '';
        b.end = e ? addMin(e.start_time, dur) : '';
      });
      return out.sort((a, b) => DAY_NAMES.indexOf(a.day_name) - DAY_NAMES.indexOf(b.day_name) || a.startSlot - b.startSlot);
    }
    function getTeacherAdminBlocks(tid) {
      return buildScheduleBlocks((STATE.db.JadwalKelas || []).filter(r => r.teacher_id === tid));
    }
    function adminBlocksJp(tid) { return getTeacherAdminBlocks(tid).reduce((a, b) => a + b.jp, 0); }

    function renderTeacherAdminScheduleCard() {
      const blocks = getTeacherAdminBlocks(STATE.user.id);
      const today = todayDayName();
      return `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border-2 border-indigo-100 overflow-hidden">
          <div class="px-5 py-3 bg-indigo-50 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-indigo-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="calendar-check" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Jadwal Resmi dari Admin / Waka Kurikulum</h3>
            <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-700">${blocks.reduce((a, b) => a + b.jp, 0)} JP / minggu</span>
          </div>
          ${blocks.length ? `
          <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
            <thead class="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700"><tr><th class="p-3">Hari</th><th class="p-3">Jam ke</th><th class="p-3">Jam Mulai</th><th class="p-3">Jam Selesai</th><th class="p-3">Kelas</th><th class="p-3">Mata Pelajaran</th><th class="p-3 text-center">JP</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody class="divide-y">${blocks.map(b => `
              <tr class="${b.day_name === today ? 'bg-amber-50' : ''}">
                <td class="p-3 font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${b.day_name}${b.day_name === today ? ' <span class="text-[10px] bg-amber-200 text-amber-900 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">Hari ini</span>' : ''}</td>
                <td class="p-3">${b.startSlot === b.endSlot ? b.startSlot : b.startSlot + '–' + b.endSlot}</td>
                <td class="p-3 font-bold">${b.start || '-'} WITA</td>
                <td class="p-3 font-bold">${b.end || '-'} WITA</td>
                <td class="p-3 font-bold">Kelas ${siEsc(className(b.class_id))}</td>
                <td class="p-3">${siEsc(subjectName(b.subject_id))}</td>
                <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${b.jp} JP</td>
              </tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`
          : `<p class="p-5 text-xs text-slate-500">Admin belum menyusun jadwal untuk Anda. Jadwal akan otomatis muncul di sini setelah disusun berdasarkan penugasan mengajar Anda.</p>`}
        </div>`;
    }

    function renderGuruTodayCard() {
      const blocks = getTeacherAdminBlocks(STATE.user.id);
      if (!blocks.length) return '';
      const today = todayDayName();
      const todays = blocks.filter(b => b.day_name === today);
      return `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border-2 border-indigo-100 p-5">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="bell-ring" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i> Jadwal Mengajar Hari Ini (${today})</h3>
            <button onclick="navigateMenu('jadwal_mengajar')" class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700 hover:underline">Lihat jadwal lengkap</button>
          </div>
          <div class="mt-3 space-y-2">
            ${todays.length ? todays.map(b => `
              <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
                <div><p class="font-extrabold text-slate-900">Kelas ${siEsc(className(b.class_id))} — ${siEsc(subjectName(b.subject_id))}</p>
                <p class="text-slate-500">Jam ke-${b.startSlot === b.endSlot ? b.startSlot : b.startSlot + '–' + b.endSlot} • ${b.jp} JP</p></div>
                <div class="text-right font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${b.start || '-'} – ${b.end || '-'}</div>
              </div>`).join('') : `<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Tidak ada jam mengajar hari ini.</p>`}
          </div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-3">Jadwal disusun Admin sesuai penugasan mengajar Anda • ${blocks.length} sesi per minggu.</p>
        </div>`;
    }

    /* ---------- SVG: batang berkelompok & radar ---------- */
    function svgGroupedBars(labels, series, o) {
      o = o || {};
      const W = 640, H = o.h || 250, pl = 36, pr = 10, pt = 14, pb = 42;
      const iw = W - pl - pr, ih = H - pt - pb;
      let maxV = o.max || Math.max(1, ...series.flatMap(s => s.values.map(v => Number(v) || 0)));
      if (!o.max) maxV = Math.max(5, Math.ceil(maxV / 5) * 5);
      let g = '';
      for (let i = 0; i <= 5; i++) {
        const v = maxV * i / 5, y = pt + ih - (v / maxV) * ih;
        g += `<line x1="${pl}" y1="${y}" x2="${W - pr}" y2="${y}" stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="1"/><text x="${pl - 5}" y="${y + 3}" font-size="9" fill="#64748b" text-anchor="end">${Math.round(v)}</text>`;
      }
      const gw = iw / Math.max(1, labels.length);
      const bw = Math.min(28, (gw * 0.8) / series.length);
      labels.forEach((lb, gi) => {
        const x0 = pl + gi * gw + (gw - bw * series.length) / 2;
        series.forEach((s, si) => {
          const v = Number(s.values[gi]) || 0, h = (v / maxV) * ih, x = x0 + si * bw, y = pt + ih - h;
          g += `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${Math.max(1, bw - 1)}" height="${h}" rx="2" fill="${s.color}"/>`;
          if (o.showValues && v > 0 && bw >= 12) g += `<text x="${x + bw / 2}" y="${y - 2}" font-size="8" fill="#334155" text-anchor="middle">${Math.round(v * 10) / 10}</text>`;
        });
        const t = String(lb).length > 10 ? String(lb).slice(0, 9) + '…' : String(lb);
        g += `<text x="${pl + gi * gw + gw / 2}" y="${H - pb + 14}" font-size="9" font-weight="700" fill="#334155" text-anchor="middle">${siEsc(t)}</text>`;
      });
      const legend = `<div class="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 mt-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600">${series.map(s => `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"><i style="display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:2px;background:${s.color}"></i>${siEsc(s.name)}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
      return `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" class="w-full h-auto" role="img">${g}</svg>${legend}`;
    }

    function svgRadar(labels, series, max) {
      const n = labels.length;
      if (n < 3) return '';
      const S = 320, cx = S / 2, cy = S / 2, R = 92;
      const pt = (i, v) => { const a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * 2 * Math.PI / n, r = R * (Math.min(v, max) / max); return [cx + r * Math.cos(a), cy + r * Math.sin(a)]; };
      let g = '';
      for (let lv = 1; lv <= max; lv++) {
        g += `<polygon points="${labels.map((_, i) => pt(i, lv).join(',')).join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="#cbd5e1" stroke-width="1"/>`;
      }
      labels.forEach((lb, i) => {
        const e = pt(i, max), a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * 2 * Math.PI / n;
        g += `<line x1="${cx}" y1="${cy}" x2="${e[0]}" y2="${e[1]}" stroke="#cbd5e1" stroke-width="1"/>`;
        const lx = cx + (R + 16) * Math.cos(a), ly = cy + (R + 16) * Math.sin(a);
        const anc = Math.cos(a) > 0.3 ? 'start' : Math.cos(a) < -0.3 ? 'end' : 'middle';
        const t = String(lb).length > 15 ? String(lb).slice(0, 14) + '…' : String(lb);
        g += `<text x="${lx}" y="${ly + 3}" font-size="9" font-weight="700" fill="#334155" text-anchor="${anc}">${siEsc(t)}</text>`;
      });
      series.forEach(s => {
        const pts = s.values.map((v, i) => pt(i, Number(v) || 0).join(',')).join(' ');
        g += `<polygon points="${pts}" fill="${s.dashed ? 'none' : s.color}" fill-opacity="0.28" stroke="${s.color}" stroke-width="1.6" ${s.dashed ? 'stroke-dasharray="4 3"' : ''}/>`;
      });
      const legend = `<div class="flex justify-center gap-4 mt-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600">${series.map(s => `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"><i style="display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:2px;background:${s.color};opacity:${s.dashed ? 0.5 : 1}"></i>${siEsc(s.name)}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
      return `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${S} ${S}" class="w-full max-w-[320px] mx-auto h-auto" role="img">${g}</svg>${legend}`;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — ADMIN: ATUR JADWAL PELAJARAN (grid kelas × jam ke-)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderJadwalAdminModule() {
      if (!STATE.jadwalDay) STATE.jadwalDay = 'Senin';
      const day = STATE.jadwalDay;
      const slots = getSlots();
      const classes = sortedClasses();
      const dur = getJpDuration();
      const tcs = STATE.db.TeacherClasses || [];
      window.__slotDraft = slots.map(s => ({ start_time: normTime(s.start_time), label: s.label || '' }));

      const cellHtml = (slotNo, c) => {
        const cur = (STATE.db.JadwalKelas || []).find(r => r.day_name === day && Number(r.slot_no) === Number(slotNo) && r.class_id === c.id);
        const seen = {};
        const opts = tcs.filter(t => t.class_id === c.id && !seen[t.subject_id] && (seen[t.subject_id] = true));
        if (!opts.length) return `<span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Belum ada penugasan mengajar</span>`;
        return `<select data-prev="${cur ? cur.subject_id : ''}" onchange="saveJadwalCellUI('${day}', ${slotNo}, '${c.id}', this)"
          class="w-full min-w-[150px] px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-[11px] font-semibold ${cur ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-300' : 'bg-white'}">
          <option value="">—</option>
          ${opts.map(t => `<option value="${t.subject_id}" ${cur && cur.subject_id === t.subject_id ? 'selected' : ''}>${siEsc(subjectName(t.subject_id))} — ${siEsc(userName(t.teacher_id))}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>`;
      };

      // beban JP per guru (seluruh minggu)
      const load = {};
      (STATE.db.JadwalKelas || []).forEach(r => { load[r.teacher_id] = (load[r.teacher_id] || 0) + 1; });

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-700 to-teal-700 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-md">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-200">Admin • Waka Kurikulum</span>
            <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1">Atur Jadwal Pelajaran</h2>
            <p class="text-xs text-indigo-100 mt-1">Pilih mata pelajaran pada tiap sel. Guru pengampu terisi otomatis dari Penugasan Mengajar, dan jadwal langsung tampil di beranda guru serta panel wali murid.</p>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-3">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="clock" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i> Jam Pelajaran (berlaku untuk semua hari)</h3>
              <span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Jam selesai otomatis = jam mulai + ${dur} menit (durasi 1 JP)</span>
            </div>
            <div id="slotEditor">${slotEditorHtml()}</div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button onclick="addSlotRow()" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Jam ke-</button>
              <button onclick="saveSlotsUI()" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold">Simpan Jam Pelajaran</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="flex gap-2 bg-white p-1.5 rounded-2xl border w-fit text-xs font-bold overflow-x-auto max-w-full">
            ${DAY_NAMES.map(d => `<button onclick="STATE.jadwalDay='${d}'; renderApp();" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl whitespace-nowrap ${d === day ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}">${d}</button>`).join('')}
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full text-xs text-left border-collapse">
                <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white">
                  <tr><th class="p-3 sticky left-0 bg-slate-800 min-w-[110px]">Jam ke-</th>${classes.map(c => `<th class="p-3 text-center">Kelas ${siEsc(c.name)}</th>`).join('')}</tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${slots.map(s => `
                    <tr>
                      <td class="p-3 sticky left-0 bg-slate-50 font-extrabold">Jam ${s.slot_no}<div class="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500">${normTime(s.start_time)} – ${addMin(s.start_time, dur)}${s.label ? ' • ' + siEsc(s.label) : ''}</div></td>
                      ${classes.map(c => `<td class="p-2 border-l border-slate-100">${cellHtml(s.slot_no, c)}</td>`).join('')}
                    </tr>`).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm mb-3">Beban Mengajar Guru (JP per minggu dari jadwal ini)</h3>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs">
              ${Object.keys(load).length ? Object.keys(load).map(t => `<div class="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border flex justify-between"><span class="font-semibold">${siEsc(userName(t))}</span><span class="font-extrabold text-indigo-700">${load[t]} JP</span></div>`).join('') : '<p class="text-slate-500">Belum ada jadwal yang diisi.</p>'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function slotEditorHtml() {
      const dur = getJpDuration();
      const d = window.__slotDraft || [];
      return `<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">${d.map((s, i) => `
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50">
          <span class="font-extrabold w-14">Jam ${i + 1}</span>
          <input type="time" value="${s.start_time}" oninput="__slotDraft[${i}].start_time=this.value; document.getElementById('slotEnd${i}').textContent=addMin(this.value, ${dur}) || '-'" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border font-bold">
          <span class="text-slate-500">s/d <strong id="slotEnd${i}" class="text-slate-800">${addMin(s.start_time, dur) || '-'}</strong></span>
          <input type="text" value="${siEsc(s.label)}" placeholder="Ket. (opsional)" oninput="__slotDraft[${i}].label=this.value" class="flex-1 min-w-0 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border">
          ${i === d.length - 1 && d.length > 1 ? `<button onclick="removeSlotRow()" class="text-rose-600 font-bold px-1" title="Hapus jam terakhir">✕</button>` : ''}
        </div>`).join('')}</div>`;
    }
    function addSlotRow() {
      const d = window.__slotDraft || [];
      const last = d[d.length - 1];
      d.push({ start_time: last ? addMin(last.start_time, getJpDuration()) : '07:30', label: '' });
      document.getElementById('slotEditor').innerHTML = slotEditorHtml();
    }
    function removeSlotRow() {
      const d = window.__slotDraft || [];
      if (d.length > 1) d.pop();
      document.getElementById('slotEditor').innerHTML = slotEditorHtml();
    }
    async function saveSlotsUI() {
      const d = window.__slotDraft || [];
      if (d.some(s => !normTime(s.start_time))) { showToast('Semua jam mulai harus diisi.', 'warning'); return; }
      showLoader('Menyimpan jam pelajaran...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        await callServer('saveJamPelajaran', d.map((s, i) => ({ slot_no: i + 1, start_time: normTime(s.start_time), label: s.label })), actor);
        await loadAppData(true);
        showToast('Jam pelajaran disimpan!', 'success');
      } catch (e) { showToast('Gagal menyimpan: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e), 'error'); }
      finally { hideLoader(); }
    }
    async function saveJadwalCellUI(day, slotNo, classId, el) {
      const subjectId = el.value, prev = el.getAttribute('data-prev') || '';
      el.disabled = true;
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        const res = await callServer('saveJadwalCell', day, slotNo, classId, subjectId, actor);
        STATE.db.JadwalKelas = (STATE.db.JadwalKelas || []).filter(r => !(r.day_name === day && Number(r.slot_no) === Number(slotNo) && r.class_id === classId));
        if (subjectId) STATE.db.JadwalKelas.push({ id: 'tmp-' + Date.now(), day_name: day, slot_no: slotNo, class_id: classId, subject_id: subjectId, teacher_id: res.teacher_id });
        el.setAttribute('data-prev', subjectId);
        el.classList.remove('bg-indigo-50', 'border-indigo-300', 'bg-white');
        (subjectId ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-300' : 'bg-white').split(' ').forEach(c => el.classList.add(c));
        showToast(subjectId ? 'Jadwal tersimpan & diteruskan ke guru terkait.' : 'Jam dikosongkan.', 'success');
      } catch (e) {
        el.value = prev;
        showToast(e && e.message ? e.message : String(e), 'error');
      } finally { el.disabled = false; }
    }
    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — ABSENSI SISWA (Wali Kelas input; Admin & Kepsek rekap)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function absCounts(rows) {
      const o = { H: 0, S: 0, I: 0, A: 0 };
      rows.forEach(r => { if (o[r.status] !== undefined) o[r.status]++; });
      return o;
    }
    function monthLabel(ym) {
      const bln = ['Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni', 'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'];
      const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})$/.exec(ym || '');
      return m ? bln[Number(m[2]) - 1] + ' ' + m[1] : '';
    }
    function absBtnClass(st, active) {
      const col = { H: 'bg-emerald-600', S: 'bg-amber-500', I: 'bg-blue-600', A: 'bg-rose-600' }[st];
      return 'w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold border transition ' + (active ? col + ' text-white border-transparent' : 'bg-white text-slate-500 border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100');
    }

    function renderAbsensiWali() {
      const cls = getActiveWaliClass();
      const students = activeStudentsOf(cls.id);
      if (!STATE.absDate) STATE.absDate = localISO();
      const date = STATE.absDate;
      const existing = (STATE.db.AbsensiHarian || []).filter(a => a.class_id === cls.id && String(a.absen_date) === date);
      window.__absDraft = {};
      students.forEach(s => {
        const e = existing.find(x => x.student_id === s.id);
        window.__absDraft[s.id] = { status: e ? e.status : 'H', note: e ? (e.note || '') : '' };
      });
      const month = date.slice(0, 7);
      const monthRows = (STATE.db.AbsensiHarian || []).filter(a => a.class_id === cls.id && String(a.absen_date).startsWith(month));

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-700 to-teal-700 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-md">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-200">Panel Wali Kelas</span>
            <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1">Absensi Siswa — Kelas ${siEsc(cls.name)}</h2>
            <p class="text-xs text-emerald-100 mt-1">Data absensi otomatis terekap di menu Absensi Siswa milik Admin & Kepala Sekolah.</p>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 space-y-4">
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Tanggal Absensi</label>
                <input type="date" value="${date}" max="${localISO()}" onchange="STATE.absDate=this.value || localISO(); renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-bold text-sm">
              </div>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button onclick="absSetAll('H')" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 text-xs font-bold">Semua Hadir</button>
                <button onclick="saveAbsensiWali('${cls.id}')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-extrabold">Simpan Absensi</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-3 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600">
              ${Object.keys(ABS_STATUS).map(k => `<span><strong>${k}</strong> = ${ABS_STATUS[k].l}</span>`).join('')}
              ${existing.length ? `<span class="text-emerald-700">• Absensi tanggal ini sudah tersimpan (${existing.length} siswa), Anda dapat mengubahnya.</span>` : ''}
            </div>
            <div class="divide-y">
              ${students.map((s, i) => `
                <div class="py-2.5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4 text-xs">
                  <div class="sm:w-64 font-bold text-slate-800">${i + 1}. ${siEsc(s.full_name)} <span class="text-slate-400 font-normal">(${siEsc(s.gender)})</span></div>
                  <div class="flex gap-1.5">${['H', 'S', 'I', 'A'].map(st => `<button id="abs_${s.id}_${st}" onclick="setAbsStatus('${s.id}','${st}')" class="${absBtnClass(st, window.__absDraft[s.id].status === st)}">${st}</button>`).join('')}</div>
                  <input type="text" value="${siEsc(window.__absDraft[s.id].note)}" placeholder="Keterangan (opsional)" oninput="__absDraft['${s.id}'].note=this.value" class="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs">
                </div>`).join('') || '<p class="py-6 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs">Belum ada siswa aktif di kelas ini.</p>'}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="px-5 py-3 bg-slate-50 font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-800">Rekap Kehadiran Bulan ${monthLabel(month)}</div>
            <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
              <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Nama Siswa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Hadir</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Sakit</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Izin</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Alpa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">% Hadir</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody class="divide-y">${students.map(s => {
                const c = absCounts(monthRows.filter(r => r.student_id === s.id));
                const tot = c.H + c.S + c.I + c.A;
                return `<tr><td class="p-3 font-bold">${siEsc(s.full_name)}</td><td class="p-3 text-center text-emerald-700 font-bold">${c.H}</td><td class="p-3 text-center text-amber-600 font-bold">${c.S}</td><td class="p-3 text-center text-blue-600 font-bold">${c.I}</td><td class="p-3 text-center text-rose-600 font-bold">${c.A}</td><td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold">${tot ? Math.round(c.H / tot * 100) + '%' : '-'}</td></tr>`;
              }).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }
    function setAbsStatus(id, st) {
      if (!window.__absDraft || !window.__absDraft[id]) return;
      window.__absDraft[id].status = st;
      ['H', 'S', 'I', 'A'].forEach(k => { const b = document.getElementById('abs_' + id + '_' + k); if (b) b.className = absBtnClass(k, k === st); });
    }
    function absSetAll(st) { Object.keys(window.__absDraft || {}).forEach(id => setAbsStatus(id, st)); }
    async function saveAbsensiWali(classId) {
      const ids = Object.keys(window.__absDraft || {});
      if (!ids.length) { showToast('Tidak ada siswa untuk disimpan.', 'warning'); return; }
      showLoader('Menyimpan absensi...');
      try {
        const actor = { id: STATE.user.id, full_name: STATE.user.full_name, activeRole: STATE.activeRole };
        const records = ids.map(id => ({ student_id: id, status: window.__absDraft[id].status, note: window.__absDraft[id].note }));
        await callServer('saveAbsensiHarian', STATE.absDate, classId, records, actor);
        await loadAppData(true);
        showToast('Absensi tanggal ' + formatIndoDate(STATE.absDate) + ' tersimpan!', 'success');
      } catch (e) { showToast('Gagal menyimpan: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e), 'error'); }
      finally { hideLoader(); }
    }

    /** Rekap absensi semua kelas (Admin & Kepala Sekolah) */
    function absPerClass(month) {
      const rows = (STATE.db.AbsensiHarian || []).filter(a => String(a.absen_date).startsWith(month));
      return sortedClasses().map(c => {
        const cr = rows.filter(r => r.class_id === c.id);
        const cnt = absCounts(cr);
        const tot = cnt.H + cnt.S + cnt.I + cnt.A;
        return { cls: c, cnt, tot, days: new Set(cr.map(r => r.absen_date)).size, pct: tot ? Math.round(cnt.H / tot * 100) : null, students: activeStudentsOf(c.id).length };
      });
    }
    function renderAbsensiRekapModule() {
      const month = STATE.absMonth || localISO().slice(0, 7);
      const per = absPerClass(month);
      const total = { H: 0, S: 0, I: 0, A: 0 };
      per.forEach(p => Object.keys(total).forEach(k => { total[k] += p.cnt[k]; }));
      const grand = total.H + total.S + total.I + total.A;
      const sel = STATE.absClass && per.find(p => p.cls.id === STATE.absClass);
      const exportData = [['Kelas', 'Wali Kelas', 'Jumlah Siswa', 'Hari Terisi', 'Hadir', 'Sakit', 'Izin', 'Alpa', '% Hadir'],
        ...per.map(p => [p.cls.name, userName(p.cls.wali_kelas_id), p.students, p.days, p.cnt.H, p.cnt.S, p.cnt.I, p.cnt.A, p.pct === null ? '-' : p.pct + '%'])];
      window.__absExport = exportData;

      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Absensi Siswa — Rekap Seluruh Kelas</h2>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Sumber data: absensi harian yang diinput para Wali Kelas • Bulan ${monthLabel(month)}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <input type="month" value="${month}" onchange="STATE.absMonth=this.value || localISO().slice(0,7); renderApp();" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold">
              <button onclick="copyTableTsv(window.__absExport)" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">Salin TSV</button>
              <button onclick="exportTableToExcel(window.__absExport, 'Rekap_Absensi', 'Rekap_Absensi_${month}.xlsx')" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Excel .xlsx</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
            ${Object.keys(ABS_STATUS).map(k => `<div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border" style="border-top:4px solid ${ABS_STATUS[k].c}"><p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">${ABS_STATUS[k].l}</p><p class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1">${total[k]}</p><p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">${grand ? Math.round(total[k] / grand * 100) : 0}% dari seluruh catatan</p></div>`).join('')}
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-800 mb-2">Grafik Kehadiran per Kelas</h3>
            ${grand ? svgGroupedBars(per.map(p => p.cls.name), ['H', 'S', 'I', 'A'].map(k => ({ name: ABS_STATUS[k].l, color: ABS_STATUS[k].c, values: per.map(p => p.cnt[k]) })), { showValues: true }) : '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 py-6 text-center">Belum ada data absensi pada bulan ini.</p>'}
          </div>

          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
              <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Kelas</th><th class="p-3">Wali Kelas</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Siswa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Hari Terisi</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Hadir</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Sakit</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Izin</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Alpa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">% Hadir</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody class="divide-y">${per.map(p => `
                <tr onclick="STATE.absClass='${p.cls.id}'; renderApp();" class="cursor-pointer hover:bg-indigo-50 ${sel && sel.cls.id === p.cls.id ? 'bg-indigo-50' : ''}">
                  <td class="p-3 font-extrabold text-indigo-700">Kelas ${siEsc(p.cls.name)}</td>
                  <td class="p-3">${siEsc(userName(p.cls.wali_kelas_id))}</td>
                  <td class="p-3 text-center">${p.students}</td>
                  <td class="p-3 text-center">${p.days}</td>
                  <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold text-emerald-700">${p.cnt.H}</td>
                  <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold text-amber-600">${p.cnt.S}</td>
                  <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold text-blue-600">${p.cnt.I}</td>
                  <td class="p-3 text-center font-bold text-rose-600">${p.cnt.A}</td>
                  <td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold">${p.pct === null ? '-' : p.pct + '%'}</td>
                </tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
            <p class="px-5 py-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400 bg-slate-50">Klik baris kelas untuk melihat rincian per siswa.</p>
          </div>

          ${sel ? (() => {
            const rows = (STATE.db.AbsensiHarian || []).filter(a => a.class_id === sel.cls.id && String(a.absen_date).startsWith(month));
            return `<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
              <div class="px-5 py-3 bg-slate-50 font-extrabold text-sm">Rincian Kelas ${siEsc(sel.cls.name)} — ${monthLabel(month)}</div>
              <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
                <thead class="bg-slate-700 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Nama Siswa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Hadir</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Sakit</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Izin</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Alpa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">% Hadir</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">${activeStudentsOf(sel.cls.id).map(s => {
                  const c = absCounts(rows.filter(r => r.student_id === s.id)); const t = c.H + c.S + c.I + c.A;
                  return `<tr><td class="p-3 font-bold">${siEsc(s.full_name)}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${c.H}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${c.S}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${c.I}</td><td class="p-3 text-center ${c.A ? 'text-rose-600 font-extrabold' : ''}">${c.A}</td><td class="p-3 text-center font-bold">${t ? Math.round(c.H / t * 100) + '%' : '-'}</td></tr>`;
                }).join('')}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
          })() : ''}
        </div>`;
    }

    /** Kartu diagram di beranda Kepsek/Admin: klik -> menu Absensi Siswa */
    function renderAbsensiChartCard() {
      const month = localISO().slice(0, 7);
      const per = absPerClass(month);
      const has = per.some(p => p.tot > 0);
      return `
        <div onclick="navigateMenu('absensi_siswa')" class="cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-400 transition shadow-2xs p-5">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-2">
            <div>
              <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">Kehadiran Siswa per Kelas</h3>
              <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Bulan ${monthLabel(month)} • rekap absensi dari seluruh Wali Kelas</p>
            </div>
            <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-700 flex items-center gap-1">Buka Absensi Siswa <i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></span>
          </div>
          ${has ? svgGroupedBars(per.map(p => p.cls.name), ['H', 'S', 'I', 'A'].map(k => ({ name: ABS_STATUS[k].l, color: ABS_STATUS[k].c, values: per.map(p => p.cnt[k]) })), { showValues: true, h: 230 })
                : '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 py-8 text-center">Belum ada data absensi bulan ini. Wali Kelas dapat mengisi pada menu Absensi Siswa.</p>'}
        </div>`;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — PANEL WALI KELAS: DATA KELAS & DATA SISWA
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderWkDataKelas() {
      const cls = getActiveWaliClass();
      const students = activeStudentsOf(cls.id);
      const tcs = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).filter(t => t.class_id === cls.id);
      const male = students.filter(s => s.gender === 'L').length;
      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-700 to-teal-700 text-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-md">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-200">Panel Wali Kelas</span>
            <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1">Data Kelas ${siEsc(cls.name)}</h2>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs">
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border"><p class="text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Nama Rombel</p><p class="text-lg font-extrabold mt-1">Kelas ${siEsc(cls.name)}</p></div>
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border"><p class="text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Tingkat</p><p class="text-lg font-extrabold mt-1">${siEsc(cls.grade_level || '-')}</p></div>
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border"><p class="text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">KKM</p><p class="text-lg font-extrabold mt-1">${siEsc(cls.kkm || '-')}</p></div>
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border"><p class="text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Tahun Ajaran</p><p class="text-lg font-extrabold mt-1">${siEsc(cls.academic_year || getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027'))}</p></div>
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border col-span-2"><p class="text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Wali Kelas</p><p class="text-sm font-extrabold mt-1">${siEsc(userName(cls.wali_kelas_id))}</p></div>
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border col-span-2"><p class="text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Peserta Didik Aktif</p><p class="text-sm font-extrabold mt-1">${students.length} siswa (L: ${male} • P: ${students.length - male})</p></div>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="px-5 py-3 bg-slate-50 font-extrabold text-sm">Guru Mata Pelajaran & Status Nilai</div>
            <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
              <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Mata Pelajaran</th><th class="p-3">Guru Pengampu</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Status Nilai</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody class="divide-y">${tcs.map(t => {
                const fin = isNilaiFinal(t.id);
                return `<tr><td class="p-3 font-bold">${siEsc(subjectName(t.subject_id))}</td><td class="p-3">${siEsc(userName(t.teacher_id))}</td><td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${fin ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800' : 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700'}">${fin ? 'Sudah final' : 'Belum final'}</span></td></tr>`;
              }).join('') || '<tr><td class="p-4 text-slate-500" colspan="3">Belum ada penugasan mengajar untuk kelas ini.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }

    function wkSiswaRows() {
      const cls = getActiveWaliClass();
      const q = String(STATE.wkSearch || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const list = activeStudentsOf(cls.id).filter(s => !q || String(s.full_name).toLowerCase().includes(q) || String(s.nis).includes(q) || String(s.nisn).includes(q));
      return list.map((s, i) => `<tr class="hover:bg-slate-50"><td class="p-3">${i + 1}</td><td class="p-3 font-semibold">${siEsc(s.nis)}</td><td class="p-3">${siEsc(s.nisn)}</td><td class="p-3 font-bold">${siEsc(s.full_name)}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${siEsc(s.gender)}</td><td class="p-3">${siEsc(s.parent_name || '-')}</td><td class="p-3">${siEsc(s.parent_phone || '-')}</td><td class="p-3">${siEsc(userName(s.guru_wali_id))}</td></tr>`).join('')
        || '<tr><td class="p-4 text-slate-500" colspan="8">Tidak ada siswa yang cocok.</td></tr>';
    }
    function renderWkDataSiswa() {
      const cls = getActiveWaliClass();
      return `
        <div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Data Siswa — Kelas ${siEsc(cls.name)}</h2><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Daftar peserta didik rombel Anda (data diubah oleh Admin)</p></div>
            <input type="search" value="${siEsc(STATE.wkSearch || '')}" placeholder="Cari nama / NIS / NISN..." oninput="STATE.wkSearch=this.value; document.getElementById('wkSiswaBody').innerHTML=wkSiswaRows();" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold w-full sm:w-64">
          </div>
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden"><div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
            <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">No</th><th class="p-3">NIS</th><th class="p-3">NISN</th><th class="p-3">Nama Lengkap</th><th class="p-3 text-center">L/P</th><th class="p-3">Orang Tua / Wali</th><th class="p-3">Telepon</th><th class="p-3">Guru Wali</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody id="wkSiswaBody" class="divide-y">${wkSiswaRows()}</tbody></table></div></div>
        </div>`;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — PERSETUJUAN NILAI (Guru Mapel -> Wali Kelas)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function isNilaiFinal(tcId) {
      return (STATE.db.StatusNilai || []).some(x => x.teacher_class_id === tcId && x.status === 'Final');
    }
    function getSubjectFinalState(classId, subjectId) {
      const tcs = (STATE.db.TeacherClasses || []).filter(t => t.class_id === classId && t.subject_id === subjectId);
      if (!tcs.length) return 'none';
      return tcs.every(t => isNilaiFinal(t.id)) ? 'final' : 'pending';
    }
    async function toggleNilaiFinal(tcId, classId, subjectId, makeFinal) {
      const sn = (STATE.db.StatusNilai || []).find(x => x.teacher_class_id === tcId);
      await apiSave('StatusNilai', {
        id: sn ? sn.id : undefined, teacher_class_id: tcId, class_id: classId, subject_id: subjectId,
        status: makeFinal ? 'Final' : 'Belum', approved_by: STATE.user.id,
        approved_at: new Date().toLocaleString('sv-SE').slice(0, 16)
      }, makeFinal ? 'Nilai disetujui — Wali Kelas melihat mapel ini sudah final.' : 'Persetujuan dibatalkan — Wali Kelas melihat nilai belum selesai.');
    }
    function renderNilaiFinalControl(tc) {
      const fin = isNilaiFinal(tc.id);
      return fin
        ? `<span class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center gap-1.5"><i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Nilai Final</span>
           <button onclick="toggleNilaiFinal('${tc.id}','${tc.class_id}','${tc.subject_id}',false)" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 text-xs font-bold">Batalkan Persetujuan</button>`
        : `<button onclick="toggleNilaiFinal('${tc.id}','${tc.class_id}','${tc.subject_id}',true)" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5" title="Beritahu Wali Kelas bahwa seluruh nilai mapel ini selesai diinput"><i data-lucide="check" class="w-4 h-4"></i> Setujui Nilai (Selesai)</button>`;
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — LOGO KOP SURAT (upload kiri & kanan)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function logoBoxHtml(src) {
      return src ? `<img src="${siEsc(src)}" class="max-w-full max-h-full object-contain">` : '<span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 text-center px-1">Belum ada logo</span>';
    }
    function handleLogoUpload(side, input) {
      const file = input.files && input.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      if (!/^image\//.test(file.type)) { showToast('File harus berupa gambar (PNG/JPG).', 'warning'); return; }
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = ev => {
        const img = new Image();
        img.onload = () => {
          let out = '';
          const sizes = [220, 180, 150, 120, 96];
          for (let k = 0; k < sizes.length && !out; k++) {
            const sc = Math.min(1, sizes[k] / Math.max(img.width, img.height));
            const c = document.createElement('canvas');
            c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.width * sc)); c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.height * sc));
            c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
            let d = c.toDataURL('image/png');
            if (d.length > 45000) {
              const c2 = document.createElement('canvas'); c2.width = c.width; c2.height = c.height;
              const x = c2.getContext('2d'); x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.fillRect(0, 0, c2.width, c2.height); x.drawImage(img, 0, 0, c2.width, c2.height);
              d = c2.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
            }
            if (d.length <= 45000) out = d;
          }
          if (!out) { showToast('Gambar terlalu kompleks/besar. Gunakan logo yang lebih sederhana.', 'error'); return; }
          window.__siLogos[side] = out;
          const box = document.getElementById('siLogo_' + side); if (box) box.innerHTML = logoBoxHtml(out);
          refreshIdentityPreview();
        };
        img.onerror = () => showToast('Gambar tidak dapat dibaca.', 'error');
        img.src = ev.target.result;
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
    function removeLogo(side) {
      window.__siLogos[side] = '';
      const box = document.getElementById('siLogo_' + side); if (box) box.innerHTML = logoBoxHtml('');
      refreshIdentityPreview();
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — PELANGGARAN: FILTER KELAS & PENCARIAN NAMA
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function plgFilterStudents() {
      const clsSel = document.getElementById('plgClass'), q = String(document.getElementById('plgSearch').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const sel = document.getElementById('plgStudent');
      const keep = sel.value;
      const list = (STATE.db.Students || []).filter(s => String(s.status || 'Aktif') === 'Aktif')
        .filter(s => clsSel.value === 'ALL' || s.class_id === clsSel.value)
        .filter(s => !q || String(s.full_name).toLowerCase().includes(q) || String(s.nis).includes(q))
        .sort((a, b) => String(className(a.class_id)).localeCompare(String(className(b.class_id)), 'id', { numeric: true }) || String(a.full_name).localeCompare(String(b.full_name), 'id'));
      sel.innerHTML = list.length
        ? list.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}" ${s.id === keep ? 'selected' : ''}>${siEsc(s.full_name)} (${siEsc(className(s.class_id))})</option>`).join('')
        : '<option value="">— Tidak ada siswa yang cocok —</option>';
      const info = document.getElementById('plgCount'); if (info) info.textContent = list.length + ' siswa ditemukan';
    }

    /**
     * ==========================================================================
     * v3 — BERANDA WALI MURID (Grafik Capaian Belajar)
     * ==========================================================================
     */
    function renderWmBeranda(child, cls, topBar) {
      const subjects = STATE.db.Subjects || [];
      const classmates = activeStudentsOf(cls.id);
      const kkm = Number(cls.kkm) || 75;
      const rows = subjects.map(sub => {
        const mine = getStudentSubjectReport(child.id, cls.id, sub.id).finalScore;
        const vals = classmates.map(s => getStudentSubjectReport(s.id, cls.id, sub.id).finalScore).filter(v => v !== null);
        const avg = vals.length ? vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length : null;
        return { name: sub.name, code: sub.code || sub.name, mine, avg };
      }).filter(r => r.mine !== null || r.avg !== null);
      const scored = rows.filter(r => r.mine !== null);
      const overall = scored.length ? (scored.reduce((a, b) => a + b.mine, 0) / scored.length) : null;
      const first = String(child.full_name).split(' ')[0];
      const firstCap = first.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + first.slice(1).toLowerCase();

      // sikap (radar)
      const aspects = STATE.db.AttitudeAspects || [];
      const ratings = STATE.db.AttitudeRatings || [];
      const avgOf = arr => arr.length ? arr.reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.score || 0), 0) / arr.length : 0;
      const myAtt = aspects.map(a => avgOf(ratings.filter(r => r.student_id === child.id && r.aspect_id === a.id)));
      const clsAtt = aspects.map(a => avgOf(ratings.filter(r => r.aspect_id === a.id && classmates.some(s => s.id === r.student_id))));
      const hasAtt = aspects.length >= 3 && myAtt.some(v => v > 0);

      // catatan capaian
      const best = [...scored].sort((a, b) => b.mine - a.mine).slice(0, 2).map(r => r.name);
      const low = scored.filter(r => r.mine < kkm).sort((a, b) => a.mine - b.mine).slice(0, 2).map(r => r.name);
      const catatan = scored.length
        ? `Capaian paling menonjol pada ${best.join(' dan ')}. ${low.length ? 'Perlu pendampingan lebih pada ' + low.join(' dan ') + '.' : 'Seluruh mata pelajaran telah mencapai KKM.'} Rata-rata nilai akhir ${overall.toFixed(1).replace('.', ',')}.`
        : 'Belum terdapat data nilai pada semester ini.';

      const violations = (STATE.db.PelanggaranSiswa || []).filter(v => v.student_id === child.id)
        .sort((a, b) => String(b.incident_date).localeCompare(String(a.incident_date)));
      const idn = getSchoolIdentity();
      const dl = (k, v) => `<div class="flex gap-2"><span class="w-28 shrink-0 text-slate-500">${k}</span><span class="font-bold text-slate-900">: ${v}</span></div>`;

      return `
        <div>
          ${topBar}
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-5 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
            <h2 class="text-center text-lg font-extrabold tracking-wide text-slate-900">GRAFIK CAPAIAN BELAJAR</h2>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-1.5 text-xs pb-4 border-b-2 border-slate-800">
              <div class="space-y-1.5">
                ${dl('Nama Murid', siEsc(String(child.full_name).toUpperCase()))}
                ${dl('NISN / NIS', siEsc(child.nisn) + ' / ' + siEsc(child.nis))}
                ${dl('Nama Sekolah', siEsc(siNameCase(idn.school_name)))}
                ${dl('Alamat', siEsc(idn.school_address))}
              </div>
              <div class="space-y-1.5">
                ${dl('Kelas', siEsc(cls.name))}
                ${dl('Fase', 'D')}
                ${dl('Semester', (getSettingVal('active_semester', 'Ganjil') === 'Genap' ? '2 (Genap)' : '1 (Ganjil)'))}
                ${dl('Tahun Pelajaran', siEsc(getSettingVal('academic_year', '2026/2027')))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="border border-slate-300 rounded-xl p-4">
              <p class="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-800 mb-2">Nilai akhir dibanding rata-rata kelas</p>
              ${rows.length ? svgGroupedBars(rows.map(r => r.code), [
                { name: 'Nilai ' + firstCap, color: '#1d4ed8', values: rows.map(r => r.mine || 0) },
                { name: 'Rata-rata kelas', color: '#c0c7d3', values: rows.map(r => r.avg ? Math.round(r.avg * 10) / 10 : 0) }
              ], { max: 100, h: 240 }) : '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 py-6 text-center">Belum ada data nilai.</p>'}
            </div>

            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div class="border border-slate-300 rounded-xl p-4">
                <p class="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-800 mb-2">Profil capaian sikap</p>
                ${hasAtt ? svgRadar(aspects.map(a => a.name), [
                  { name: firstCap, color: '#2563eb', values: myAtt },
                  { name: 'Kelas', color: '#64748b', values: clsAtt, dashed: true }
                ], 5) : '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 py-10 text-center">Belum ada nilai sikap dari guru mata pelajaran.</p>'}
              </div>
              <div class="border border-slate-300 rounded-xl p-4">
                <p class="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-800 mb-2">Ringkasan</p>
                <table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse">
                  <thead><tr class="bg-slate-100"><th class="border border-slate-400 p-1.5 text-left">Mapel</th><th class="border border-slate-400 p-1.5 text-center w-14">Nilai</th><th class="border border-slate-400 p-1.5 text-center w-14">Kelas</th></tr></thead>
                  <tbody>${rows.map(r => `<tr><td class="border border-slate-300 p-1.5">${siEsc(r.code)}</td><td class="border border-slate-300 p-1.5 text-center font-extrabold ${r.mine !== null && r.mine < kkm ? 'text-rose-600' : ''}">${r.mine ?? '-'}</td><td class="border border-slate-300 p-1.5 text-center">${r.avg !== null ? r.avg.toFixed(1).replace('.', ',') : '-'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="border border-slate-300 rounded-xl p-4 text-xs">
              <p><strong>Catatan capaian:</strong> ${siEsc(catatan)}</p>
            </div>

            ${violations.length ? `
            <div class="border border-rose-300 bg-rose-50/50 rounded-xl p-4 text-xs space-y-2">
              <p class="font-extrabold text-rose-800">Catatan Pelanggaran Tata Tertib <span class="font-semibold text-rose-600">(total ${violations.reduce((a, v) => a + (Number(v.points) || 0), 0)} poin)</span></p>
              ${violations.map(v => `<div class="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-rose-200"><div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 font-bold"><span>${siEsc(v.incident_date)} • ${siEsc(v.category)}</span><span class="text-rose-700">+${siEsc(v.points)} poin</span></div><p class="mt-1">${siEsc(v.description)}</p><p class="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Tindak lanjut: ${siEsc(v.sanction || '-')}</p></div>`).join('')}
            </div>` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }

    /** Tampilkan pesan error di layar (menggantikan layar putih) selama belum login. */
    function showFatalError(title, err) {
      try {
        if (STATE.user) return;
        const root = document.getElementById('appRoot');
        if (!root) return;
        const msg = String(err && err.message ? err.message : err);
        root.innerHTML = `
          <div style="max-width:640px;margin:40px auto;padding:20px;border-radius:16px;background:#fff1f2;border:1px solid #fecdd3;color:#9f1239;font-family:sans-serif;font-size:14px;">
            <p style="font-weight:800;font-size:16px;margin:0 0 8px;">${title}</p>
            <pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:12px;margin:0 0 12px;">${msg.replace(/[&<>]/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;' }[c]))}</pre>
            <button onclick="location.reload()" style="padding:8px 16px;border-radius:10px;background:#be123c;color:#fff;font-weight:700;border:0;cursor:pointer;">Muat Ulang</button>
          </div>`;
      } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
    }
    window.addEventListener('error', ev => { showFatalError('Terjadi kesalahan skrip', ev.error || ev.message); });
    window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', ev => { showFatalError('Terjadi kesalahan', ev.reason); });

    // START APP ON LOAD
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      loadAppData(false);
    });
  </script>
