      PembiasaanKarakter: { t: 'Pembiasaan Karakter', cols: ['activity_date', 'activity_name', 'category', 'coordinator_id', 'participation_pct'], f: [['activity_date', 'Tanggal', 'd'], ['activity_name', 'Kegiatan', 't'], ['category', 'Kategori', 't'], ['coordinator_id', 'Koordinator', 'u'], ['participation_pct', 'Partisipasi %', 'n'], ['note', 'Catatan', 'a']] },
      PelanggaranSiswa: { t: 'Pelanggaran', f: [['student_id', 'Siswa', 'st'], ['reporter_id', 'Pelapor', 'U'], ['incident_date', 'Tanggal', 'd'], ['category', 'Kategori', 's', ['Ringan', 'Sedang', 'Berat']], ['description', 'Uraian', 'a'], ['points', 'Poin', 'n'], ['sanction', 'Sanksi / Tindak Lanjut', 'a'], ['status', 'Status', 's', ['Diproses', 'Dibina', 'Selesai']]] },
      KehadiranGTK: { t: 'Kehadiran GTK', cols: ['tanggal', 'nama', 'jabatan', 'jam_masuk', 'jam_pulang', 'status', 'keterangan'], f: [['tanggal', 'Tanggal', 'd'], ['nama', 'Nama', 'g'], ['jabatan', 'Jabatan', 't'], ['jam_masuk', 'Jam Masuk (HH:MM)', 't'], ['jam_pulang', 'Jam Pulang (HH:MM)', 't'], ['status', 'Status', 's', ['Hadir', 'Terlambat', 'Sakit', 'Izin', 'Alpa', 'Dinas Luar', 'Cuti']], ['keterangan', 'Keterangan', 't']] }
    };

    /* ---------- FORM CRUD UNIVERSAL ---------- */
    function nameFor(type, v) {
      if (!v) return '';
      if (type === 'u' || type === 'U') return (users().find(x => x.id === v) || {}).full_name || v;
      if (type === 'st') return (db('Students').find(x => x.id === v) || {}).full_name || v;
      if (type === 'c') return (db('Classes').find(x => x.id === v) || {}).name || v;
      if (type === 'sub') return (db('Subjects').find(x => x.id === v) || {}).name || v;
      if (type === 'r') return (db('InventarisRuang').find(x => x.id === v) || {}).room_name || v;
      return v;
    }
    function optionsFor(type, cur, opts) {
      let list = [];
      if (type === 's') list = (opts || []).map(o => [o, o]);
      if (type === 'u') list = gurus().map(x => [x.id, x.full_name]);
      if (type === 'U') list = users().filter(x => x.role !== 'wali_murid').map(x => [x.id, x.full_name]);
      if (type === 'st') list = db('Students').map(x => [x.id, x.full_name]);
      if (type === 'c') list = db('Classes').map(x => [x.id, x.name]);
      if (type === 'sub') list = db('Subjects').map(x => [x.id, x.name]);
      if (type === 'r') list = db('InventarisRuang').map(x => [x.id, x.room_name]);
      if (type === 'g') list = users().filter(x => ['guru', 'kepala_sekolah'].includes(x.role)).map(x => [x.full_name, x.full_name]);
      const curS = String(cur == null ? '' : cur);
      if (curS && !list.some(o => String(o[0]) === curS)) list.unshift([curS, curS]);
      return '<option value="">— pilih —</option>' + list.map(o => `<option value="${esc(o[0])}" ${String(o[0]) === curS ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(o[1])}</option>`).join('');
    }
    window.openCrudForm = function (sheet, id) {
      const cfg = F[sheet]; if (!cfg) return;
      const rec = id ? (db(sheet).find(r => String(r.id) === String(id)) || {}) : {};
      const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const fields = cfg.f.map(([k, label, type, opts]) => {
        let v = rec[k]; if (v == null) v = (type === 'd' && !id) ? today : '';
        let inp;
        if (type === 'n') inp = `<input id="cf_${k}" type="number" value="${esc(v)}" class="${INP}">`;
        else if (type === 'd') inp = `<input id="cf_${k}" type="date" value="${esc(v)}" class="${INP}">`;
        else if (type === 'a') inp = `<textarea id="cf_${k}" rows="3" class="${INP}">${esc(v)}</textarea>`;
        else if (type === 't') inp = `<input id="cf_${k}" type="${k === 'password' ? 'password' : 'text'}" value="${k === 'password' ? '' : esc(v)}" class="${INP}">`;
        else inp = `<select id="cf_${k}" class="${INP}">${optionsFor(type, v, opts)}</select>`;
        return `<div class="${type === 'a' ? 'sm:col-span-2' : ''}"><label class="block font-bold mb-1">${label}</label>${inp}</div>`;
      }).join('');
      openModal((id ? 'Edit ' : 'Tambah ') + cfg.t,
        `<form onsubmit="saveCrudForm(event,'${sheet}','${id || ''}')" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">${fields}<button type="submit" class="sm:col-span-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold">Simpan</button></form>`);
    };
    window.saveCrudForm = async function (e, sheet, id) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const cfg = F[sheet];
      const old = id ? (db(sheet).find(r => String(r.id) === String(id)) || {}) : {};
      const obj = Object.assign({}, old);
      cfg.f.forEach(([k, , type]) => {
        let v = $q('#cf_' + k).value;
        if (type === 'n' && v !== '') v = Number(v);
        obj[k] = v;
      });
      if (id) obj.id = id; else delete obj.id;
      if (sheet === 'KehadiranGTK' && !obj.sumber) obj.sumber = 'Input manual';
      closeModal();
      await apiSave(sheet, obj, id ? 'Perubahan disimpan!' : 'Data ditambahkan!');
    };

    /* Tabel CRUD siap pakai (cari/filter/urut/Excel ditambahkan otomatis oleh enhance) */
    function crudTable(sheet, opt) {
      opt = opt || {}; const cfg = F[sheet];
      const cols = cfg.cols || cfg.f.map(x => x[0]);
      const types = {}; cfg.f.forEach(x => types[x[0]] = x[2]);
      const rows = db(sheet).slice().sort((a, b) => String(b[cols[0]] || '').localeCompare(String(a[cols[0]] || '')));
      const label = k => (cfg.f.find(x => x[0] === k) || [k, k])[1];
      return `<div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center gap-2"><h3 class="font-extrabold text-sm">${opt.title || cfg.t}</h3>
          <button onclick="openCrudForm('${sheet}','')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah</button></div>
        <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left"><thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr>${cols.map(c => `<th class="p-2.5">${esc(label(c))}</th>`).join('')}<th class="p-2.5 text-right">Aksi</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody class="divide-y">${rows.map(r => `<tr class="hover:bg-slate-50">${cols.map(c => `<td class="p-2.5">${esc(nameFor(types[c], r[c]))}</td>`).join('')}
          <td class="p-2.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap"><button onclick="openCrudForm('${sheet}','${r.id}')" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold mr-2">Edit</button><button onclick="apiDelete('${sheet}','${r.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td></tr>`).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="${cols.length + 1}" class="p-4 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada data</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
    }
    window.renderWakaHumasModule = () => `<div class="space-y-5"><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Panel Wakil Kepala Sekolah Bidang Humas</h2><div class="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${crudTable('KemitraanKomite', { title: 'Kemitraan Komite Sekolah' })}${crudTable('MouKerjasama', { title: 'MoU & Kerjasama Mitra' })}${crudTable('PublikasiSekolah', { title: 'Publikasi Sekolah' })}${crudTable('BukuTamu', { title: 'Buku Tamu Digital' })}</div></div>`;
    window.renderWakaSarprasModule = () => `<div class="space-y-5"><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Panel Wakil Kepala Sekolah Bidang Sarpras</h2><div class="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${crudTable('InventarisRuang', { title: 'Inventaris Ruang' })}${crudTable('BarangSarpras', { title: 'Barang Sarpras (KIB)' })}${crudTable('TiketPerbaikan', { title: 'Tiket Perbaikan' })}${crudTable('PeminjamanSarpras', { title: 'Peminjaman Sarpras' })}</div></div>`;
    window.renderWakaKurikulumModule = () => `<div class="space-y-5"><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Panel Wakil Kepala Sekolah Bidang Kurikulum</h2><div class="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${crudTable('KaldikEvents', { title: 'Kalender Pendidikan (Kaldik)' })}${crudTable('SupervisiGuru', { title: 'Supervisi Akademik Guru' })}</div></div>`;

    /* ---------- PENUGASAN MENGAJAR: semua mapel otomatis + dropdown guru ---------- */
    const _TA = window.renderTeachingAssignmentsModule;
    window.renderTeachingAssignmentsModule = function () {
      const cid = STATE.selectedAssignClassId;
      if (!cid) return _TA();
      const cls = db('Classes').find(c => c.id === cid) || { name: cid };
      const subs = db('Subjects');
      const tcs = db('TeacherClasses').filter(t => t.class_id === cid);
      const others = db('Classes').filter(c => c.id !== cid);
      const opt = sel => '<option value="">— Belum ditugaskan —</option>' + gurus().map(g => `<option value="${g.id}" ${g.id === sel ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(g.full_name)}</option>`).join('');
      return `<div class="space-y-5">
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3"><button onclick="STATE.selectedAssignClassId=null; renderApp();" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">← Kembali ke Daftar Kelas</button>
            <div><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Penugasan Mengajar — Kelas ${esc(cls.name)}</h2><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Mapel mengikuti urutan menu Kelas &amp; Mapel. Pilih guru di sebelah kanan setiap mapel, lalu klik Simpan.</p></div></div>
          <span id="taCount" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-800 text-xs font-bold"></span></div>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center text-xs"><select id="taCopy" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"><option value="">Salin penugasan dari kelas...</option>${others.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">Kelas ${esc(c.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select>
          <button onclick="taCopyFrom()" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 font-bold">Terapkan Salinan</button></div>
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden"><div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left" data-noenh="1">
          <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3 w-12">No</th><th class="p-3">Kode</th><th class="p-3">Mata Pelajaran</th><th class="p-3 text-center">JP/Mgg</th><th class="p-3 min-w-[240px]">Guru Pengampu</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody class="divide-y">${subs.map((s, i) => { const tc = tcs.find(t => t.subject_id === s.id); return `<tr class="hover:bg-slate-50"><td class="p-3 font-bold">${i + 1}</td><td class="p-3 font-mono">${esc(s.code)}</td><td class="p-3 font-extrabold text-slate-800">${esc(s.name)}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${esc(s.jp_per_week)}</td><td class="p-3"><select class="ta-sel w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border font-semibold text-indigo-700" data-sub="${s.id}" onchange="taCount()">${opt(tc ? tc.teacher_id : '')}</select></td></tr>`; }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="5" class="p-4 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada mata pelajaran. Tambahkan di menu Kelas &amp; Mapel.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div></div>
        <div class="flex justify-end"><button onclick="taSaveAll('${cid}')" class="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-sm font-bold">Simpan Semua Penugasan</button></div></div>`;
    };
    window.taCount = function () { const s = $$('.ta-sel'); const n = s.filter(x => x.value).length; const el = $q('#taCount'); if (el) el.textContent = n + ' dari ' + s.length + ' mapel sudah ada gurunya'; };
    window.taCopyFrom = function () {
      const src = $q('#taCopy').value; if (!src) { showToast('Pilih kelas sumber terlebih dahulu.', 'warning'); return; }
      const t = db('TeacherClasses').filter(x => x.class_id === src);
      $$('.ta-sel').forEach(sel => { const m = t.find(x => x.subject_id === sel.dataset.sub); sel.value = m ? m.teacher_id : ''; });
      taCount(); showToast('Disalin. Klik "Simpan Semua Penugasan" untuk menyimpan.', 'success');
    };
    window.taSaveAll = async function (cid) {
      const pairs = $$('.ta-sel').map(s => ({ subject_id: s.dataset.sub, teacher_id: s.value }));
      showLoader('Menyimpan penugasan mengajar...');
      try { await callServer('saveClassAssignments', cid, pairs, actorObj()); await loadAppData(true); showToast('Penugasan mengajar tersimpan!', 'success'); }
      catch (err) { showToast('Gagal menyimpan: ' + err.message, 'error'); } finally { hideLoader(); }
    };

    /* ---------- EKSTRAKURIKULER: kartu klik -> anggota ---------- */
    window.renderEkskulModule = function () {
      const eks = db('Ekstrakurikuler'), ass = db('EkskulAssessments'), id = STATE.selectedEkskulId;
      const coachOf = e => (gurus().find(g => g.id === e.coach_id) || { full_name: '-' }).full_name;
      const wajib = e => String(e.is_mandatory) === 'true';
      if (id) {
        const e = eks.find(x => x.id === id);
        if (!e) { STATE.selectedEkskulId = null; return window.renderEkskulModule(); }
        const mem = ass.filter(a => a.ekskul_id === id).map(a => ({ a, s: db('Students').find(s => s.id === a.student_id) })).filter(x => x.s);
        const cn = cid => (db('Classes').find(c => c.id === cid) || { name: '-' }).name;
        mem.sort((x, y) => cn(x.s.class_id).localeCompare(cn(y.s.class_id)) || x.s.full_name.localeCompare(y.s.full_name));
        return `<div class="space-y-5">
          <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3"><div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onclick="STATE.selectedEkskulId=null; renderApp();" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-xs font-bold">← Kembali ke Daftar Ekskul</button>
            <div><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">${esc(e.name)} — Anggota</h2><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Pembina: <strong>${esc(coachOf(e))}</strong> • ${esc(e.schedule)} • ${mem.length} anggota${wajib(e) ? ' • diikuti seluruh siswa' : ''}</p></div></div>
            <div class="flex gap-2"><button onclick="openCrudForm('Ekstrakurikuler','${e.id}')" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-xs font-bold">Edit Ekskul</button>
            <button onclick="openEkskulAddMembers('${e.id}')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Anggota</button></div></div>
          <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden"><div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left">
            <thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">No</th><th class="p-3">NIS</th><th class="p-3">Nama Siswa</th><th class="p-3">Kelas</th><th class="p-3">Predikat</th><th class="p-3">Catatan</th><th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody class="divide-y">${mem.map((m, i) => `<tr class="hover:bg-slate-50"><td class="p-3">${i + 1}</td><td class="p-3 font-mono">${esc(m.s.nis)}</td><td class="p-3 font-bold">${esc(m.s.full_name)}</td><td class="p-3">${esc(cn(m.s.class_id))}</td><td class="p-3 font-bold">${esc(m.a.predicate)}</td><td class="p-3 text-slate-500">${esc(m.a.note)}</td>
              <td class="p-3 text-right"><button onclick="apiDelete('EkskulAssessments','${m.a.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Keluarkan</button></td></tr>`).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="7" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada anggota. Klik "+ Tambah Anggota".</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div></div></div>`;
      }
      return `<div class="space-y-5"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><div><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Manajemen Ekstrakurikuler</h2><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Klik kartu untuk melihat dan mengelola anggota ekskul</p></div>
        <button onclick="openAddEkskulModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">+ Tambah Ekstrakurikuler</button></div>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">${eks.map(e => `<div onclick="STATE.selectedEkskulId='${e.id}'; renderApp();" class="cursor-pointer bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border-2 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-600 hover:shadow-md transition flex flex-col justify-between gap-3">
          <div><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-bold ${wajib(e) ? 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800' : 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800'}">${wajib(e) ? 'Wajib' : 'Pilihan'}</span><span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">${ass.filter(a => a.ekskul_id === e.id).length} Anggota</span></div>
          <h3 class="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-base mt-2">${esc(e.name)}</h3><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Pembina: <strong>${esc(coachOf(e))}</strong></p><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Jadwal: ${esc(e.schedule)}</p></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center pt-2 border-t"><span class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600">Lihat anggota →</span><span class="flex gap-2">
            <button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); openCrudForm('Ekstrakurikuler','${e.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-xs font-bold">Edit</button>
            <button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); apiDelete('Ekstrakurikuler','${e.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 text-xs font-bold">Hapus</button></span></div></div>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
    };
    window.openEkskulAddMembers = function (eid) {
      const have = new Set(db('EkskulAssessments').filter(a => a.ekskul_id === eid).map(a => a.student_id));
      const sts = db('Students');
      const body = `<div class="space-y-3 text-xs"><input id="emSearch" oninput="emFilter()" placeholder="Cari nama / NIS siswa..." class="${INP}">
        <p class="text-slate-500">Siswa yang sudah menjadi anggota ditandai abu-abu. Centang siswa yang akan ditambahkan.</p>
        ${db('Classes').map(c => { const ls = sts.filter(s => s.class_id === c.id); if (!ls.length) return '';
          return `<details open class="em-cls border rounded-xl p-2"><summary class="font-bold cursor-pointer py-1">Kelas ${esc(c.name)} (${ls.length}) <label class="ml-3 font-normal" onclick="event.stopPropagation()"><input type="checkbox" onchange="emAll(this,'${c.id}')"> Pilih semua kelas ini</label></summary>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-1 mt-1">${ls.map(s => `<label class="em-row flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg border ${have.has(s.id) ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400' : 'hover:bg-indigo-50 cursor-pointer'}" data-t="${esc((s.full_name + ' ' + s.nis).toLowerCase())}"><input type="checkbox" class="em-cb" data-c="${c.id}" value="${s.id}" ${have.has(s.id) ? 'checked disabled' : ''} onchange="emCount()"><span class="font-semibold">${esc(s.full_name)}</span><span class="text-[10px] ml-auto">${esc(s.nis)}</span></label>`).join('')}</div></details>`; }).join('')}</div>`;
      openModal('Tambah Anggota Ekstrakurikuler', body, `<span id="emCnt" class="mr-auto text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700">0 siswa dipilih</span><button onclick="closeModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold">Batal</button><button onclick="emSave('${eid}')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-sm font-bold">Simpan Anggota</button>`);
    };
    window.emCount = () => { const n = $$('.em-cb:checked:not(:disabled)').length; const el = $q('#emCnt'); if (el) el.textContent = n + ' siswa dipilih'; };
    window.emAll = (cb, cid) => { $$('.em-cb[data-c="' + cid + '"]:not(:disabled)').forEach(x => { if (x.closest('.em-row').style.display !== 'none') x.checked = cb.checked; }); emCount(); };
    window.emFilter = () => { const q = $q('#emSearch').value.toLowerCase(); $$('.em-row').forEach(r => r.style.display = r.dataset.t.includes(q) ? '' : 'none'); };
    window.emSave = async function (eid) {
      const ids = $$('.em-cb:checked:not(:disabled)').map(x => x.value);
      if (!ids.length) { showToast('Belum ada siswa yang dicentang.', 'warning'); return; }
      showLoader('Menambahkan anggota...');
      try { const r = await callServer('addEkskulMembers', eid, ids, actorObj()); closeModal(); await loadAppData(true); showToast((r && r.added != null ? r.added : ids.length) + ' anggota ditambahkan!', 'success'); }
      catch (err) { showToast('Gagal: ' + err.message, 'error'); } finally { hideLoader(); }
    };

    /* ---------- REKAP KEHADIRAN KEPALA SEKOLAH & GURU ---------- */
    const KG = { month: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7), who: 'ALL', tab: 'rekap' };
    const MON = { jan: 1, januari: 1, feb: 2, februari: 2, mar: 3, maret: 3, apr: 4, april: 4, mei: 5, may: 5, jun: 6, juni: 6, jul: 7, juli: 7, agu: 8, agt: 8, agustus: 8, aug: 8, sep: 9, sept: 9, september: 9, okt: 10, oktober: 10, oct: 10, nov: 11, november: 11, des: 12, desember: 12, dec: 12 };
    const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    function kgDate(s) {
      s = String(s || '').trim(); let m;
      if ((m = s.match(/^(\d{4})[-\/](\d{1,2})[-\/](\d{1,2})/))) return `${m[1]}-${p2(m[2])}-${p2(m[3])}`;
      if ((m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{4})/))) return `${m[3]}-${p2(m[2])}-${p2(m[1])}`;
      if ((m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z]+)\.?,?\s+(\d{4})/)) && MON[m[2].toLowerCase()]) return `${m[3]}-${p2(MON[m[2].toLowerCase()])}-${p2(m[1])}`;
      return '';
    }
    const kgTime = s => { const m = String(s || '').match(/(\d{1,2})[:.](\d{2})/); return m ? p2(m[1]) + ':' + m[2] : ''; };
    function kgStatus(s) {
      const t = String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!t) return 'Hadir';
      if (/^(h|hadir|present|masuk)/.test(t)) return /terlambat|late/.test(t) ? 'Terlambat' : 'Hadir';
      if (/terlambat|telat|late/.test(t)) return 'Terlambat';
      if (/^(s|sakit|sick)/.test(t)) return 'Sakit';
      if (/^(i|izin|ijin|permit)/.test(t)) return 'Izin';
      if (/^(a|alpa|alpha|absen|tanpa|mangkir)/.test(t)) return 'Alpa';
      if (/dinas|^dl|tugas/.test(t)) return 'Dinas Luar';
      if (/cuti|leave/.test(t)) return 'Cuti';
      return s.trim();
    }
    const canKgEdit = () => ['admin', 'kepala_sekolah'].includes(STATE.activeRole);
    function kgPeople() {
      const names = new Map();
      users().filter(u => ['guru', 'kepala_sekolah'].includes(u.role)).forEach(u => names.set(u.full_name.toLowerCase(), { nama: u.full_name, jabatan: u.role === 'kepala_sekolah' ? 'Kepala Sekolah' : 'Guru' }));
      db('KehadiranGTK').forEach(r => { const k = String(r.nama || '').toLowerCase(); if (k && !names.has(k)) names.set(k, { nama: r.nama, jabatan: r.jabatan || '-' }); });
      return Array.from(names.values()).sort((a, b) => (a.jabatan === 'Kepala Sekolah' ? -1 : 0) - (b.jabatan === 'Kepala Sekolah' ? -1 : 0) || a.nama.localeCompare(b.nama));
    }
    function kgRecap() {
      const recs = db('KehadiranGTK').filter(r => String(r.tanggal).startsWith(KG.month));
      return kgPeople().map(p => {
        const mine = recs.filter(r => String(r.nama || '').toLowerCase() === p.nama.toLowerCase());
        const c = k => mine.filter(r => kgStatus(r.status) === k).length;
        const hadir = c('Hadir'), tl = c('Terlambat'), dl = c('Dinas Luar'), tot = mine.length;
        return { p, hadir, tl, sakit: c('Sakit'), izin: c('Izin'), alpa: c('Alpa'), dl, cuti: c('Cuti'), tot, pct: tot ? Math.round((hadir + tl + dl) * 100 / tot) : null };
      });
    }
    window.renderKehadiranGtk = function () {
      const ed = canKgEdit(), me = STATE.user ? STATE.user.full_name.toLowerCase() : '';
      const recap = kgRecap();
      const person = kgPeople();
      let body;
      if (KG.tab === 'rekap') {
        body = `<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left" id="kgTable"><thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">No</th><th class="p-3">Nama</th><th class="p-3">Jabatan</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Hadir</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Terlambat</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Sakit</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Izin</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Alpa</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Dinas Luar</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Cuti</th><th class="p-3 text-center">Total Hari</th><th class="p-3 text-center">% Hadir</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody class="divide-y">${recap.map((r, i) => `<tr class="${r.p.nama.toLowerCase() === me ? 'bg-indigo-50 font-bold' : 'hover:bg-slate-50'}"><td class="p-3">${i + 1}</td><td class="p-3 font-bold">${esc(r.p.nama)}</td><td class="p-3">${esc(r.p.jabatan)}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${r.hadir}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${r.tl}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${r.sakit}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${r.izin}</td><td class="p-3 text-center ${r.alpa ? 'text-rose-600 font-bold' : ''}">${r.alpa}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${r.dl}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${r.cuti}</td><td class="p-3 text-center">${r.tot}</td><td class="p-3 text-center font-extrabold ${r.pct == null ? 'text-slate-400' : r.pct >= 90 ? 'text-emerald-700' : r.pct >= 75 ? 'text-amber-600' : 'text-rose-600'}">${r.pct == null ? '-' : r.pct + '%'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
      } else {
        const rows = db('KehadiranGTK').filter(r => String(r.tanggal).startsWith(KG.month) && (KG.who === 'ALL' || String(r.nama).toLowerCase() === KG.who.toLowerCase())).sort((a, b) => String(b.tanggal).localeCompare(String(a.tanggal)) || String(a.nama).localeCompare(String(b.nama)));
        body = `<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs text-left" id="kgTable"><thead class="bg-slate-800 text-white"><tr><th class="p-3">Tanggal</th><th class="p-3">Nama</th><th class="p-3">Jabatan</th><th class="p-3">Masuk</th><th class="p-3">Pulang</th><th class="p-3">Status</th><th class="p-3">Keterangan</th>${ed ? '<th class="p-3 text-right">Aksi</th>' : ''}</tr></thead>
          <tbody class="divide-y">${rows.map(r => `<tr class="hover:bg-slate-50"><td class="p-3 whitespace-nowrap">${esc(r.tanggal)}</td><td class="p-3 font-bold">${esc(r.nama)}</td><td class="p-3">${esc(r.jabatan)}</td><td class="p-3">${esc(r.jam_masuk)}</td><td class="p-3">${esc(r.jam_pulang)}</td><td class="p-3 font-bold">${esc(r.status)}</td><td class="p-3 text-slate-500">${esc(r.keterangan)}</td>${ed ? `<td class="p-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap"><button onclick="openCrudForm('KehadiranGTK','${r.id}')" class="text-indigo-600 font-bold mr-2">Edit</button><button onclick="apiDelete('KehadiranGTK','${r.id}')" class="text-rose-600 font-bold">Hapus</button></td>` : ''}</tr>`).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="8" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada data kehadiran pada bulan ini.</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div>`;
      }
      return `<div class="space-y-4"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Rekapan Kehadiran Kepala Sekolah & Guru</h2><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Sumber: sheet <strong>KehadiranGTK</strong> (hasil salin dari aplikasi Attendance Management)${ed ? '' : ' • tampilan hanya-baca'}</p></div>
        ${ed ? `<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><button onclick="openCrudForm('KehadiranGTK','')" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-xs font-bold">+ Input Manual</button><button onclick="openKgImport()" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold">Impor / Tempel dari Attendance Management</button></div>` : ''}</div>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs"><div class="flex rounded-xl overflow-hidden border"><button onclick="KGset('tab','rekap')" class="px-3 py-2 font-bold ${KG.tab === 'rekap' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-white'}">Rekap per Orang</button><button onclick="KGset('tab','harian')" class="px-3 py-2 font-bold ${KG.tab === 'harian' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-white'}">Data Harian</button></div>
          <input type="month" value="${KG.month}" onchange="KGset('month',this.value)" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border">
          ${KG.tab === 'harian' ? `<select onchange="KGset('who',this.value)" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border"><option value="ALL">Semua orang</option>${person.map(p => `<option ${p.nama === KG.who ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(p.nama)}</option>`).join('')}</select>` : ''}
          <button onclick="kgExport()" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold">⬇ Excel</button><button onclick="kgPrint()" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 font-bold">Cetak</button></div>
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-3">${body}</div></div>`;
    };
    window.KGset = (k, v) => { KG[k] = v; renderApp(); };
    function kgAoa() {
      const t = $q('#kgTable'); if (!t) return [];
      const last = /aksi/i.test((t.tHead.rows[0].lastElementChild || {}).textContent || '') ? 1 : 0;
      return Array.from(t.rows).filter(r => r.style.display !== 'none').map(r => Array.from(r.cells).slice(0, r.cells.length - last).map(c => c.textContent.trim()));
    }
    window.kgExport = () => exportTableToExcel(kgAoa(), 'Kehadiran GTK', 'Rekap-Kehadiran-GTK-' + KG.month + '.xlsx');
    window.kgPrint = function () {
      const ks = users().find(u => u.role === 'kepala_sekolah') || { full_name: '-', nip: '-' };
      const t = $q('#kgTable').cloneNode(true); $$('input,button', t).forEach(x => x.remove());
      triggerPrintHtml(renderOfficialLetterhead() + `<h3 style="text-align:center;font-weight:bold;margin:6px 0">REKAPAN KEHADIRAN KEPALA SEKOLAH DAN GURU<br>Bulan ${KG.month}</h3><div style="font-size:9pt">${t.outerHTML.replace(/<table/, '<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse" border="1" cellpadding="4"')}</div>` + renderSignatureBlock('Kepala Sekolah', ks.full_name, ks.nip));
    };
    window.openKgImport = function () {
      openModal('Impor Kehadiran dari Attendance Management v4.8.9', `<div class="space-y-3 text-xs">
        <p>Salin (Ctrl+C) tabel kehadiran dari aplikasi Attendance Management, lalu tempel di bawah. Jika baris pertama berisi judul kolom (Nama, Tanggal, Masuk, Pulang, Status, Keterangan), urutan kolom dikenali otomatis. Tanpa judul, urutan dianggap: <strong>Tanggal, Nama, Jam Masuk, Jam Pulang, Status, Keterangan</strong>. Data dengan tanggal + nama yang sama akan diperbarui, bukan digandakan.</p>
        <textarea id="kgPaste" rows="8" oninput="kgPreview()" class="${INP} font-mono" placeholder="Tempel data di sini..."></textarea><div id="kgPrev" class="text-slate-500">Belum ada data.</div></div>`,
        `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold">Batal</button><button onclick="kgSaveImport()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white text-sm font-bold">Simpan ke Database</button>`, 'max-w-4xl');
    };
    function kgParse(text) {
      const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(l => l.trim()); if (!lines.length) return [];
      const sp = l => l.includes('\t') ? l.split('\t') : l.includes(';') ? l.split(';') : l.split(',');
      let map = { tanggal: 0, nama: 1, jam_masuk: 2, jam_pulang: 3, status: 4, keterangan: 5, jabatan: -1 };
      const h = sp(lines[0]).map(x => x.trim().toLowerCase());
      if (h.some(x => /^(nama|name|tanggal|date)/.test(x))) {
        map = { tanggal: -1, nama: -1, jam_masuk: -1, jam_pulang: -1, status: -1, keterangan: -1, jabatan: -1 };
        h.forEach((x, i) => { if (/tanggal|date/.test(x)) map.tanggal = i; else if (/nama|name/.test(x)) map.nama = i; else if (/masuk|check.?in|datang/.test(x)) map.jam_masuk = i; else if (/pulang|keluar|check.?out/.test(x)) map.jam_pulang = i; else if (/status|kehadiran/.test(x)) map.status = i; else if (/ket|catatan|note/.test(x)) map.keterangan = i; else if (/jabatan|role|posisi/.test(x)) map.jabatan = i; });
        lines.shift();
      }
      const g = (c, k) => map[k] >= 0 ? (c[map[k]] || '').trim() : '';
      return lines.map(l => { const c = sp(l); return { tanggal: kgDate(g(c, 'tanggal')), nama: g(c, 'nama'), jabatan: g(c, 'jabatan'), jam_masuk: kgTime(g(c, 'jam_masuk')), jam_pulang: kgTime(g(c, 'jam_pulang')), status: kgStatus(g(c, 'status')), keterangan: g(c, 'keterangan') }; });
    }
    window.kgPreview = function () {
      const rows = kgParse($q('#kgPaste').value), ok = rows.filter(r => r.tanggal && r.nama), known = new Set(kgPeople().map(p => p.nama.toLowerCase()));
      const unk = [...new Set(ok.filter(r => !known.has(r.nama.toLowerCase())).map(r => r.nama))];
      $q('#kgPrev').innerHTML = `<p><strong>${ok.length}</strong> baris valid dari ${rows.length} baris${rows.length - ok.length ? ` <span class="text-rose-600">(${rows.length - ok.length} dilewati: tanggal/nama tidak terbaca)</span>` : ''}.${unk.length ? ` <span class="text-amber-600">Nama belum ada di data pengguna: ${esc(unk.slice(0, 5).join(', '))}${unk.length > 5 ? '...' : ''}</span>` : ''}</p>
        <div class="overflow-x-auto max-h-48 mt-2"><table class="w-full"><tbody>${ok.slice(0, 8).map(r => `<tr><td class="p-1">${r.tanggal}</td><td class="p-1">${esc(r.nama)}</td><td class="p-1">${r.jam_masuk}</td><td class="p-1">${r.jam_pulang}</td><td class="p-1 font-bold">${esc(r.status)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
    };
    window.kgSaveImport = async function () {
      const rows = kgParse($q('#kgPaste').value).filter(r => r.tanggal && r.nama);
      if (!rows.length) { showToast('Tidak ada baris valid untuk disimpan.', 'warning'); return; }
      showLoader('Mengimpor ' + rows.length + ' baris kehadiran...');
      try { const r = await callServer('importKehadiranGtk', rows, actorObj()); closeModal(); await loadAppData(true); showToast(`Impor selesai: ${r.added} baru, ${r.updated} diperbarui.`, 'success'); }
      catch (err) { showToast('Gagal impor: ' + err.message, 'error'); } finally { hideLoader(); }
    };

    /* ---------- HOOK: menu, router, navigasi, konfirmasi hapus ---------- */
    const _menus = window.getMenusForActiveRole;
    window.getMenusForActiveRole = function () {
      const m = _menus();
      if (['admin', 'kepala_sekolah'].includes(STATE.activeRole)) {
        const i = m.findIndex(x => x.id === 'additional_assignments');
        m.splice(i >= 0 ? i + 1 : m.length, 0, { id: 'kehadiran_gtk', label: 'Kehadiran Kepsek & Guru', icon: 'user-check' });
      }
      return m;
    };
    const _page = window.renderActivePage;
    window.renderActivePage = function () {
      if (STATE.activeMenu === 'kehadiran_gtk' && STATE.activeRole !== 'wali_murid') return renderKehadiranGtk();
      return _page();
    };
    const _nav = window.navigateMenu;
    window.navigateMenu = function (id) { STATE.selectedEkskulId = null; return _nav(id); };
    const _del = window.apiDelete;
    window.apiDelete = function (sheet, id, msg) {
      if (sheet === 'AttendanceSessions') return _del(sheet, id, msg);
      window.__pendDel = [sheet, id, msg];
      openModal('Konfirmasi Hapus', '<p class="text-sm">Data yang dihapus tidak dapat dikembalikan. Yakin ingin menghapus?</p>',
        '<button onclick="closeModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold">Batal</button><button onclick="closeModal(); window.__doDel()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 text-white text-sm font-bold">Ya, Hapus</button>', 'max-w-md');
    };
    window.__doDel = () => _del.apply(null, window.__pendDel);

    /* ---------- ENHANCER: Edit otomatis, cari, filter kolom, urut, Excel ---------- */
    function enhance() {
      const main = $q('main'); if (!main) return;
      if (STATE.activeMenu === 'waka_kesiswaan' && !$q('[data-ks]', main)) {
        main.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<div data-ks class="mt-6 space-y-4"><h3 class="text-lg font-extrabold">Kelola Data Kesiswaan (Tambah / Edit / Hapus)</h3><div class="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${crudTable('PrestasiSiswa', { title: 'Prestasi Siswa' })}${crudTable('OsisMembers', { title: 'Pengurus OSIS & MPK' })}${crudTable('PembiasaanKarakter', { title: 'Pembiasaan Karakter & Ibadah' })}</div></div>`);
      }
      $$('button[onclick^="apiDelete("]', main).forEach(b => {
        const m = b.getAttribute('onclick').match(/apiDelete\('(\w+)',\s*'([^']+)'/);
        if (!m || !F[m[1]] || (STATE.activeMenu === 'kehadiran_gtk')) return;
        const host = b.parentElement;
        if (host.dataset.ed || /\bEdit\b/.test(host.textContent)) return;
        host.dataset.ed = '1';
        const e = document.createElement('button'); e.className = 'text-indigo-600 font-bold mr-2'; e.textContent = 'Edit';
        e.setAttribute('onclick', `openCrudForm('${m[1]}','${m[2]}')`); host.insertBefore(e, b);
      });
      $$('table', main).forEach(t => {
        const body = t.tBodies[0];
        if (t.dataset.enh || t.dataset.noenh || t.closest('[data-noenh]') || !body || body.rows.length < 5 || body.querySelector('input,select,textarea') || t.id === 'kgTable') return;
        t.dataset.enh = '1';
        const heads = Array.from(t.tHead ? t.tHead.rows[0].cells : []).map(c => c.textContent.trim());
        const bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center my-2 text-xs no-print';
        bar.innerHTML = '<input type="search" placeholder="🔍 Cari..." class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border w-48"><span class="flt-n text-slate-500"></span>';
        const sels = [];
        heads.forEach((h, i) => {
          if (!/^(kelas|status|kategori|jabatan|peran|role|mapel|mata pelajaran|hari|tingkat|predikat)/i.test(h)) return;
          const vals = [...new Set(Array.from(body.rows).map(r => (r.cells[i] ? r.cells[i].textContent.trim() : '')).filter(Boolean))];
          if (vals.length < 2 || vals.length > 30) return;
          const s = document.createElement('select'); s.className = 'px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border'; s.dataset.col = i;
          s.innerHTML = `<option value="">Semua ${esc(h)}</option>` + vals.sort().map(v => `<option>${esc(v)}</option>`).join(''); bar.appendChild(s); sels.push(s);
        });
        const xb = document.createElement('button'); xb.className = 'px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold'; xb.textContent = '⬇ Excel'; bar.appendChild(xb);
        const wrap = t.closest('.overflow-x-auto') || t; wrap.parentNode.insertBefore(bar, wrap);
        const inp = bar.querySelector('input'), cnt = bar.querySelector('.flt-n');
        const apply = () => { const q = inp.value.toLowerCase(); let n = 0; Array.from(body.rows).forEach(r => { const ok = r.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q) && sels.every(s => !s.value || (r.cells[s.dataset.col] && r.cells[s.dataset.col].textContent.trim() === s.value)); r.style.display = ok ? '' : 'none'; if (ok) n++; }); cnt.textContent = n + ' / ' + body.rows.length + ' data'; };
        inp.oninput = apply; sels.forEach(s => s.onchange = apply); apply();
        xb.onclick = () => { const hasAksi = /aksi/i.test(heads[heads.length - 1] || ''); const rows = [heads.slice(0, heads.length - (hasAksi ? 1 : 0))].concat(Array.from(body.rows).filter(r => r.style.display !== 'none').map(r => Array.from(r.cells).slice(0, r.cells.length - (hasAksi ? 1 : 0)).map(c => c.textContent.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')))); exportTableToExcel(rows, 'Data', 'Data-' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) + '.xlsx'); };
        Array.from(t.tHead.rows[0].cells).forEach((th, i) => {
          if (/^(no|aksi)$/i.test(th.textContent.trim())) return;
          th.style.cursor = 'pointer'; th.title = 'Klik untuk mengurutkan'; let asc = true;
          th.onclick = () => { const rs = Array.from(body.rows); const val = r => (r.cells[i] ? r.cells[i].textContent.trim() : ''); rs.sort((a, b) => { const x = val(a), y = val(b), nx = parseFloat(x), ny = parseFloat(y); return (!isNaN(nx) && !isNaN(ny) ? nx - ny : x.localeCompare(y, 'id')) * (asc ? 1 : -1); }); asc = !asc; rs.forEach(r => body.appendChild(r)); };
        });
      });
      if (STATE.activeRole === 'guru' && STATE.activeMenu === 'dashboard' && !$q('[data-kg]', main)) {
        const b = $q('button[onclick="navigateMenu(\'cp_atp\')"]', main);
        if (b) { const g = b.parentElement; g.className = g.className.replace('sm:grid-cols-3', 'sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4');
          g.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<button data-kg onclick="navigateMenu('kehadiran_gtk')" class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border-2 border-emerald-100 hover:border-emerald-500 shadow-2xs flex items-center gap-4 text-left transition"><span class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 flex items-center justify-center"><i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-5 h-5"></i></span><div><p class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 text-sm">Rekap Kehadiran</p><p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Kepala Sekolah &amp; Guru</p></div></button>`); }
      }
      if (STATE.activeMenu === 'teaching_assignments' && STATE.selectedAssignClassId) taCount();
      lucide.createIcons();
    }
    const _render = window.renderApp;
    window.renderApp = function () { _render.apply(this, arguments); try { enhance(); } catch (e) { console.error('enhance', e); } };
  })();
  </script>

</body>
</html>
